# 专属美式英语口语教练 - 存档

> 任务：可直接说出口的英语 chunks / collocations / sentence frames，美式拼写/用词/发音/语气
> 默认设置：水平 B2-C1；正式度中性偏商务；中英双语；每轮5个主题 x 8个表达；覆盖至少3个场景
> 文件用途：保存之前和以后所有轮次，不重复。说“更多”时在此文件追加新轮次。

---

## 第一轮 (2026-10-13) - 默认设置

主题：商务职场 / AI科技 / 情感表达 / 播客主持 / 公开演讲
场景覆盖：会议 / 播客 / 演讲 / 日常深度交流

### 主题1：商务职场 Business at Work【会议/远程协作】

**1. to loop someone in**
- 中文：把某人拉进来同步信息
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'll loop you in on the email thread so you're up to date.
- 例句2 [会议]：Let me loop in Sarah real quick — she has the latest numbers.
- 替换：随意 loop in Sarah / 正式 include Sarah in the communication / 更强 bring Sarah into the loop ASAP
- 发音：LOOP someone IN，重音LOOP，连读 loop‿in 【IPA head: /tə luːp ˈsʌmwʌn ɪn/】
- 提醒：跨部门协作，不要对老板说 loop me in? 说 Please keep me in the loop.
- 练习：I'll ___ you ___ once we have an update.

**2. to move the needle**
- 中文：产生实质性影响
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：This new campaign really moved the needle on sales.
- 例句2 [会议]：I like the idea, but will it actually move the needle?
- 替换：随意 make a difference / 正式 drive measurable impact / 更强 really shift the needle
- 发音：MOVE the NEE-dle，重读move+needle，the弱读 【IPA head: /tə muːv ðə ˈniːdəl/】
- 提醒：商务高频，不用于个人小事
- 练习：Did it move the needle? 跟读3遍

**3. Let's double-click on that.**
- 中文：深入聊一下那点
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：That's interesting — let's double-click on that pricing point.
- 例句2 [采访]：You mentioned burnout. Can we double-click on that?
- 替换：随意 dig a little deeper / 正式 explore that in more detail / 更强 let's unpack that fully
- 发音：/ˈdʌbəl klɪk/，Let's弱读 /lɛts/ 【IPA head: /lɛts ˈdʌbəl ˈklɪk ɑn ðæt/】
- 提醒：美国科技/咨询圈常用，书面正式不用
- 练习：A: We lost two clients. B: Let's double-click on ___.

**4. to be on the same page**
- 中文：在同一频道，达成共识
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Just to make sure we're on the same page, the deadline is Friday, right?
- 例句2 [会议]：Before we move on, are we all on the same page here?
- 替换：随意 we're good? / 正式 ensure alignment / 更强 100% aligned
- 发音：same PAGE重读，连读 on‿the‿same 【IPA head: /tə bi ɑn ðə seɪm peɪdʒ/】
- 提醒：万能确认句，不要说 in the same page
- 练习：Are we ___ the ___ page on this?

**5. I don't have the bandwidth.**
- 中文：我目前没精力/没时间接
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'd love to help, but I just don't have the bandwidth this week.
- 例句2 [日常]：Honestly, I have zero bandwidth for small talk today.
- 替换：随意 I'm swamped / 正式 My capacity is limited / 更强 I'm completely maxed out
- 发音：BAND-width重音首音节，don't have连读 【IPA head: /aɪ doʊnt hæv ðə ˈbændwɪdθ/】
- 提醒：拒绝神器，比 I'm busy 更商务委婉
- 练习：I don't have the bandwidth for that right now. 跟读

**6. to circle back**
- 中文：稍后再回来讨论/回复
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let me circle back to you by tomorrow with an answer.
- 例句2 [播客]：We're out of time, but we'll circle back to AI safety in part two.
- 替换：随意 get back to you / 正式 revisit this later / 更强 I promise I'll circle back
- 发音：CIR-cle BACK，circle美式 /ˈsɜrkəl/ 【IPA head: /tə ˈsɜrkəl bæk/】
- 提醒：会议结束必备
- 练习：Let's ___ ___ to this after lunch.

**7. At a high level...**
- 中文：宏观来说，大体上
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：At a high level, our program is focused on customer retention.
- 例句2 [演讲]：At a high level, there are three shifts happening in AI.
- 替换：随意 big picture / 正式 from a strategic perspective
- 发音：HIGH LE-vel重音high，at弱读，后停顿 【IPA head: /æt ə haɪ ˈlɛvəl/】
- 提醒：开场总览好用，避免滥用
- 练习：用它开头讲你的工作30秒

**8. I'll take the lead on this.**
- 中文：这事我来牵头
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Don't worry, I'll take the lead on organizing the client call.
- 例句2 [会议]：Who wants to take the lead? — I'll take it.
- 替换：随意 I'll handle it / 正式 I'll take ownership / 更强 I'll champion this
- 发音：take the LEAD重读lead 【IPA head: /aɪl teɪk ðə liːd ɑn ðɪs/】
- 提醒：升职加薪口头禅
- 练习：角色扮演主动接任务说2遍

### 主题2：AI/科技 AI & Tech【播客/深度交流】

**1. to ship a feature**
- 中文：上线/发布功能
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：We just shipped a new voice search feature last week.
- 例句2 [播客]：They ship so fast, they center the whole program around user feedback.
- 替换：随意 roll out / 正式 release
- 发音：SHIP a FEA-ture，连读 ship‿a 【IPA head: /tə ʃɪp ə ˈfiːtʃər/】
- 提醒：科技圈说ship，program不是programme
- 练习：We ___ a new update yesterday.

**2. It's a total game-changer.**
- 中文：彻底改变规则的东西
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Real-time translation? That's a total game-changer for travelers.
- 例句2 [采访]：Do you think AI agents will be a game-changer for small businesses?
- 替换：随意 mind-blowing / 正式 transformative
- 发音：GAME-CHAN-ger重读game 【IPA head: /ɪts ə ˈtoʊtəl ˈɡeɪm ˌtʃeɪndʒər/】
- 提醒：演讲/播客好用，论文别用
- 练习：带兴奋语调跟读3遍

**3. to scale up**
- 中文：扩大规模
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Once we find product-market fit, we can scale up fast.
- 例句2 [演讲]：The question isn't if AI will scale up, it's how we organize it.
- 替换：随意 grow big / 正式 expand operations / 更强 scale up aggressively
- 发音：SCALE UP重读scale 【IPA head: /tə skeɪl ʌp/】
- 提醒：scale back=缩减，别混
- 练习：We need to ___ ___ our server capacity.

**4. to keep a human in the loop**
- 中文：保留人工参与/把关
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We automate a lot, but we always keep a human in the loop.
- 例句2 [播客]：With medical AI, you have to keep a human in the loop.
- 替换：随意 have a person check it / 正式 maintain human oversight
- 发音：HU-man in the LOOP，in the弱读 【IPA head: /tə kiːp ə ˈhjuːmən ɪn ðə luːp/】
- 提醒：谈AI安全/伦理万能句
- 练习：Should AI make hiring decisions? 用它回答

**5. to double down on...**
- 中文：加倍投入
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Instead of cutting costs, we're doubling down on AI research.
- 例句2 [日常]：I'm doubling down on my English this year.
- 替换：随意 go all in on / 正式 increase investment in
- 发音：DOU-ble DOWN，double美式 /ˈdʌbəl/ 【IPA head: /tə ˈdʌbəl daʊn ɑn/】
- 提醒：表决心
- 练习：We're ___ ___ on video content.

**6. It's still early days.**
- 中文：还处于早期
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：AI video looks cool, but it's still early days.
- 例句2 [采访]：It's still early days for us to analyze the long-term impact.
- 替换：随意 super early / 正式 in the nascent stage
- 发音：EAR-ly DAYS重读，still弱读 【IPA head: /ɪts stɪl ˈɜrli deɪz/】
- 提醒：保持理性神器
- 练习：带耸肩语气跟读2遍

**7. to connect the dots**
- 中文：理清关联
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Once you connect the dots, you can see why users canceled.
- 例句2 [演讲]：My job today is to help you connect the dots between data and decisions.
- 替换：随意 put it together / 正式 synthesize the information
- 发音：con-NECT the DOTS 【IPA head: /tə kəˈnɛkt ðə dɑts/】
- 提醒：乔布斯演讲名句
- 练习：Can you help me ___ the ___?

**8. That begs the question...**
- 中文：这就引出一个问题
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：AI can write code now. That begs the question: what should engineers learn?
- 例句2 [播客]：Which begs the question — where do we go from here?
- 替换：随意 so... / 正式 this raises the question
- 发音：BEGS the QUES-tion，连读 begs‿the 【IPA head: /ðæt bɛɡz ðə ˈkwɛstʃən/】
- 提醒：播客转折万能句
- 练习：用它造一个关于AI的问题

### 主题3：情感表达 Emotional Expression【日常深度交流】

**1. That really hit home for me.**
- 中文：那真的触动了我
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：When you talked about burnout, that really hit home for me.
- 例句2 [演讲]：I know that story hit home for a lot of us in this room.
- 替换：随意 that hit me hard / 正式 that resonated deeply / 更强 hit me right in the heart
- 发音：HIT HOME连读，really弱读，语速放慢 【IPA head: /ðæt ˈriːli hɪt hoʊm fər miː/】
- 提醒：表共鸣
- 练习：降调跟读 That really hit home.

**2. I'm kind of torn between...**
- 中文：我有点纠结
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I'm kind of torn between staying and trying something new.
- 例句2 [日常]：I'm torn — I love the job, but I'm exhausted.
- 替换：随意 I'm stuck / 正式 I'm conflicted / 更强 I'm deeply torn
- 发音：TORN拉长，kind of连读 kinda /ˈkaɪndə/ 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈkaɪnd əv tɔrn bɪˈtwiːn/】
- 提醒：kinda是口语精髓
- 练习：I'm ___ ___ torn between A and B.

**3. I'm still processing it.**
- 中文：我还在消化
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：The news was a lot. I'm still processing it.
- 例句2 [采访]：After the layoff, honestly, I'm still processing it.
- 替换：随意 still wrapping my head around it / 正式 still coming to terms with it
- 发音：pro-CESS-ing美式 /ˈprɑsɛsɪŋ/ 【IPA head: /aɪm stɪl ˈprɑsɛsɪŋ ɪt/】
- 提醒：process在此是动词
- 练习：A: How are you after the move? B: ...

**4. to wear your heart on your sleeve**
- 中文：真性情
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：He's the kind of guy who wears his heart on his sleeve.
- 例句2 [播客]：As a host, I wear my heart on my sleeve — listeners can tell.
- 替换：随意 super open / 正式 emotionally transparent
- 发音：HEART SLEEVE重读，连读 wear‿your 【IPA head: /tə wɛr jɔr hɑrt ɑn jɔr sliːv/】
- 提醒：褒义，形容真诚
- 练习：I ___ my heart on my sleeve when ___.

**5. to light up my day**
- 中文：照亮我的一天
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Your message totally lit up my day.
- 例句2 [日常]：Seeing old friends just lights up my day.
- 替换：随意 made my day / 正式 brightened my day
- 发音：LIGHT UP MY DAY，连读 light‿up，上扬语调 【IPA head: /tə laɪt ʌp maɪ deɪ/】
- 提醒：社交好话术
- 练习：笑脸说3遍

**6. to feel burned out**
- 中文：精疲力尽
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I've been working 60-hour weeks — I'm totally burned out.
- 例句2 [采访]：When did you first realize you were burned out?
- 替换：随意 I'm fried / 正式 experiencing burnout
- 发音：BURNED OUT，美式 /bɜrnd/重r音 【IPA head: /tə fiːl bɜrnd aʊt/】
- 提醒：美式常用burned不是burnt
- 练习：I feel ___ ___ lately.

**7. I feel seen.**
- 中文：我感觉被看见/被理解
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：When you said that, I really felt seen.
- 例句2 [播客]：I think a lot of listeners feel seen when we share these stories.
- 替换：随意 you get me / 正式 feel acknowledged / 更强 feel truly seen and heard
- 发音：FEEL SEEN拉长，慢速真诚 【IPA head: /aɪ fiːl siːn/】
- 提醒：深度对话杀手锏，也常用 I feel heard
- 练习：I feel really seen right now. 跟读

**8. to be on an emotional rollercoaster**
- 中文：大起大落
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：This year has been an emotional rollercoaster for our family.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Starting a company is an emotional rollercoaster — gray days and bright days.
- 替换：随意 ups and downs / 正式 emotional turbulence
- 发音：e-MO-tion-al ROL-ler-coas-ter，注意美式r 【IPA head: /tə bi ɑn æn ɪˈmoʊʃənəl ˈroʊlərˌkoʊstər/】
- 提醒：gray美式拼写
- 练习：Last month was ___.

### 主题4：播客主持 Podcast Hosting【播客】

**1. Let's unpack that.**
- 中文：拆解一下
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：You said failure teaches you. Let's unpack that — what did you learn?
- 例句2 [日常]：That's a big idea. Let's unpack it a little.
- 替换：随意 break that down / 正式 analyze that further
- 发音：un-PACK THAT 【IPA head: /lɛts ʌnˈpæk ðæt/】
- 提醒：主持人必备
- 练习：采访朋友时用一次

**2. To kick things off...**
- 中文：开场
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：To kick things off, tell us where you grew up.
- 例句2 [会议]：To kick things off, let's do a quick round of intros.
- 替换：随意 to start / 正式 to begin
- 发音：KICK THINGS OFF连读，上扬开场感 【IPA head: /tə kɪk ˈθɪŋz ɔf/】
- 提醒：canceled单l美式拼写注意
- 练习：___ kick things ___, introduce yourself.

**3. That ties right into...**
- 中文：正好衔接到
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：That ties right into my next question about leadership.
- 例句2 [播客]：Which ties right into what you wrote about personal growth.
- 替换：随意 that leads to / 正式 that connects directly to
- 发音：TIES RIGHT连读 【IPA head: /ðæt taɪz raɪt ˈɪntu/】
- 提醒：让访谈丝滑过渡
- 练习：用它连接两个话题

**4. Stick around for...**
- 中文：别走开，锁定接下来
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Stick around for the second half — it's super practical.
- 例句2 [播客]：Stick around, we'll be right back after the break.
- 替换：随意 don't go anywhere / 正式 stay tuned for
- 发音：STICK a-ROUND，美式t浊化 【IPA head: /stɪk əˈraʊnd fər/】
- 提醒：留存听众神器
- 练习：热情版跟读2遍

**5. Tell me the story behind...**
- 中文：讲讲背后的故事
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Tell me the story behind quitting your job to travel.
- 例句2 [采访]：Tell me the story behind that gray hoodie!
- 替换：随意 how did... happen? / 正式 share the background of
- 发音：STO-ry be-HIND，连读 tell‿me 【IPA head: /tɛl miː ðə ˈstɔri bɪˈhaɪnd/】
- 提醒：比Why好10倍，采访必用
- 练习：Tell me the story behind ___.

**6. Let's zoom out for a second.**
- 中文：跳出来看大局
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's zoom out for a second — why does this matter for society?
- 例句2 [演讲]：Let's zoom out: what does this mean for the next ten years?
- 替换：随意 big picture time / 正式 consider the broader context
- 发音：ZOOM OUT重读zoom，for a连读 /fərə/ 【IPA head: /lɛts zuːm aʊt fər ə ˈsɛkənd/】
- 提醒：讨论卡住时救场
- 练习：讨论卡住时用它

**7. Quick break, we'll be right back.**
- 中文：休息一下马上回来
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Quick break — we'll be right back with more tips.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Quick pause — we'll be right back to the main story.
- 替换：随意 hold on / 正式 take a brief intermission
- 发音：QUICK BREAK重读，we'll弱读快 【IPA head: /kwɪk breɪk wiːl biː raɪt bæk/】
- 提醒：美式播客固定块
- 练习：模仿播客语调说3遍

**8. Where do we go from here?**
- 中文：接下来何去何从
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：AI is everywhere. So where do we go from here?
- 例句2 [演讲]：We've talked about the past. Where do we go from here?
- 替换：随意 what's next? / 正式 what are the next steps?
- 发音：WHERE...HERE首尾重读，疑问上扬 【IPA head: /wɛr duː wiː ɡoʊ frʌm hɪr/】
- 提醒：收尾/转折万能
- 练习：So ___ do we go ___ here?

### 主题5：公开演讲 Public Speaking【演讲】

**1. Let me paint you a picture.**
- 中文：描绘一个画面
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let me paint you a picture: it's 2030, and your AI assistant runs your day.
- 例句2 [日常]：Let me paint you a picture of what our trip looked like.
- 替换：随意 imagine this / 正式 envision this scenario
- 发音：PAINT PICTURE，连读 paint‿you /peɪntʃu/ 【IPA head: /lɛt miː peɪnt juː ə ˈpɪktʃər/】
- 提醒：开场抓注意力
- 练习：用它开头讲未来场景30秒

**2. Here's the bottom line.**
- 中文：关键是
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：We've tried a lot. Here's the bottom line: users want simplicity.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Here's the bottom line — we need to finalize by Friday.
- 替换：随意 long story short / 正式 in summary
- 发音：BOT-tom LINE，后停顿 【IPA head: /hɪrz ðə ˈbɑtəm laɪn/】
- 提醒：总结拍板用
- 练习：Here's the ___ ___: we need more time.

**3. If you take away one thing...**
- 中文：如果只带走一件事
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：If you take away one thing today, let it be this: start small.
- 例句2 [演讲]：If you take away one thing, remember to center people, not tech.
- 替换：随意 remember one thing / 正式 the key takeaway is
- 发音：TAKE a-WAY ONE THING，连读 take‿away 【IPA head: /ɪf juː teɪk əˈweɪ wʌn θɪŋ/】
- 提醒：结尾金句前摇，center美式拼写
- 练习：用它总结今天学的一件事

**4. Let me be real with you.**
- 中文：跟你说实话
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Let me be real with you — I almost gave up last year.
- 例句2 [播客]：Let me be real — I was scared to publish that episode.
- 替换：随意 honestly / 正式 to be candid
- 发音：REAL拉长，let me连读 lemme /ˈlɛmi/ 【IPA head: /lɛt miː biː riːl wɪθ juː/】
- 提醒：lemme只说不写正式文
- 练习：lemme be real with you 5遍

**5. The stakes couldn't be higher.**
- 中文：至关重要
- 正式度：formal-professional
- 例句1：With climate and AI, the stakes couldn't be higher.
- 例句2 [演讲]：For our kids' future, the stakes couldn't be higher.
- 替换：随意 super important / 正式 of critical importance
- 发音：STAKES HIGH-er，couldn't浊化 【IPA head: /ðə steɪks ˈkʊdənt biː ˈhaɪər/】
- 提醒：大演讲高潮用
- 练习：The stakes couldn't be higher when ___.

**6. So here's my ask.**
- 中文：这是我的请求/呼吁
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：So here's my ask: talk to one stranger this week.
- 例句2 [会议]：So here's my ask — can we get this organized by Monday?
- 替换：随意 here's what I need / 正式 my request is
- 发音：HERE'S MY ASK，ask美式 /æsk/ 【IPA head: /soʊ hɪrz maɪ æsk/】
- 提醒：演讲结尾call to action标配，organize美式拼写
- 练习：用它向朋友提小请求

**7. To turn things around**
- 中文：扭转局面
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：One mentor helped me turn things around in six months.
- 例句2 [演讲]：This city turned things around by focusing on education.
- 替换：随意 turn it around / 正式 reverse the situation
- 发音：TURN THINGS a-ROUND，连读 【IPA head: /tə tɜrn θɪŋz əˈraʊnd/】
- 提醒：讲逆袭故事必备
- 练习：She ___ things ___ after failing.

**8. Let me break this down.**
- 中文：拆解一下
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：There's a lot of hype. Let me break this down in plain English.
- 例句2 [播客]：Let me break this down: step one is to analyze the data.
- 替换：随意 make it simple / 正式 simplify this concept
- 发音：BREAK DOWN，analyze美式 /ˈænəlaɪz/ 【IPA head: /lɛt miː breɪk ðɪs daʊn/】
- 提醒：analyze不是analyse
- 练习：解释一个AI概念，用它开头

### 本轮10个最值得背的chunk
1. Let's unpack that. 2. To be on the same page 3. To move the needle 4. That really hit home for me. 5. I feel seen. 6. Let's double-click on that. 7. It's still early days. 8. Tell me the story behind... 9. If you take away one thing... 10. Where do we go from here?

万能公式：To kick things off... + That ties right into... + Where do we go from here? = 完整播客串场

### 7天复习计划
Day1 跟读：10个各10遍，录音，练 kinda/lemme/loop in/schedule /ˈskedʒuːl/
Day2 会议：loop in, same page, circle back, bottom line各造句2个
Day3 播客：3分钟自播 To kick things off... Let's unpack... That ties... Where do we go?
Day4 情感：100词日记用 hit home, torn, processing, burned out, feel seen
Day5 演讲：2分钟演讲 Let me paint... If you take away... So here's my ask.
Day6 实战：采访+谈判用 double-click, move the needle, stakes, turn things around
Day7 测试：默写10个，抽5个即兴30秒

---

## 第二轮 (2026-10-13) - 全新不重复

主题：采访提问 / 社交闲聊 / 会议谈判 / 远程协作 / 个人成长
场景覆盖：采访 / 日常对话 / 社交闲聊 / 会议 / 谈判 / 远程协作

### 主题1：采访提问 Interview Questions【采访/播客】

**1. What got you into...?**
- 中文：你当初怎么入行/入坑...的？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What got you into AI safety in the first place?
- 例句2 [采访]：What got you into hosting this show?
- 替换：随意 How did you get into...? / 正式 What motivated you to pursue...? / 更强 What really pulled you into...?
- 发音：WHAT GOT you IN-to，重读got+into，连读 what‿got‿you，into弱读 【IPA head: /wʌt ɡɑt juː ˈɪntu/】
- 提醒：比Why do you do this?自然100倍，必备开场
- 练习：What got you into ___? 问朋友3遍

**2. Walk me through...**
- 中文：带我过一遍.../讲讲过程
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Walk me through how you made that career switch.
- 例句2 [采访]：Walk me through the moment you decided to quit.
- 替换：随意 Talk me through / 正式 Describe the process of / 更强 Walk me through step by step
- 发音：WALK me THROUGH，重读walk+through，walk美式 /wɔk/ l不发音 【IPA head: /wɔk miː θruː/】
- 提醒：要故事不要yes/no时用它
- 练习：Walk me through your morning routine. 造句

**3. What's something people get wrong about...?**
- 中文：关于...大家最常见的误解是什么？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What's something people get wrong about working in tech?
- 例句2 [播客]：What's something people get wrong about meditation?
- 替换：随意 What's the biggest myth about...? / 正式 What is commonly misunderstood about...?
- 发音：GET WRONG重读，连读 something‿people，语调上扬提问 【IPA head: /wʌts ˈsʌmθɪŋ ˈpiːpəl ɡɛt rɔŋ əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：挖观点金句，嘉宾最爱答
- 练习：用它问一个你的专业话题

**4. Looking back, what...?**
- 中文：回头看，...?
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Looking back, what would you do differently?
- 例句2 [采访]：Looking back on that gray period, what kept you going?
- 替换：随意 In hindsight... / 正式 Upon reflection... / 更强 Looking all the way back...
- 发音：LOOK-ing BACK，重读back，后停顿再问 【IPA head: /ˈlʊkɪŋ bæk wʌt/】
- 提醒：gray美式拼写，问反思和成长专用
- 练习：Looking back, what ___? 造2句

**5. What was the turning point?**
- 中文：转折点是什么？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What was the turning point for your business?
- 例句2 [深度对话]：What was the turning point in your recovery?
- 替换：随意 When did things click? / 正式 What was the pivotal moment? / 更强 What was the real turning point?
- 发音：TURN-ing POINT，两词重读 【IPA head: /wʌt wʌz ðə ˈtɜrnɪŋ pɔɪnt/】
- 提醒：人物故事必挖，比When好用
- 练习：填空 What was the ___ ___?

**6. What's your take on...?**
- 中文：你怎么看...?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What's your take on remote work in 2026?
- 例句2 [会议]：Quick gut check — what's your take on this deadline?
- 替换：随意 What do you think about...? / 正式 What is your perspective on...? / 更强 What's your honest take on...?
- 发音：TAKE ON重读，what's连读 /wʌts/，take美式 /teɪk/ 【IPA head: /wʌts jɔr teɪk ɑn/】
- 提醒：take在此是名词观点，要观点不要事实时用
- 练习：What's your take on AI? 问3个人

**7. If you had to start over...?**
- 中文：如果重来，你会...?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：If you had to start over, what would you focus on?
- 例句2 [演讲]：If I had to start over, I'd organize my time completely differently.
- 替换：随意 If you did it again... / 正式 If you were to begin anew...
- 发音：START O-ver重读，had to连读 hafta /ˈhæftə/ 【IPA head: /ɪf juː hæd tə stɑrt ˈoʊvər/】
- 提醒：organize美式拼写，假设语气练虚拟语气好句
- 练习：If you had to start over, ___? 造句

**8. What's one thing you wish more people knew about...?**
- 中文：有一件事你希望更多人知道，是什么？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What's one thing you wish more people knew about burnout?
- 例句2 [采访收尾]：What's one thing you wish more people knew about your work?
- 替换：随意 Anything people should know? / 正式 What do you wish were more widely understood?
- 发音：ONE THING WISH重读，语速放慢表真诚 【IPA head: /wʌts wʌn θɪŋ juː wɪʃ mɔr ˈpiːpəl nuː əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：收尾金句题，精美表达
- 练习：用它结束一次朋友聊天

### 主题2：社交闲聊 Small Talk【社交闲聊/日常对话】

**1. What have you been up to lately?**
- 中文：最近在忙啥？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Hey! Long time no see. What have you been up to lately?
- 例句2 [社交]：I canceled my trip — what have you been up to?
- 替换：随意 What's up? What's new? / 正式 How have you been spending your time? / 更强 What have you been up to these days?
- 发音：been up to连读 been‿up‿to，been美式 /bɪn/，上扬热情 【IPA head: /wʌt hæv juː bɪn ʌp tuː ˈleɪtli/】
- 提醒：canceled单l美式，比What are you doing?自然
- 练习：见面打招呼用它3次

**2. How's that going for you?**
- 中文：那进展咋样？对你来说如何？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：You're training for a marathon? How's that going for you?
- 例句2 [日常]：New program, huh? How's that going?
- 替换：随意 How's it going? / 正式 How is that progressing? / 更强 How's that really going?
- 发音：HOW'S THAT GO-ing，重读how+go，连读 how's‿that 【IPA head: /haʊz ðæt ˈɡoʊɪŋ fər juː/】
- 提醒：万能跟进句，显得关心
- 练习：朋友说一件事，你回 How's that going for you?

**3. No way! Are you serious?**
- 中文：不会吧！真的吗？
- 正式度：casual
- 例句1：I just got promoted! — No way! Are you serious? Congrats!
- 例句2 [闲聊]：You traveled to 10 countries this year? No way!
- 替换：随意 Get out! / 正式 Really? That's surprising. / 更强 No freaking way!
- 发音：NO WAY拉长上扬，are you连读 are‿ya /ɑr jə/ 【IPA head: /noʊ weɪ ɑr juː ˈsɪriəs/】
- 提醒：traveled单l美式，情绪要到位
- 练习：惊讶表情说5遍

**4. That sounds awesome / rough.**
- 中文：听起来太棒了/太不容易了
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：You shipped your app? That sounds awesome!
- 例句2 [日常]：No sleep for a week? Man, that sounds rough.
- 替换：随意 Cool! That sucks. / 正式 That sounds wonderful/challenging. / 更强 That sounds absolutely amazing/brutal.
- 发音：SOUNDS AWESOME连读 sounds‿awesome，awesome美式 /ˈɔsəm/ 【IPA head: /ðæt saʊndz ˈɔsəm/ /ðæt saʊndz rʌf/】
- 提醒：awesome/rough是美国闲聊二极，快速共情
- 练习：朋友说事，你回 That sounds ___.

**5. Let's grab coffee sometime.**
- 中文：改天一起喝杯咖啡
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Great meeting you — let's grab coffee sometime!
- 例句2 [社交]：Are you in town? Let's grab lunch next week.
- 替换：随意 Let's hang out / 正式 I'd love to schedule a time to meet / 更强 We gotta grab coffee soon!
- 发音：GRAB COF-fee，重读grab，gotta /ˈɡɑtə/，schedule美式 /ˈskedʒuːl/ 【IPA head: /lɛts ɡræb ˈkɔfi ˈsʌmtaɪm/】
- 提醒：sometime是客套，不定时间也正常
- 练习：告别时说一次

**6. How do you know...?**
- 中文：你跟...怎么认识的？
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：How do you know the host? We went to college together.
- 例句2 [派对]：How do you know Sarah? We work at the same center.
- 替换：随意 How'd you meet? / 正式 How are you acquainted with...?
- 发音：HOW do you KNOW，重读how+know，do you连读 d'ya 【IPA head: /haʊ duː juː noʊ/】
- 提醒：center美式拼写，社交破冰必问
- 练习：How do you know ___? 造句

**7. What do you do when you're not working?**
- 中文：不工作时你喜欢干啥？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：So what do you do when you're not working?
- 例句2 [闲聊]：When you're not analyzing data, what do you do for fun?
- 替换：随意 Any hobbies? / 正式 What are your interests outside of work?
- 发音：NOT WORK-ing重读，when you're连读 when‿yer 【IPA head: /wʌt duː juː duː wɛn jɔr nɑt ˈwɜrkɪŋ/】
- 提醒：比What do you do?更深入，analyze美式z
- 练习：用它问朋友爱好

**8. So good to finally put a face to the name!**
- 中文：终于见到本人了，对上号了！
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：We've emailed for months — so good to finally put a face to the name!
- 例句2 [远程首次见]：I love your posts! So good to put a face to the name.
- 替换：随意 Finally meet you in person! / 正式 Wonderful to finally meet you face to face.
- 发音：FACE to the NAME重读，连读 face‿to，finally /ˈfaɪnəli/ 【IPA head: /soʊ ɡʊd tə ˈfaɪnəli pʊt ə feɪs tə ðə neɪm/】
- 提醒：网友/远程 meeting破冰精美表达
- 练习：视频会议开场用一次

### 主题3：会议谈判 Meeting & Negotiation【会议/谈判】

**1. Let's put a pin in that.**
- 中文：这个先暂停，放一下
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Good point — let's put a pin in that and come back to it.
- 例句2 [会议]：We're running low on time, let's put a pin in this.
- 替换：随意 Let's park that / 正式 Let's table this for now / 更强 Let's put a hard pin in that
- 发音：PUT a PIN重读，pin in连读 pin‿in 【IPA head: /lɛts pʊt ə pɪn ɪn ðæt/】
- 提醒：比Shut up委婉100倍，控场必备
- 练习：跑题时说 Let's put a pin in ___.

**2. Can we meet in the middle?**
- 中文：各让一步，折中行吗？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：You want Friday, I want Monday — can we meet in the middle?
- 例句2 [谈判]：We can't do $10k, but can we find middle ground at $7k?
- 替换：随意 Split the difference? / 正式 Seek a compromise / 更强 Let's find common ground here
- 发音：MEET in the MID-dle，重读meet+middle 【IPA head: /kæn wiː miːt ɪn ðə ˈmɪdəl/】
- 提醒：middle ground=com promise，谈判万能
- 练习：Can we meet in the middle on ___?

**3. What's the timeline on this?**
- 中文：这个时间线是咋样的？
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Sounds good — what's the timeline on this project?
- 例句2 [会议]：Just so we're aligned, what's the timeline?
- 替换：随意 When's this due? / 正式 What is the expected timeframe?
- 发音：TIME-line ON THIS重读，what's /wʌts/ 【IPA head: /wʌts ðə ˈtaɪmlaɪn ɑn ðɪs/】
- 提醒：timeline比time更商务具体
- 练习：What's the timeline on ___? 造句

**4. I have to push back a little.**
- 中文：我得提点不同意见
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I hear you, but I have to push back a little on the budget.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Can I push back on that deadline? It's too tight.
- 替换：随意 I don't think so / 正式 I respectfully disagree / 更强 I have to strongly push back
- 发音：PUSH BACK重读，have to连读 hafta 【IPA head: /aɪ hæv tə pʊʃ bæk ə ˈlɪtəl/】
- 提醒：softener经典，heter+push back=礼貌反对
- 练习：I have to push back on ___. 造句

**5. Let's get the ball rolling.**
- 中文：咱们开始行动吧
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Enough planning — let's get the ball rolling!
- 例句2 [演讲]：To get the ball rolling, let me share one quick win.
- 替换：随意 Let's get started / 正式 Let's initiate / 更强 Let's get this ball rolling!
- 发音：BALL ROL-ling重读ball，连读 ball‿rolling 【IPA head: /lɛts ɡɛt ðə bɔl ˈroʊlɪŋ/】
- 提醒：开项目/开会热场用
- 练习：开会开头说一次

**6. I think we're going in circles.**
- 中文：我觉得我们在原地打转
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We've discussed this for 20 minutes — I think we're going in circles.
- 例句2 [远程会]：Sorry, I feel like we're going in circles here.
- 替换：随意 We're stuck / 正式 We're not making progress / 更强 We're totally going in circles
- 发音：CIR-cles重读，going in连读 going‿in 【IPA head: /aɪ θɪŋk wɪr ˈɡoʊɪŋ ɪn ˈsɜrkəlz/】
- 提醒：I think/I feel like开头更 soft，不伤人
- 练习：讨论卡住时用它救场

**7. Let's do a quick gut check.**
- 中文：快速探一下大家直觉/意见
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's do a quick gut check — thumbs up or down?
- 例句2 [会议]：Quick gut check: does everyone like the gray design or the blue?
- 替换：随意 Quick poll / 正式 Quick pulse check
- 发音：GUT CHECK两词重读，gut美式 /ɡʌt/ 【IPA head: /lɛts duː ə kwɪk ɡʌt tʃɛk/】
- 提醒：gray美式拼写，gut=直觉，美国味浓
- 练习：Let's do a quick gut check on ___.

**8. That works for me / That doesn't work for me.**
- 中文：这个对我可以/不行
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Tuesday at 2? Yeah, that works for me.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Hmm, that price doesn't work for me. Can we adjust?
- 替换：随意 I'm good with that / I'm not good with that / 正式 That is acceptable / That is not feasible
- 发音：WORKS FOR ME重读，works连读 works‿for 【IPA head: /ðæt wɜrks fər miː/ /ðæt ˈdʌzənt wɜrk fər miː/】
- 提醒：比Yes/No更商务，拒绝不伤人
- 练习：That ___ work for me. 造句2句

### 主题4：远程协作 Remote Collaboration【远程/会议】

**1. You're on mute.**
- 中文：你静音了
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Hey, you're on mute — we can't hear you!
- 例句2 [Zoom会]：You froze for a sec. Can you repeat that?
- 替换：随意 Muted! / 正式 You appear to be muted. / 更强 You're still on mute!!
- 发音：YOU'RE ON MUTE重读mute，you're /jɔr/ 【IPA head: /jɔr ɑn mjuːt/】
- 提醒：You froze=卡了，远程万能
- 练习：You're on ___. 跟读3遍带笑意

**2. Can you see my screen?**
- 中文：能看到我屏幕吗？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Can you see my screen? I'll walk you through the doc.
- 例句2 [演示]：Can everyone see my screen? Give me a thumbs up.
- 替换：随意 See this? / 正式 Is my screen visible?
- 发音：SEE MY SCREEN重读，can you连读 can‿ya 【IPA head: /kæn juː siː maɪ skriːn/】
- 提醒：share前必确认
- 练习：Can you see my ___? 造句

**3. Let's take this offline.**
- 中文：这个我们私下/会后聊
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：This is a great detail — let's take this offline so we can stay on track.
- 例句2 [会议]：Let's take this offline and loop in the tech team.
- 替换：随意 Let's chat after / 正式 Let's discuss this separately
- 发音：OFF-line重读，take this连读 take‿this /teɪkðɪs/ 【IPA head: /lɛts teɪk ðɪs ˌɔfˈlaɪn/】
- 提醒：控场+省时间神器
- 练习：跑题时说 Let's take this ___.

**4. Just ping me on Slack.**
- 中文：Slack上戳我一下就行
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：If you need anything, just ping me on Slack.
- 例句2 [协作]：Ping me when you've dropped it in the chat!
- 替换：随意 DM me / 正式 Please message me via Slack
- 发音：PING ME重读，ping美式 /pɪŋ/ 【IPA head: /dʒʌst pɪŋ miː ɑn slæk/】
- 提醒：ping=发短消息，美国职场高频
- 练习：Just ping me on ___. 造句

**5. Let's handle this async.**
- 中文：这个异步处理吧，不用开会
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：No need for a call — let's handle this async.
- 例句2 [远程]：I'll drop a Loom video so we can do this async.
- 替换：随意 No meeting needed / 正式 Let's proceed asynchronously
- 发音：a-SYNC /eɪˈsɪŋk/重读sync 【IPA head: /lɛts ˈhændəl ðɪs eɪˈsɪŋk/】
- 提醒：async是远程高效黑话
- 练习：Let's handle this ___. 跟读

**6. Can we jump on a quick call?**
- 中文：能快速通个话吗？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：This is getting messy over text — can we jump on a quick call?
- 例句2 [协作]：Are you free? Can we hop on a call for 5 mins?
- 替换：随意 Hop on a call? / 正式 Could we schedule a brief call?
- 发音：JUMP ON a QUICK CALL重读jump+call，连读 jump‿on 【IPA head: /kæn wiː dʒʌmp ɑn ə kwɪk kɔl/】
- 提醒：文字说不清时转语音，美国人常用
- 练习：Can we jump on a ___? 造句

**7. After you — go ahead.**
- 中文：你先说
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Oh sorry, we talked over each other — after you, go ahead!
- 例句2 [Zoom]：No no, after you! What were you saying?
- 替换：随意 You go first / 正式 Please, go ahead
- 发音：AF-ter YOU GO a-HEAD，上扬礼貌 【IPA head: /ˈæftər juː ɡoʊ əˈhɛd/】
- 提醒：talk over each other=同时说话抢麦，远程必备礼貌
- 练习：抢话时说 After you 3遍

**8. I'll drop it in the chat / doc.**
- 中文：我扔到聊天框/文档里
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Here's the link — I'll drop it in the chat.
- 例句2 [会议]：I'll drop the organized notes in the shared doc after.
- 替换：随意 I'll send it / 正式 I'll share it via chat
- 发音：DROP IT重读连读 drop‿it，drop美式 /drɑp/ 【IPA head: /aɪl drɑp ɪt ɪn ðə tʃæt/ /aɪl drɑp ɪt ɪn ðə dɑk/】
- 提醒：organized美式z，drop比send更口语
- 练习：I'll drop it in the ___. 造句

### 主题5：个人成长 Personal Growth【深度对话/演讲】

**1. to get out of your comfort zone**
- 中文：走出舒适区
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Hosting a podcast really got me out of my comfort zone.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Growth starts when you get out of your comfort zone.
- 替换：随意 Try something new / 正式 Step beyond your comfort zone / 更强 Way outside your comfort zone
- 发音：COM-fort ZONE重读，comfort美式 /ˈkʌmfərt/ 【IPA head: /tə ɡɛt aʊt əv jɔr ˈkʌmfərt zoʊn/】
- 提醒：成长话题万能，面试也爱问
- 练习：___ got me out of my comfort zone. 造句

**2. to build momentum**
- 中文：积累势头/ momentum
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Start small — once you build momentum, it gets easier.
- 例句2 [日常]：I finally worked out 3 days in a row. Trying to build momentum!
- 替换：随意 Get on a roll / 正式 Gain traction / 更强 Build serious momentum
- 发音：BUILD mo-MEN-tum，重读build+men 【IPA head: /tə bɪld moʊˈmɛntəm/】
- 提醒：moMENtum三音节，别读成moment
- 练习：I'm trying to build momentum with ___.

**3. to get back on track**
- 中文：回到正轨
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I fell off track last month, but I'm getting back on track now.
- 例句2 [对话]：We all fall off track — what matters is getting back on.
- 替换：随意 Get back to it / 正式 Resume progress / 更强 Get fully back on track
- 发音：BACK ON TRACK全重读，连读 back‿on 【IPA head: /tə ɡɛt bæk ɑn træk/】
- 提醒：fall off track=掉队，pair使用才自然
- 练习：I ___ off track, now I'm getting back on.

**4. One step at a time / baby steps.**
- 中文：一次一步，慢慢来
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Don't try to change everything — one step at a time.
- 例句2 [采访]：How did you learn English? Baby steps, man. Baby steps.
- 替换：随意 Slowly but surely / 正式 Incrementally / 更强 One tiny baby step at a time
- 发音：ONE STEP AT a TIME，step重读，连读 step‿at 【IPA head: /wʌn stɛp æt ə taɪm/ /ˈbeɪbi stɛps/】
- 提醒：安慰人+自我安慰神器
- 练习：One ___ at a time. 跟读放慢

**5. to hold yourself accountable**
- 中文：对自己负责，自我监督
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I tell a friend my goals to hold myself accountable.
- 例句2 [成长]：To stay organized, I hold myself accountable with a checklist.
- 替换：随意 Keep yourself honest / 正式 Maintain accountability
- 发音：a-COUNT-a-ble重读count，hold连读 hold‿yourself 【IPA head: /tə hoʊld jɔrˈsɛlf əˈkaʊntəbəl/】
- 提醒：organized美式z，accountable美国成长圈高频
- 练习：I hold myself accountable by ___.

**6. to level up**
- 中文：升级，进阶
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：I want to level up my public speaking this year.
- 例句2 [播客]：If you want to level up your career, master storytelling.
- 替换：随意 Step up your game / 正式 Enhance / advance / 更强 Really level up fast
- 发音：LE-vel UP，重读le+up，连读 level‿up 【IPA head: /tə ˈlɛvəl ʌp/】
- 提醒：游戏术语转成长黑话，年轻商务通用
- 练习：I want to level up my ___.

**7. I'm a work in progress.**
- 中文：我还在成长中，不完美
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：My English isn't perfect — I'm a work in progress!
- 例句2 [深度对话]：We're all works in progress, and that's okay.
- 替换：随意 Still learning / 正式 Continuously improving / 更强 Definitely still a work in progress
- 发音：WORK IN PRO-gress，重读work+pro-，连读 work‿in 【IPA head: /aɪm ə wɜrk ɪn ˈprɑɡrɛs/】
- 提醒：示弱+真诚，拉近距离
- 练习：I'm a work in progress when it comes to ___.

**8. to own your story / mistakes**
- 中文：接纳/直面自己的故事/错误
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Once I owned my story, I stopped feeling ashamed.
- 例句2 [演讲]：She owned her mistakes and the audience trusted her even more.
- 替换：随意 Admit it / 正式 Take ownership of / 更强 Fully own your story
- 发音：OWN YOUR STO-ry，重读own+sto，连读 own‿your 【IPA head: /tə oʊn jɔr ˈstɔri/ /tə oʊn jɔr mɪˈsteɪks/】
- 提醒：own=认领不逃避，美国演讲情感高潮常用
- 练习：I own my ___. 造句

### 本轮10个最值得背的chunk
1. What got you into...? 2. Walk me through... 3. What's your take on...? 4. What have you been up to lately? 5. Let's put a pin in that. 6. I have to push back a little. 7. Let's take this offline. 8. Just ping me on Slack. 9. to get out of your comfort zone 10. I'm a work in progress.

万能公式：What got you into...? + Walk me through... + What's one thing you wish more people knew...? = 采访三连

### 7天复习计划
Day1 采访：用8个采访句采访朋友/AI 15分钟并录音
Day2 社交：What have you been up to? + That sounds awesome/rough 各用3次
Day3 谈判：put a pin, meet in middle, push back, that works/doesn't work 各造句2个
Day4 远程：You're on mute, see my screen, take offline, ping me, async 情景模拟
Day5 成长：写150词英文复盘用 comfort zone, momentum, back on track, level up, work in progress
Day6 串场：完整播客5分钟 What got you + Walk me + take on + story + start over
Day7 测试：默写Top10，抽 What was turning point? / How's that going? 即兴30秒

---

## 第三轮 (2026-10-13) - 长句连说版 2-3句

> 本轮要求：句子更长，两三句一起说，可直接用于播客/采访/演讲/深度交流
主题：日常交流 / 深度对话 / AI科技进阶 / 商务职场进阶 / 演讲故事
场景覆盖：日常对话 / 深度交流 / 播客 / 采访 / 演讲 / 会议

### 主题1：日常交流 Daily Talk【日常对话/社交】

**1. I've been meaning to tell you...**
- 中文：我一直想跟你说...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1 (2句连说)：I've been meaning to tell you — I finally organized my home office. It totally changed how I work in the mornings.
- 例句2 [日常]：I've been meaning to tell you, I canceled my weekend trip. I've been feeling burned out, so I just need to rest.
- 替换：随意 Wanted to tell you / 正式 I intended to inform you / 更强 I've been dying to tell you
- 发音：been /bɪn/弱读，meaning to连读 meaning‿to，重音 MEANING，organize美式z，canceled单l 【IPA head: /aɪv biːn ˈmiːnɪŋ tə tɛl juː/】
- 提醒：开场引出憋了很久的话，比I want to say自然
- 练习：I've been meaning to tell you ___. 连说2句

**2. That reminds me...**
- 中文：这让我想起.../顺便说...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：That reminds me — I ran into Sarah yesterday. We talked for an hour about her new program at the center.
- 例句2 [社交]：Oh that reminds me, have you analyzed last month's travel costs? I think we can cut them in half.
- 替换：随意 Oh yeah / 正式 That brings to mind / 更强 Oh wow that totally reminds me
- 发音：re-MINDS ME重读reminds，连读 reminds‿me，center美式拼写，analyze美式z，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ðæt rɪˈmaɪndz miː/】
- 提醒：话题跳转神器，自然不生硬
- 练习：That reminds me, ___ + 追一句细节

**3. Speaking of which...**
- 中文：说到这个...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Speaking of which, did you see the new AI feature they just shipped? I tried it this morning and it actually works great.
- 例句2 [播客]：Speaking of which, that ties right into our next topic. Let's unpack how remote teams stay organized.
- 替换：随意 While we're at it / 正式 On that note / 更强 Which brings us right to...
- 发音：SPEAK-ing OF WHICH重读speak+which，连读 speaking‿of，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈspiːkɪŋ əv wɪtʃ/】
- 提醒：承接上文，比By the way更连贯
- 练习：Speaking of which, ___ + 2句展开

**4. Long story short...**
- 中文：长话短说...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Long story short, we missed our flight, canceled the hotel, and ended up road-tripping. Honestly it turned out even better.
- 例句2 [采访]：Long story short, I got burned out, quit my job, and started over. It was scary but it moved the needle for me.
- 替换：随意 To make it short / 正式 In summary / 更强 Cut a long story short
- 发音：LONG STO-ry SHORT全重读，canceled单l，连读 story‿short 【IPA head: /lɔːŋ ˈstɔːri ʃɔːrt/】
- 提醒：总结 messy 故事，美国人超爱用
- 练习：Long story short, ___ + 结果1句

**5. You know what? I take that back.**
- 中文：你知道吗，我收回刚才那句
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：You know what? I take that back. I said the movie was boring, but the ending really hit home for me.
- 例句2 [深度对话]：You know what, I take that back. I thought I was fine, but I'm still processing it all.
- 替换：随意 Never mind / 正式 I retract that statement / 更强 OK I totally take that back
- 发音：YOU KNOW WHAT上扬停顿，TAKE THAT BACK重读，连读 take‿that‿back 【IPA head: /juː noʊ wʌt aɪ ˈteɪk ˈðæt ˈbæk/】
- 提醒：自我纠正，显得真诚会反思
- 练习：You know what? I take that back + 原因2句

**6. I swear, this week has been...**
- 中文：我发誓，这周简直...
- 正式度：casual
- 例句1：I swear, this week has been nonstop. I have zero bandwidth and my calendar is just packed.
- 例句2 [日常]：I swear, this gray weather is killing my energy. I can't focus and I just want to stay home.
- 替换：随意 Honestly / 正式 Frankly / 更强 I swear to God
- 发音：SWEAR拉长，重音swear+week，gray美式拼写不是grey 【IPA head: /aɪ swɛr ðɪs ˈwiːk həz biːn/】
- 提醒：夸张抱怨开场，朋友间用，正式场合别用swear to God
- 练习：I swear, this week has been ___ + 细节1句

**7. Fair enough, but...**
- 中文：有道理，但是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Fair enough, but will it actually work on a big team? We've tried similar tools and they never stick.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Fair enough, but that timeline doesn't work for me. Can we meet in the middle and push it to Monday?
- 替换：随意 OK but / 正式 That's reasonable, however / 更强 Fair point, but still...
- 发音：FAIR e-NOUGH重读fair，连读 fair‿enough，后停顿转折 【IPA head: /ˌfɛr ɪˈnʌf bʌt/】
- 提醒：先认同再反对，高情商转折
- 练习：Fair enough, but ___ + 理由1句

**8. Don't even get me started on...**
- 中文：别提...了，一提我就停不下来
- 正式度：casual
- 例句1：Don't even get me started on airline food. Last time I traveled for 12 hours and they ran out of everything.
- 例句2 [闲聊]：Don't even get me started on my inbox. I get 200 emails a day and half of them don't even apply to me.
- 替换：随意 Don't ask about / 正式 I'd rather not delve into / 更强 Oh man don't get me started
- 发音：START重读拉长，get me连读 get‿me，traveled单l 【IPA head: /doʊnt ˈiːvən ɡɛt miː ˈstɑːrtəd ɑːn/】
- 提醒：吐槽专用，语气兴奋+无奈
- 练习：Don't even get me started on ___ + 抱怨2句

### 主题2：深度对话 Deep Conversation【深度交流/采访】

**1. What I'm hearing is...**
- 中文：我听到的是.../你的意思是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What I'm hearing is you've been carrying a lot alone. You've been pushing through without really asking for help.
- 例句2 [采访]：What I'm hearing is you traded money for freedom. You took a pay cut because you wanted to live differently.
- 替换：随意 Sounds like / 正式 My understanding is / 更强 What I'm really hearing is
- 发音：HEAR-ing IS重读hearing，what I'm连读 what‿I'm /wʌtaɪm/ 【IPA head: /wʌt aɪm ˈhɪrɪŋ ɪz/】
- 提醒：复述共情，咨询/采访神器
- 练习：What I'm hearing is ___ + 复述1句

**2. Correct me if I'm wrong, but...**
- 中文：如果我说错请纠正，但...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Correct me if I'm wrong, but it sounds like you felt unseen for a long time. You were doing everything right and nobody noticed.
- 例句2 [会议]：Correct me if I'm wrong, but at a high level we're still not aligned on the budget. We need to circle back tomorrow.
- 替换：随意 If I'm wrong tell me / 正式 Please correct me if I am mistaken
- 发音：cor-RECT ME重读rect，if I'm连读 if‿I'm，语速放慢显尊重 【IPA head: /kəˈrɛkt miː ɪf aɪm rɔːŋ bʌt/】
- 提醒：soften质疑，深度对话不冒犯
- 练习：Correct me if I'm wrong, but ___?

**3. The way I see it...**
- 中文：依我看...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：The way I see it, burnout isn't about working too hard. It's about not feeling seen for the hard work you do.
- 例句2 [演讲]：The way I see it, AI won't replace us. It'll just force us to organize our creativity in a totally new way.
- 替换：随意 In my view / 正式 From my perspective / 更强 Honestly the way I see it
- 发音：WAY SEE IT重读way+see，连读 see‿it，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ðə weɪ aɪ ˈsiː ɪt/】
- 提醒：温和表达观点，比I think更有分量
- 练习：The way I see it, ___ + 解释1句

**4. That must have been a lot to carry.**
- 中文：那一定很不容易/压力很大
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：That must have been a lot to carry. Losing your mom while starting a new job — I can't even imagine how heavy that felt.
- 例句2 [深度]：After you told me about the layoff, all I could think was that must have been a lot to carry. You kept showing up anyway.
- 替换：随意 That sounds tough / 正式 That must have been challenging / 更强 That must have been so much to hold
- 发音：MUST HAVE BEEN连读 must‿have /mʌstəv/，CAR-ry重读，降调共情 【IPA head: /ðæt ˈmʌst əv biːn ə lɑːt tə ˈkæri/】
- 提醒：carry=承受，深度共情精美句
- 练习：That must have been a lot to carry + 安慰1句

**5. I've been sitting with...**
- 中文：我一直在琢磨/消化...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I've been sitting with what you said last week. It really hit home and I think you're right about slowing down.
- 例句2 [播客]：I've been sitting with this idea of gray areas. Not everything is black or white, and that's uncomfortable but true.
- 替换：随意 I've been thinking about / 正式 I've been reflecting on / 更强 I've been really sitting with
- 发音：SIT-ting WITH重读sit，连读 sitting‿with，gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪv biːn ˈsɪtɪŋ wɪð/】
- 提醒：sit with=沉浸思考，文艺深度感
- 练习：I've been sitting with ___ + 感悟1句

**6. Where's your head at these days?**
- 中文：你最近状态/想法如何？
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Where's your head at these days? Are you still torn between staying and leaving, or have you made peace with it?
- 例句2 [朋友]：Hey, where's your head at with work? You seemed burned out last time and I just wanted to check in.
- 替换：随意 How are you really doing? / 正式 What is your current mindset?
- 发音：HEAD AT重读head，连读 head‿at，these days弱读快 【IPA head: /wɛrz jʊər hɛd æt ðiːz deɪz/】
- 提醒：比How are you深10倍，要答案不要客套
- 练习：Where's your head at with ___?

**7. That part really stayed with me.**
- 中文：那部分让我久久难忘
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：That part really stayed with me. When you talked about forgiving your dad, I thought about it for days afterward.
- 例句2 [采访]：What you said about owning your story really stayed with me. I quoted you to my team the next morning.
- 替换：随意 I keep thinking about that / 正式 That resonated deeply
- 替换：随意 I keep thinking about that / 正式 That resonated deeply / 更强 That never left me
- 发音：STAYED WITH ME重读stayed，连读 stayed‿with，降调真诚 【IPA head: /ðæt pɑːrt ˈrɪli steɪd wɪð miː/】
- 提醒：反馈+赞美，嘉宾/朋友都受用
- 练习：That part really stayed with me when ___.

**8. Thanks for trusting me with that.**
- 中文：谢谢你愿意跟我说这些
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Thanks for trusting me with that. I know it wasn't easy to share, and I really honor how open you've been.
- 例句2 [深度]：Thanks for trusting me with that story. I'll hold it with care, and I'm here if you want to unpack it more.
- 替换：随意 Thanks for sharing / 正式 I appreciate your openness / 更强 Thank you so much for trusting me
- 发音：TRUST-ing ME WITH THAT重读trust，trust美式 /trʌst/ r音重 【IPA head: /θæŋks fər ˈtrʌstɪŋ miː wɪð ðæt/】
- 提醒：对方示弱后必回，建信任
- 练习：Thanks for trusting me with that + 1句支持

### 主题3：AI/科技进阶 AI Advanced【播客/演讲/会议】

**1. We're barely scratching the surface of...**
- 中文：我们才刚触及...皮毛
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：We're barely scratching the surface of what AI agents can do. Right now they handle simple tasks, but in two years they'll run whole workflows.
- 例句2 [演讲]：We're barely scratching the surface of personalized learning. We have the data, we just haven't organized it well yet.
- 替换：随意 Just getting started with / 正式 In the early stages of exploring
- 发音：BARE-ly SCRATCH-ing SUR-face，重读scratch+sur-，scratching /skrætʃɪŋ/，organize美式z 【IPA head: /wɪr ˈbɛrli ˈskrætʃɪŋ ðə ˈsɜːrfəs əv/】
- 提醒：表潜力大，演讲/路演好用
- 练习：We're barely scratching the surface of ___.

**2. The model still hallucinates a lot.**
- 中文：模型还是经常胡说八道/幻觉
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The model still hallucinates a lot. It invents citations and links, so you have to double-check everything before you ship it.
- 例句2 [播客]：People think AI never lies, but it still hallucinates. That's why we always keep a human in the loop.
- 替换：随意 It makes stuff up / 正式 It generates inaccurate outputs
- 发音：MO-del hal-LU-ci-nates，重读lu-，hallucinate /həˈluːsɪneɪt/ 【IPA head: /ðə ˈmɑːdəl stɪl həˈluːsəneɪts ə lɑːt/】
- 提醒：hallucinate是AI圈黑话，懂行信号
- 练习：The model still hallucinates, so ___.

**3. Garbage in, garbage out.**
- 中文：输入垃圾，输出垃圾
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Garbage in, garbage out. If you feed the model messy data, you can't expect clean insights. You have to analyze it first.
- 例句2 [会议]：Our dashboard is off because the inputs are off. Garbage in, garbage out — let's fix the source data.
- 替换：随意 Bad data = bad results / 正式 Output quality depends on input quality
- 发音：GAR-bage IN GAR-bage OUT，两遍重读garbage，analyze美式 /ˈænəlaɪz/ 【IPA head: /ˈɡɑːrbədʒ ɪn ˈɡɑːrbədʒ aʊt/】
- 提醒：工程师口头禅，怼人不带脏字
- 练习：Garbage in, garbage out + 例子1句

**4. to automate away...**
- 中文：把...自动化掉/取代掉
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We don't want to automate away the human touch. We want to automate the boring parts so our team can focus on creative work.
- 例句2 [采访]：Do you think AI will automate away junior roles? My take is it'll change them, not erase them completely.
- 替换：随意 Replace with AI / 正式 Automate
- 发音：AU-to-mate a-WAY，重读mate+way，连读 automate‿away 【IPA head: /tə ˈɔːtəmeɪt əˈweɪ/】
- 提醒：automate away自带取代意味，比automate强
- 练习：We automated away ___ + 保留了什么1句

**5. It's not a silver bullet, but...**
- 中文：它不是万能药，但是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：It's not a silver bullet, but it moved the needle on support tickets. We cut response time by 40% in just one month.
- 例句2 [演讲]：AI isn't a silver bullet, but it's the best lever we have right now. If we use it well, we can totally level up.
- 替换：随意 Not perfect but / 正式 Not a panacea, however
- 发音：SIL-ver BUL-let重读silver，连读 silver‿bullet 【IPA head: /ɪts nɑːt ə ˈsɪlvər ˈbʊlət bʌt/】
- 提醒：理性降温+肯定价值，hedging高手
- 练习：It's not a silver bullet, but ___.

**6. We're drowning in data but starving for insights.**
- 中文：数据泛滥却缺洞察
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We're drowning in data but starving for insights. We track everything in three dashboards, yet nobody knows what to do next.
- 例句2 [播客]：Most startups are drowning in data. They collect gray numbers all day but never sit down to connect the dots.
- 替换：随意 Too much data, no answers / 正式 Data-rich but insight-poor
- 发音：DROWN-ing...STARV-ing对比重读，gray美式，drowning /draʊnɪŋ/ 【IPA head: /wɪr ˈdraʊnɪŋ ɪn ˈdeɪtə bʌt ˈstɑːrvɪŋ fər ˈɪnsaɪts/】
- 提醒：精美对比句，演讲金句
- 练习：We're drowning in ___, but starving for ___.

**7. to be ahead of / behind the curve**
- 中文：领先/落后于趋势
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：They're way ahead of the curve on video AI. They shipped early, learned fast, and now everyone else is playing catch-up.
- 例句2 [会议]：Honestly we're behind the curve on automation. Our competitors organized their pipeline last year and we're just starting.
- 替换：随意 Leading / lagging / 正式 At the forefront of / behind competitors
- 发音：a-HEAD OF the CURVE重读head+curve，curve /kɜrv/美式r，organize美式z 【IPA head: /tə biː əˈhɛd əv / bɪˈhaɪnd ðə kɜːrv/】
- 提醒：curve=趋势线，ahead/behind成对记
- 练习：We're ahead/behind the curve on ___.

**8. At the end of the day, it's just a tool.**
- 中文：说到底，它只是个工具
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：At the end of the day, it's just a tool. What matters is how you use it and whether you stay centered on the user.
- 例句2 [深度]：People fear AI will take over, but at the end of the day it's just a tool. We still get to choose what kind of future we build.
- 替换：随意 Bottom line, it's just a tool / 正式 Ultimately it remains a tool
- 发音：END OF the DAY重读end+day，连读 end‿of，center美式拼写 【IPA head: /æt ðiː ɛnd əv ðə deɪ ɪts dʒʌst ə tuːl/】
- 提醒：降温+收尾万能，center不是centre
- 练习：At the end of the day, ___ + 观点1句

### 主题4：商务职场进阶 Business Advanced【会议/谈判】

**1. Let's align on next steps...**
- 中文：咱们对齐一下下一步...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's align on next steps so we're on the same page. I'll take the lead on the proposal and you'll ping the client by Friday.
- 例句2 [会议收尾]：Before we jump off, let's align on next steps. Who's owning what, and what's the timeline?
- 替换：随意 What's next? / 正式 Let's clarify action items
- 发音：a-LIGN ON NEXT STEPS，重读lign+next，align /əˈlaɪn/，jump off=挂电话 【IPA head: /lɛts əˈlaɪn ɑːn nɛkst stɛps/】
- 提醒：align=对齐，会议收尾必备
- 练习：Let's align on next steps + 分工1句

**2. To give you some context...**
- 中文：给你点背景...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：To give you some context, we canceled the old vendor last quarter. Their quality dropped and costs went up 30%.
- 例句2 [采访]：To give you some context, I grew up in a small town. We didn't have internet until I was 15, so tech felt like magic.
- 替换：随意 For background / 正式 For context
- 发音：CON-text重读con，to give连读 give‿you，canceled单l 【IPA head: /tə ɡɪv juː səm ˈkɑːntɛkst/】
- 提醒：解释前摇，听众不懵
- 练习：To give you some context, ___ + 2句背景

**3. The elephant in the room is...**
- 中文：大家心知肚明却不敢说的是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The elephant in the room is budget. We can brainstorm all day, but if we don't have money, none of this will scale.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Let's name the elephant in the room — your team is burned out. We need to fix workload before we add new goals.
- 替换：随意 The big issue / 正式 The unaddressed concern
- 发音：E-le-phant IN the ROOM重读e-+room 【IPA head: /ðiː ˈɛləfənt ɪn ðə ruːm ɪz/】
- 提醒：点破尴尬，控场显勇气
- 练习：The elephant in the room is ___.

**4. Let's not boil the ocean here.**
- 中文：咱们别贪大求全/别搞太大
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's not boil the ocean here. Let's focus on one market, nail it, and then scale up from there.
- 例句2 [远程会]：I love the ambition, but let's not boil the ocean. Can we ship a simple version this week?
- 替换：随意 Keep it simple / 正式 Let's scope this narrowly
- 发音：BOIL the O-cean重读boil+o-，boil /bɔɪl/ 【IPA head: /lɛts nɑːt bɔɪl ðiː ˈoʊʃən hɪr/】
- 提醒：劝聚焦神器，美国管理层高频
- 练习：Let's not boil the ocean + 聚焦1句

**5. I want to flag a risk that...**
- 中文：我想指出一个风险...
- 正式度：professional-formal
- 例句1：I want to flag a risk that we're moving too fast. If we don't pressure-test the data, we'll ship bugs to customers.
- 例句2 [会议]：Can I flag a risk? Our timeline assumes no delays, and that's rarely how it works in real life.
- 替换：随意 Heads up / 正式 I'd like to highlight a risk
- 发音：FLAG a RISK重读flag+risk，连读 flag‿a 【IPA head: /aɪ wɔːnt tə flæɡ ə rɪsk ðæt/】
- 提醒：flag=标记，预警不背锅
- 练习：I want to flag a risk that ___.

**6. Can we pressure-test this idea?**
- 中文：能给这个想法上点压力测试吗？
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Can we pressure-test this idea before we go all in? Let's poke holes in it now so it doesn't fall apart later.
- 例句2 [播客]：I love your plan, but let's pressure-test it. What happens if growth stalls for three months straight?
- 替换：随意 Stress-test / 正式 Rigorously evaluate
- 发音：PRES-sure TEST重读pres+test，pressure /ˈpreʃər/ 【IPA head: /kæn wiː ˈprɛʃər tɛst ðɪs aɪˈdiːə/】
- 提醒：pressure-test=高强度验证，显得专业
- 练习：Can we pressure-test ___?

**7. Let's land the plane here.**
- 中文：咱们收个尾/落地吧
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We've been going in circles for a while. Let's land the plane here and lock in two decisions before we jump off.
- 例句2 [演讲]：To land the plane here, here's my ask — pick one habit and build momentum for seven days straight.
- 替换：随意 Wrap up / 正式 Conclude
- 发音：LAND the PLANE重读land+plane 【IPA head: /lɛts lænd ðə pleɪn hɪr/】
- 提醒：land the plane=收尾决策，会议超时救星
- 练习：Let's land the plane + 总结1句

**8. I'll own that — my bad.**
- 中文：这个我认，是我的问题
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I'll own that — my bad. I forgot to loop you in and the email went out without your review.
- 例句2 [日常]：You're right, I'll own that. I said I'd organize the files and I totally dropped the ball.
- 替换：随意 My fault / 正式 I take responsibility / 更强 I fully own that
- 发音：OWN THAT重读own，连读 own‿that，my bad /maɪ bæd/快读，organize美式z 【IPA head: /aɪl oʊn ðæt maɪ bæd/】
- 提醒：own=认领，my bad超美式口语，敢认错更可信
- 练习：I'll own that + 错误1句

### 主题5：演讲故事 Storytelling【演讲/播客/采访】

**1. Here's what nobody tells you about...**
- 中文：没人告诉你的是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Here's what nobody tells you about success. It's mostly boring habits, not big breakthroughs. You just show up every day.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Here's what nobody tells you about burnout. It doesn't hit all at once — it creeps in slowly while you're still smiling.
- 替换：随意 Secret about / 正式 Little-known truth about
- 发音：NO-bo-dy TELLS YOU重读no-+tells，连读 tells‿you /tɛlzju/ 【IPA head: /hɪrz wʌt ˈnoʊbɑːdi tɛlz juː əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：钩子开头，抓注意力
- 练习：Here's what nobody tells you about ___.

**2. I learned this the hard way...**
- 中文：我是吃了大亏才学会...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I learned this the hard way. I tried to do everything myself, burned out in three months, and had to start over from zero.
- 例句2 [播客]：I learned hiring the hard way. I picked skills over values once, and it nearly tore the team apart.
- 替换：随意 Tough lesson / 正式 Through difficult experience
- 发音：HARD WAY重读hard+way，learned /lɜrnd/美式r 【IPA head: /aɪ lɜrnd ðɪs ðə hɑrd weɪ/】
- 提醒：示弱开场，信任感拉满
- 练习：I learned ___ the hard way + 故事2句

**3. Let me leave you with this...**
- 中文：最后送大家一句话...
- 正式度：professional-formal
- 例句1：Let me leave you with this. If you take away one thing today, let it be this — center people, not just programs.
- 例句2 [演讲收尾]：Let me leave you with this thought. The stakes couldn't be higher, but neither could the opportunity.
- 替换：随意 Final thought / 正式 In closing
- 发音：LEAVE YOU WITH THIS重读leave，连读 leave‿you，center美式，program美式 【IPA head: /lɛt miː liːv juː wɪð ðɪs/】
- 提醒：收尾金句前摇，全场安静听你说
- 练习：Let me leave you with this + 金句1句

**4. Picture this...**
- 中文：想象一下...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Picture this. It's 7am, your AI plans your day, and you actually have time for coffee. That's the future we're building.
- 例句2 [日常]：Picture this — we travel for a month, work async, and never miss a deadline. That's why I love remote work.
- 替换：随意 Imagine / 正式 Envision
- 发音：PIC-ture THIS重读pic+this，picture /ˈpɪktʃər/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ˈpɪktʃər ðɪs/】
- 提醒：比Imagine更画面感，演讲开场王
- 练习：Picture this + 场景2句

**5. It wasn't until... that I realized...**
- 中文：直到...我才意识到...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：It wasn't until I got laid off that I realized how much I tied my worth to my job. I had to rebuild my identity from scratch.
- 例句2 [采访]：It wasn't until my second year traveling that I realized home isn't a place. It's the people who feel like safety.
- 替换：随意 Only then did I realize / 正式 It was only at that point I understood
- 发音：was-N'T un-TIL THAT I RE-a-lized，重音n't+til+real-，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ɪt ˈwʌzənt ənˈtɪl ðæt aɪ ˈriːəlaɪzd/】
- 提醒：倒装强调，故事转折点专用
- 练习：It wasn't until ___ that I realized ___.

**6. And that's when it clicked.**
- 中文：就在那时我突然明白了
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：And that's when it clicked. I wasn't failing — I was just building momentum in the wrong direction. So I changed course.
- 例句2 [播客]：I heard that feedback twice in one week, and that's when it clicked. I needed to listen more and talk less.
- 替换：随意 It hit me / 正式 I had a realization
- 发音：THAT'S WHEN IT CLICKED重读that's+clicked，clicked /klɪkt/干脆收尾 【IPA head: /ænd ðæts wɛn ɪt klɪkt/】
- 提醒：click=顿悟，短句有力，停顿说效果翻倍
- 练习：And that's when it clicked + 领悟1句

**7. Don't sleep on...**
- 中文：别小看.../别错过...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Don't sleep on journaling. I thought it was silly for years, but now it's how I process stress and stay organized.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Don't sleep on small habits. They seem tiny, but they compound and eventually move the needle in a huge way.
- 替换：随意 Don't ignore / 正式 Don't underestimate
- 发音：DON'T SLEEP ON重读sleep+on，连读 sleep‿on，organize美式z 【IPA head: /doʊnt sliːp ɑːn/】
- 提醒：sleep on=忽视，美式年轻商务通用
- 练习：Don't sleep on ___ + 理由1句

**8. If nothing else, remember this...**
- 中文：别的记不住也行，记住这句...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：If nothing else, remember this. You don't have to be perfect — you just have to get back on track one more time than you fall off.
- 例句2 [演讲]：If nothing else, remember this — own your story. People forget slides, but they never forget honesty.
- 替换：随意 At least remember / 正式 Above all, remember
- 发音：NO-thing ELSE re-MEM-ber THIS重读no-+mem-+this 【IPA head: /ɪf ˈnʌθɪŋ ɛls rɪˈmɛmbər ðɪs/】
- 提醒：终极金句，說完停顿让观众记
- 练习：If nothing else, remember ___.

### 本轮10个最值得背的chunk（长句版）
1. I've been meaning to tell you — ... 2. Long story short, ... 3. What I'm hearing is... 4. That must have been a lot to carry. 5. We're barely scratching the surface of... 6. Garbage in, garbage out. 7. Let's not boil the ocean here. 8. I'll own that — my bad. 9. I learned this the hard way... 10. If nothing else, remember this...

万能连说公式：To give you some context... + It wasn't until... that I realized... + And that's when it clicked.

### 7天复习计划（长句输出）
Day1 日常：I've been meaning / That reminds me / Long story short 各连说2-3句并录音
Day2 深度：What I'm hearing / must have been a lot / Thanks for trusting me 找朋友深聊15分钟
Day3 AI：scratching surface / silver bullet / drowning in data 讲一个AI观点90秒
Day4 商务：align next steps / elephant / land the plane 开模拟会收尾
Day5 故事：nobody tells you / hard way / when it clicked 讲自己故事2分钟
Day6 串场：Picture this + If nothing else 完整演讲1分钟
Day7 测试：默写Top10，每个连说2-3句不停顿

---

## 第四轮 (2026-10-13) - 长句连说版

> 主题：情感表达进阶 / 播客主持进阶 / 采访深挖 / 会议谈判说服 / 个人成长进阶
> 场景：日常深度交流 / 播客 / 采访 / 会议 / 谈判 / 演讲

### 主题1：情感表达进阶 Emotional Depth【深度交流/日常】

**1. I'm still wrapping my head around...**
- 中文：我还在努力消化/理解...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I'm still wrapping my head around the move. We packed up ten years in two weeks, and now everything feels unreal.
- 例句2 [深度]：I'm still wrapping my head around what you told me last night. It hit home, and I need a little time to process it.
- 替换：随意 Still trying to get it / 正式 Still coming to terms with / 更强 Still really wrapping my head around
- 发音：WRAP-ping MY HEAD a-ROUND，重读wrap+head，wrapping /ˈræpɪŋ/，organize如用注意z 【IPA head: /aɪm stɪl ˈræpɪŋ maɪ hɛd əˈraʊnd/】
- 提醒：wrap head=费解，比processing更口语画面感
- 练习：I'm still wrapping my head around ___ + 细节1句

**2. It hit me like a ton of bricks.**
- 中文：像砖头一样砸中我，突然重击
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：It hit me like a ton of bricks. I was driving home when I realized I hadn't called my mom in a month.
- 例句2 [演讲]：When the doctor said burnout, it hit me like a ton of bricks. I thought I was fine, but my body was telling a different story.
- 替换：随意 It hit me hard / 正式 It was a sudden realization / 更强 It slammed into me
- 发音：HIT ME LIKE a TON OF BRICKS，重读hit+ton+bricks，连读 hit‿me 【IPA head: /ɪt hɪt miː laɪk ə tʌn əv brɪks/】
- 提醒：ton美式重音，讲顿悟/打击，演讲效果强
- 练习：It hit me like a ton of bricks when ___.

**3. I have mixed feelings about...**
- 中文：对此心情复杂
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I have mixed feelings about the promotion. I'm excited for the pay bump, but I'm scared I'll have zero bandwidth for my family.
- 例句2 [采访]：I have mixed feelings about AI art. I love how fast it is, yet I worry we're losing something human in the process.
- 替换：随意 Torn about / 正式 Ambivalent about / 更强 Really torn / deeply mixed
- 发音：MIXED FEE-lings a-BOUT，重读mixed+feel-，mixed /mɪkst/ 【IPA head: /aɪ hæv mɪkst ˈfiːlɪŋz əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：have mixed feelings固定搭配，比torn更正式一点
- 练习：I have mixed feelings about ___ + 正反1句

**4. I'm learning to sit with discomfort.**
- 中文：我在学着与不适共处
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I'm learning to sit with discomfort. Instead of fixing my anxiety right away, I just notice it and let it pass through.
- 例句2 [播客]：Growth means sitting with discomfort. I used to cancel hard talks, but now I stay centered and listen all the way through.
- 替换：随意 Getting used to being uncomfortable / 正式 Learning to tolerate discomfort
- 发音：SIT WITH dis-COM-fort，重读sit+com-，discomfort /dɪsˈkʌmfərt/，center美式拼写 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈlɜːrnɪŋ tə sɪt wɪð dɪsˈkʌmfərt/】
- 提醒：sit with=不逃避，治疗/成长圈高频
- 练习：I'm learning to sit with ___.

**5. That gave me goosebumps.**
- 中文：让我起鸡皮疙瘩，震撼感动
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：That gave me goosebumps. When the choir stood up and sang, the whole room went silent for three seconds.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Hearing her own her story gave me goosebumps. She was shaking, but she kept going and we all felt it.
- 替换：随意 Gave me chills / 正式 Deeply moving / 更强 Total goosebumps
- 发音：GOOSE-bumps /ˈɡuːsbʌmps/重读goose，连读 gave‿me 【IPA head: /ðæt ɡeɪv miː ˈɡuːsbʌmps/】
- 提醒：goosebumps美式常用，表震撼感动
- 练习：That gave me goosebumps when ___.

**6. My heart sank when...**
- 中文：当...时我的心一沉
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：My heart sank when I saw the email. We had organized everything for months, and one line canceled the whole launch.
- 例句2 [日常]：My heart sank when he didn't show up. I had traveled an hour in gray rain, and then he texted sorry I forgot.
- 替换：随意 I felt crushed / 正式 I was disheartened / 更强 My heart totally sank
- 发音：HEART SANK重读heart+sank，sank /sæŋk/短促，organize美式z，canceled单l，traveled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /maɪ hɑːrt sæŋk wɛn/】
- 提醒：sank是sink过去式，失望瞬间专用
- 练习：My heart sank when ___ + 后果1句

**7. I'm slowly making peace with...**
- 中文：正在慢慢与...和解
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I'm slowly making peace with slowing down. I used to feel guilty resting, but now I see rest as part of the work.
- 例句2 [深度]：I'm making peace with not knowing. I analyzed every option for weeks, and finally I just let the answer come to me.
- 替换：随意 Coming to accept / 正式 Reconciling with / 更强 Finally making peace with
- 发音：SLOW-ly MA-king PEACE WITH，重读slow+peace，making连读 making‿peace，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈsloʊli ˈmeɪkɪŋ piːs wɪð/】
- 提醒：make peace with=和解，温柔成长感
- 练习：I'm making peace with ___.

**8. It brought tears to my eyes.**
- 中文：让我眼眶湿润
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：It brought tears to my eyes. He thanked his dad on stage, and you could tell he meant every single word.
- 例句2 [播客]：Reading those letters brought tears to my eyes. They were from listeners who said the show helped them get back on track.
- 替换：随意 Made me cry / 正式 Deeply moving / 更强 Brought me to tears
- 发音：BROUGHT TEARS TO MY EYES，重读brought+tears，brought /brɔt/ 【IPA head: /ɪt brɔt tɪrz tə maɪ aɪz/】
- 提醒：brought是bring过去式，真诚不夸张，正式演讲也可用
- 练习：It brought tears to my eyes when ___.

### 主题2：播客主持进阶 Podcast Pro【播客】

**1. Let's pause on that for a moment.**
- 中文：咱们在这停一下
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's pause on that for a moment. You said you felt unseen for years — can you take us back to where that started?
- 例句2 [播客]：Let's pause here for a sec. That idea of gray progress is huge, and I don't want listeners to miss it.
- 替换：随意 Hold on / 正式 Let's dwell on that / 更强 Let's really sit with that
- 发音：PAUSE ON THAT FOR a MO-ment，重读pause+mo-，pause /pɔz/美式，gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts pɔːz ɑːn ðæt fər ə ˈmoʊmənt/】
- 提醒：pause=强调金句，控节奏
- 练习：Let's pause on that + 追问1句

**2. For folks who are just tuning in...**
- 中文：刚进来的朋友...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：For folks who are just tuning in, we're talking with Maya about burnout. She just shared how she rebuilt her energy from zero.
- 例句2 [直播]：For folks just tuning in, quick recap — we covered morning routines, and now we're getting into deep work.
- 替换：随意 If you're just joining / 正式 For new listeners
- 发音：FOLKS WHO ARE JUST TU-ning IN，重读folks+tun-，folks /foʊks/，tuning /ˈtuːnɪŋ/ 【IPA head: /fər foʊks huː ɑːr dʒʌst ˈtuːnɪŋ ɪn/】
- 提醒：folks美式亲切，比people更播客味
- 练习：For folks just tuning in, we're talking about ___.

**3. We'll link it in the show notes.**
- 中文：我们会放链接在节目注释里
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：We'll link it in the show notes. It's the book that helped you analyze your habits, plus the program you mentioned for sleep.
- 例句2 [播客]：Love that tool — we'll link it in the show notes so folks can check it out right after this episode.
- 替换：随意 Link in description / 正式 Available in the notes
- 发音：LINK IT IN the SHOW NOTES，重读link+show，link it连读 link‿it，analyze美式z，program美式 【IPA head: /wiːl lɪŋk ɪt ɪn ðə ʃoʊ noʊts/】
- 提醒：show notes固定说法，播客必备
- 练习：We'll link ___ in the show notes.

**4. Let's play devil's advocate here...**
- 中文：咱们唱个反调...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's play devil's advocate here. You say hustle is toxic, but what about people who love working hard and feel alive doing it?
- 例句2 [采访]：I'll play devil's advocate for a sec. If AI is so great, why do so many teams still feel overwhelmed after adopting it?
- 替换：随意 Push back a bit / 正式 Consider the counterargument
- 发音：DE-vil's AD-vo-cate /ˈdɛvəlz ˈædvəkət/，重读de-+ad-，advocate名词重音在前 【IPA head: /lɛts pleɪ ˈdɛvəlz ˈædvəkət hɪr/】
- 提醒：devil's advocate=故意反对以 testing，别真吵架
- 练习：Let's play devil's advocate + 反方1句

**5. Take it away...**
- 中文：交给你了，开始吧
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：You lived it, so take it away. Walk us through that night when everything changed for your family.
- 例句2 [播客]：Maya, take it away. Tell us the story behind quitting that high-paying job to travel for a year.
- 替换：随意 Go ahead / 正式 The floor is yours / 更强 Take it away, my friend
- 发音：TAKE IT a-WAY，重读take+way，连读 take‿it，traveled单l 【IPA head: /teɪk ɪt əˈweɪ/】
- 提醒：take it away=请开始你的表演，热情递麦
- 练习：Take it away + 话题1句

**6. That deserves its own episode.**
- 中文：这个值得单独做一期
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：That deserves its own episode. We barely scratched the surface of trauma and parenting, and I know listeners want more.
- 例句2 [闲聊]：Your dating stories deserve their own episode. That last one about the gray suit guy had me laughing for ten minutes.
- 替换：随意 We need a part two / 正式 That warrants further discussion
- 发音：de-SERVES ITS OWN E-pi-sode，重读serves+own+ep-，episode /ˈɛpɪsoʊd/，gray美式 【IPA head: /ðæt dɪˈzɜːrvz ɪts oʊn ˈɛpɪsoʊd/】
- 提醒：抬嘉宾+留钩子，续集预定
- 练习：That deserves its own episode + 原因1句

**7. Let's bring it back to...**
- 中文：咱们拉回到...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's bring it back to habits. We went deep on mindset, but I want to get practical for folks who feel stuck right now.
- 例句2 [会议]：Let's bring it back to the timeline. We drifted into features, but we still haven't aligned on launch day.
- 替换：随意 Back to / 正式 Let's refocus on
- 发音：BRING IT BACK TO，重读bring+back，连读 bring‿it‿back 【IPA head: /lɛts brɪŋ ɪt bæk tuː/】
- 提醒：bring back=拉回主线，控场不生硬
- 练习：Let's bring it back to ___.

**8. Before we wrap, rapid-fire...**
- 中文：结束前玩个快问快答...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Before we wrap, rapid-fire — coffee or tea? Morning or night? One book you'd re-read every year?
- 例句2 [采访收尾]：Before we wrap, quick rapid-fire. What's one habit, one tool, and one phrase you live by these days?
- 替换：随意 Quick fire round / 正式 Brief closing questions
- 发音：be-FORE WE WRAP RA-pid FIRE，重读fore+wrap+rap-+fire，rapid /ˈræpɪd/ 【IPA head: /bɪˈfɔːr wiː ræp ˈræpəd faɪər/】
- 提醒：wrap=结束，rapid-fire=快问，收尾气氛神器
- 练习：Before we wrap, ___? 连问3个

### 主题3：采访深挖 Interview Deep-Dive【采访/深度对话】

**1. What was going through your head when...?**
- 中文：当...时你脑子里在想啥？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What was going through your head when you got the offer? You had worked toward it for years, yet you looked scared on stage.
- 例句2 [深度]：What was going through your head during that gray winter? You stopped posting, canceled trips, and just went quiet.
- 替换：随意 What were you thinking? / 正式 What were your thoughts at the time? / 更强 What was racing through your mind?
- 发音：GO-ing THROUGH YOUR HEAD WHEN，重读go+head，连读 through‿your，canceled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌt wəz ˈɡoʊɪŋ θruː jʊər hɛd wɛn/】
- 提醒：going through head=脑中闪念，挖心理活动
- 练习：What was going through your head when ___?

**2. What's the cost of staying...?**
- 中文：维持现状的代价是？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What's the cost of staying in a job you hate? You keep the paycheck, but you lose energy, health, and years you can't get back.
- 例句2 [采访]：Everyone asks the cost of leaving, but what's the cost of staying? Sometimes staying put is the riskier move.
- 替换：随意 What do you lose by staying? / 正式 What is the opportunity cost?
- 发音：COST OF STAY-ing重读cost+stay，连读 cost‿of 【IPA head: /wʌts ðə kɔːst əv ˈsteɪɪŋ/】
- 提醒：cost of staying=灵魂拷问，反转思维
- 练习：What's the cost of staying ___?

**3. How has that shaped...?**
- 中文：那如何塑造了...?
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：How has that shaped how you lead now? You burned out once, so I wonder if you run your team differently because of it.
- 例句2 [采访]：How has traveling shaped your work? You've lived in five cities, and your stories feel way more open than before.
- 替换：随意 How did that change...? / 正式 In what way did that influence...?
- 发音：HOW HAS THAT SHAPED，重读how+shaped，shaped /ʃeɪpt/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /haʊ həz ðæt ʃeɪpt/】
- 提醒：shape=塑造，问长期影响
- 练习：How has ___ shaped ___?

**4. What do you do differently now because of...?**
- 中文：因为...你现在做法有啥不同？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What do you do differently now because of burnout? Do you still check Slack at midnight, or have you learned to protect your evenings?
- 例句2 [播客]：What do you do differently because of that failure? You lost a big client, yet you say it made you more organized than ever.
- 替换：随意 What changed for you? / 正式 How has your approach changed?
- 发音：DIF-fer-ent-ly NOW be-CAUSE OF，重读dif-+now+cause，organize美式z 【IPA head: /wʌt duː juː duː ˈdɪfərəntli naʊ bɪˈkɔːz əv/】
- 提醒：different because of=行为改变，挖成长
- 练习：What do you do differently because of ___?

**5. When did you know it was time to...?**
- 中文：你何时知道该...了？
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：When did you know it was time to quit? Was there one morning you woke up and just couldn't do it anymore?
- 例句2 [深度]：When did you know it was time to ask for help? A lot of us wait until we crash, and I'm curious what your moment was.
- 替换：随意 How did you know? / 正式 At what point did you realize?
- 发音：WHEN DID YOU KNOW IT WAS TIME TO，连读 when‿did‿you，know /noʊ/ 【IPA head: /wɛn dɪd juː noʊ ɪt wəz taɪm tuː/】
- 提醒：time to=时机，问决断瞬间
- 练习：When did you know it was time to ___?

**6. What's the hardest part that no one sees?**
- 中文：没人看见的最难部分是？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What's the hardest part that no one sees? On Instagram you look happy traveling, but I bet lonely nights in hotels are rough.
- 例句2 [采访]：What's the hardest part of leading that no one sees? Everyone sees the stage, but nobody sees the 2am decisions you carry alone.
- 替换：随意 What's tough behind the scenes? / 正式 What are the unseen challenges?
- 发音：HARD-est PART THAT NO ONE SEES，重读hard+part+sees，traveling单l美式 【IPA head: /wʌts ðə ˈhɑːrdest pɑːrt ðæt noʊ wʌn siːz/】
- 提醒：no one sees=共情+深挖，嘉宾易破防
- 练习：What's the hardest part about ___ that no one sees?

**7. Who saw you through...?**
- 中文：谁陪你熬过了...?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Who saw you through that dark year? You lost funding, canceled the launch, and still kept going — someone must have had your back.
- 例句2 [深度]：Who saw you through grief? When my dad passed, one friend just showed up every week and never asked for anything back.
- 替换：随意 Who helped you? / 正式 Who supported you through...?
- 发音：WHO SAW YOU THROUGH，重读who+saw+through，连读 saw‿you，canceled单l 【IPA head: /huː sɔː juː θruː/】
- 提醒：see someone through=陪伴度过难关，温暖问法
- 练习：Who saw you through ___?

**8. What would you tell your younger self about...?**
- 中文：你会给年轻的自己什么建议关于...?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What would you tell your younger self about work? Would you say slow down, protect your peace, and don't tie worth to titles?
- 例句2 [演讲]：If I could tell my younger self one thing about fear, I'd say feel it and do it anyway. Courage compounds just like interest.
- 替换：随意 Advice to young you? / 正式 What guidance would you offer?
- 发音：YOUNG-er SELF a-BOUT，重读young+self，tell your连读 tell‿your 【IPA head: /wʌt wʊd juː tɛl jʊər ˈjʌŋɡər sɛlf əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：younger self经典收尾，易出金句
- 练习：What would you tell your younger self about ___?

### 主题4：会议谈判说服 Persuasion【会议/谈判】

**1. The data tells a different story...**
- 中文：数据说的可不一样...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The data tells a different story. We thought users loved video, but they actually spend twice as long on text guides.
- 例句2 [会议]：I hear we're growing, but the data tells a different story. Churn is up 15% and we haven't analyzed why yet.
- 替换：随意 Numbers say otherwise / 正式 The evidence suggests otherwise
- 发音：DA-ta TELLS a DIF-fer-ent STO-ry，重读da-+tells+dif-+sto-，data美式 /ˈdeɪtə/，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ðə ˈdeɪtə tɛlz ə ˈdɪfərənt ˈstɔːri/】
- 提醒：data美式发音，用数据温柔打脸
- 练习：The data tells a different story + 数据1句

**2. Let's look at the trade-offs...**
- 中文：看看权衡...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's look at the trade-offs. If we ship fast, we risk bugs. If we wait, we miss the holiday window and lose momentum.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Every option has trade-offs. Lower price means slower delivery, so let's be clear on what we're optimizing for.
- 替换：随意 Pros and cons / 正式 Evaluate the trade-offs
- 发音：TRADE-OFFS /ˈtreɪdɔfs/重读trade，连读 look‿at 【IPA head: /lɛts lʊk æt ðə ˈtreɪdɔfs/】
- 提醒：trade-offs=取舍，成熟决策信号
- 练习：Let's look at the trade-offs + AB面1句

**3. What's our walk-away point?**
- 中文：我们的底线/离场点是？
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：What's our walk-away point? If they won't go below $20k, we need to know when to thank them and walk away gracefully.
- 例句2 [谈判准备]：Before we jump on the call, what's our walk-away point? We want the deal, but not at the cost of burning out the team.
- 替换：随意 Bottom line? / 正式 What is our BATNA?
- 发音：WALK-a-WAY POINT，重读walk+point，连读 walk‿away 【IPA head: /wʌts ˈaʊər ˈwɔk əˈweɪ pɔɪnt/】
- 提醒：walk-away point=底线，谈判前必对齐
- 练习：What's our walk-away point on ___?

**4. Help me understand where you're coming from...**
- 中文：帮我理解下你的立场...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Help me understand where you're coming from. You want to delay launch, and I want to hear what worries you most about going now.
- 例句2 [冲突]：I hear you pushing back, so help me understand. Are you worried about quality, or is it really about bandwidth?
- 替换：随意 What's your thinking? / 正式 Please elaborate on your perspective
- 发音：HELP ME UN-der-STAND WHERE YOU'RE CO-ming FROM，放慢真诚，连读 help‿me 【IPA head: /hɛlp mi ˌʌndərˈstænd wɛr jʊər ˈkʌmɪŋ frʌm/】
- 提醒：coming from=立场，化解对抗神器
- 练习：Help me understand where you're coming from + 倾听1句

**5. If we can..., would you be open to...?**
- 中文：如果我们能...，你愿意...吗？
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：If we can move the timeline to next Friday, would you be open to adding two more interviews? That would give us real stories to share.
- 例句2 [谈判]：If we can organize payment in two parts, would you be open to starting this week? We can get the ball rolling right away.
- 替换：随意 If we X, will you Y? / 正式 Should we achieve X, would you consider Y?
- 发音：IF WE CAN... WOULD YOU BE O-pen TO...，重读can+o-，open /ˈoʊpən/，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ɪf wi kæn wʊd ju bi ˈoʊpən tu/】
- 提醒：if...would条件交换，谈判万能框架
- 练习：If we can ___, would you be open to ___?

**6. Let's sanity-check the numbers...**
- 中文：咱们核对下数字是否靠谱...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's sanity-check the numbers. We projected 10x growth, but we haven't factored in churn or support costs yet.
- 例句2 [远程]：Before we present, let's sanity-check. Did we analyze last quarter correctly, or are we double-counting travelers?
- 替换：随意 Double-check / 正式 Validate the figures
- 发音：SA-ni-ty CHECK NUM-bers，重读san-+check+num-，sanity /ˈsænəti/，analyze美式z，travelers单l 【IPA head: /lɛts ˈsænəti tʃɛk ðə ˈnʌmbərz/】
- 提醒：sanity-check=合理性检查，比double-check更地道
- 练习：Let's sanity-check ___.

**7. I'm aligned on the goal, just not the approach...**
- 中文：目标一致，只是方法不同...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'm aligned on the goal, just not the approach. I want the same launch date, but I think we should cut features instead of adding hours.
- 例句2 [会议]：We're aligned on helping users, just not on how. You favor ads, I favor a paid program centered on depth over scale.
- 替换：随意 Same goal, different way / 正式 Agree on objectives, differ on methods
- 发音：a-LIGNED ON the GOAL JUST NOT the ap-PROACH，重读ligned+goal+proach，center美式，program美式 【IPA head: /aɪm əˈlaɪnd ɑn ðə ɡoʊl dʒʌst nɑt ði əˈproʊtʃ/】
- 提醒：aligned on goal先站队，再分歧不伤和气
- 练习：I'm aligned on ___, just not ___.

**8. Let's agree on what success looks like...**
- 中文：先定义啥叫成功...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's agree on what success looks like. Is it 1000 signups, or 100 happy customers who stay for a year?
- 例句2 [项目启动]：Before we build, let's agree on success. Otherwise we'll move fast, ship stuff, and still argue about whether it worked.
- 替换：随意 Define done / 正式 Establish success criteria
- 发音：a-GREE ON WHAT suc-CESS LOOKS LIKE，重读gree+suc-+looks 【IPA head: /lɛts əˈɡri ɑn wʌt səkˈsɛs lʊks laɪk/】
- 提醒：success looks like=成功画面，开工前必问
- 练习：What does success look like for ___?

### 主题5：个人成长进阶 Growth Pro【深度/演讲/日常】

**1. I'm trying to be more intentional about...**
- 中文：我想更刻意地对待...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I'm trying to be more intentional about mornings. I stopped checking my phone first thing, and now I journal for ten minutes instead.
- 例句2 [深度]：I'm being more intentional with friendships. I schedule calls like meetings, because otherwise gray weeks just slip by.
- 替换：随意 More mindful about / 正式 More deliberate about
- 发音：in-TEN-tion-al a-BOUT，重读ten+a-，intentional /ɪnˈtɛnʃənəl/，gray美式，schedule美式 /ˈskedʒuːl/ 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈtraɪɪŋ tə biː mɔːr ɪnˈtɛnʃənəl əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：intentional=刻意，成长圈热词
- 练习：I'm trying to be more intentional about ___.

**2. I'm in a season of...**
- 中文：我正处在...的阶段
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I'm in a season of slowing down. I canceled two projects, said no more often, and finally feel like I can breathe again.
- 例句2 [播客]：I'm in a season of rebuilding. After burnout I had to re-organize my life around sleep, walks, and honest talks.
- 替换：随意 Going through a phase of / 正式 In a period of
- 发音：SEA-son OF，重读sea-，season /ˈsiːzən/，canceled单l，organize美式z 【IPA head: /aɪm ɪn ə ˈsiːzən əv/】
- 提醒：season of=人生阶段，诗意不矫情
- 练习：I'm in a season of ___.

**3. to protect your peace / energy**
- 中文：保护你的平静/能量
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I stopped saying yes to everything to protect my peace. Now I check my energy first, and I only commit if it feels right.
- 例句2 [日常]：Protecting your energy isn't selfish. I mute noisy chats, travel less for work, and save Fridays for deep thinking.
- 替换：随意 Guard your energy / 正式 Preserve well-being
- 发音：pro-TECT YOUR PEACE / EN-er-gy，重读tect+peace+en-，travel单l美式 【IPA head: /tə prəˈtɛkt jʊər piːs / ˈɛnərdʒi/】
- 提醒：protect peace=边界感，美国自我关怀高频
- 练习：I protect my peace by ___.

**4. to rewire your brain to...**
- 中文：重塑大脑去...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：You can rewire your brain to notice good things. I write three wins every night, and now I naturally look for progress each day.
- 例句2 [演讲]：We rewired our team to default to async. We stopped defaulting to meetings, documented everything, and got hours back each week.
- 替换：随意 Retrain yourself to / 正式 Recondition
- 发音：re-WIRE YOUR BRAIN TO，重读wire+brain，rewire /riˈwaɪər/ 【IPA head: /tə ˌriːˈwaɪər jʊər breɪn tuː/】
- 提醒：rewire=神经重塑，科学感+行动感
- 练习：I'm rewiring my brain to ___.

**5. to show up for yourself...**
- 中文：为自己挺身而出/对自己负责
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Showing up for yourself is quiet. It's going for a walk when you're tired, keeping promises you made to no one but you.
- 例句2 [深度]：I tell clients to show up for themselves first. You can't pour from an empty cup, so rest isn't lazy — it's loyal.
- 替换：随意 Have your own back / 正式 Honor commitments to yourself
- 发音：SHOW UP FOR your-SELF，重读show+self，连读 show‿up‿for 【IPA head: /tə ʃoʊ ʌp fər jʊərˈsɛlf/】
- 提醒：show up for=到场支持，对自己用很治愈
- 练习：I show up for myself by ___.

**6. to give yourself grace...**
- 中文：对自己宽容点
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Give yourself grace. You missed a week, traveled, got sick — that doesn't erase the months you showed up consistently.
- 例句2 [采访]：I give myself grace now. I used to beat myself up for typos, but now I fix it, learn, and move on fast.
