- 替换：随意 Go easy on yourself / 正式 Practice self-compassion
- 发音：GIVE your-SELF GRACE，重读give+self+grace，grace /ɡreɪs/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /tə ɡɪv jʊərˈsɛlf ɡreɪs/】
- 提醒：grace=恩典/宽容，比forgive更温柔
- 练习：I need to give myself grace about ___.

**7. to stay the course...**
- 中文：坚持既定路线不跑偏
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Stay the course. Results are slow for the first month, but small wins compound and month three looks totally different.
- 例句2 [演讲]：When I wanted to quit, my mentor said stay the course. We stayed centered on users, kept shipping, and it finally clicked.
- 替换：随意 Stick with it / 正式 Remain committed
- 发音：STAY THE COURSE，重读stay+course，course /kɔrs/美式r，center美式 【IPA head: /tə steɪ ðə kɔːrs/】
- 提醒：stay course=航海术语，坚持到底
- 练习：I will stay the course on ___.

**8. Small wins compound...**
- 中文：小胜利会复利
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Small wins compound. I read ten pages a day, and a year later I'd finished 20 books without ever feeling rushed.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Never ignore small wins — they compound. Each organized doc, each honest talk, each workout builds the next one.
- 替换：随意 Little things add up / 正式 Incremental gains accumulate
- 发音：SMALL WINS com-POUND，重读small+wins+pound，compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/，organize美式z 【IPA head: /smɔːl wɪnz kəmˈpaʊnd/】
- 提醒：compound=复利，成长演讲金句
- 练习：Small wins compound + 例子1句

### 本轮10个最值得背的chunk（长句版）
1. I'm still wrapping my head around... 2. That must have been...No— That gave me goosebumps. 3. Let's pause on that for a moment. 4. For folks who are just tuning in... 5. What was going through your head when...? 6. What's the hardest part that no one sees? 7. The data tells a different story... 8. Help me understand where you're coming from... 9. I'm trying to be more intentional about... 10. Small wins compound...

万能连说公式：What was going through your head...? + How has that shaped...? + What do you do differently now because of it? = 深挖三连

### 7天复习计划（长句输出）
Day1 情感：wrapping head / goosebumps / heart sank 各连说2-3句录音
Day2 播客：pause on that / tuning in / bring it back 模拟主持5分钟
Day3 采访：going through head / hardest no one sees / younger self 深挖朋友15分钟
Day4 谈判：data story / trade-offs / walk-away 模拟谈判各2句
Day5 成长：intentional / season of / protect peace 写150词复盘
Day6 串场：Take it away + That deserves episode + rapid-fire 完整收尾
Day7 测试：默写Top10，每个连说2-3句不停顿

---

## 第五轮 (2026-10-13) - Keep Talking 持续说不断片

> 本轮专注：描述 / 回忆 / 讲解 / 分析 / 续航，全部2-3句连说，播客/采访/演讲/日常直接用
> 主题：描述细节 Describe / 回忆故事 Recall / 讲解说明 Explain / 分析评价 Analyze / 续航 Keep Talking

### 主题1：描述细节 Describe【keep talking描述】

**1. It's the kind of place where...**
- 中文：那是个...的地方
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：It's the kind of place where everyone knows your name. You walk in tired, and within five minutes you're laughing with strangers.
- 例句2 [播客]：It's the kind of program where you learn by doing. You don't just watch videos — you build, you ship, and you get feedback the same day.
- 替换：随意 Kind of spot where / 正式 An environment in which / 更强 It's exactly the kind of place where
- 发音：KIND OF PLACE WHERE，重读kind+place，kind of连读 kinda /ˈkaɪndə/，program美式 【IPA head: /ɪts ðə ˈkaɪnd əv ˈpleɪs ˈwɛr/】
- 提醒：kind of place=氛围描述，where后跟人情味细节才生动
- 练习：It's the kind of place where ___ + 细节2句

**2. The vibe is...**
- 中文：氛围是...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：The vibe is super chill but focused. People have laptops open, gray hoodies on, and nobody's pretending to be busy.
- 例句2 [日常]：The vibe at home changed completely. We organized the living room, added warm lights, and now we actually want to stay in.
- 替换：随意 Feels like / 正式 The atmosphere is / 更强 The vibe is totally
- 发音：VIBE IZ /vaɪb ɪz/重读vibe，gray美式，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ðə ˈvaɪb ˈɪz/】
- 提醒：vibe美式高频，比atmosphere年轻自然
- 练习：The vibe is ___ + 2个细节

**3. What stands out is...**
- 中文：最突出的是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What stands out is how quiet the office is. No loud calls, no drama — just people doing deep work for hours at a time.
- 例句2 [采访]：What stood out on your trip? What stands out for me is the food in Texas. I traveled for a week and still dream about the barbecue.
- 替换：随意 What hits you is / 正式 What is most notable is
- 发音：STANDS OUT IZ，重读stands+out，stand /stænd/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ˈwʌt ˈstændz ˈaʊt ˈɪz/】
- 提醒：stand out=显眼，描述抓焦点用
- 练习：What stands out is ___ + 解释1句

**4. To put it in perspective...**
- 中文：给你个概念...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：To put it in perspective, our app went from 100 users to 10,000 in three months. We didn't buy ads — it was all word of mouth.
- 例句2 [演讲]：To put it in perspective, I practiced 15 minutes a day. That's one canceled Netflix episode, and a year later my English leveled up completely.
- 替换：随意 To give you an idea / 正式 For context
- 发音：PUT IT IN per-SPEC-tive，重读put+spec-，put it连读 put‿it，canceled单l 【IPA head: /tə pʊt ɪt ɪn pərˈspɛktɪv/】
- 提醒：perspective=参照系，讲数字必用才有体感
- 练习：To put it in perspective, ___ + 对比1句

**5. It's roughly... / It's about...**
- 中文：大概.../大约...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：It's roughly a 20-minute walk from downtown. You pass a park, cross one bridge, and you're right in front of our center.
- 例句2 [讲解]：It's about three times faster than before. We analyzed the old workflow, cut two steps, and now it takes ten minutes instead of thirty.
- 替换：随意 Around / like / 正式 Approximately
- 发音：ROUGH-ly /ˈrʌfli/重读rough，about弱读 /əˈbaʊt/，center美式，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ɪts ˈrʌfli/ / ɪts əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：roughly+数字=美式模糊精确，显得靠谱不死板
- 练习：It's roughly ___ + 路线/对比1句

**6. If I had to describe it in three words...**
- 中文：三个词形容就是...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：If I had to describe it in three words — calm, honest, and alive. That's why I keep going back every single month.
- 例句2 [采访]：If I had to describe LA in three words? Sunny, sprawling, and a little lonely. You love it and miss home at the same time.
- 替换：随意 In three words / 正式 Succinctly
- 发音：de-SCRIBE IT IN THREE WORDS，重读scribe+three，describe /dɪˈskraɪb/ 【IPA head: /ɪf ˈaɪ hæd tə dɪˈskraɪb ɪt ɪn ˈθri ˈwɜrdz/】
- 提醒：three words=逼自己提炼，播客金句体
- 练习：If I had to describe ___ in three words...

**7. It has this... feel to it...**
- 中文：它有种...的感觉
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：It has this cozy, lived-in feel to it. The couch is worn, books are everywhere, and you instantly feel like you can breathe.
- 例句2 [日常]：Austin has this open, try-anything feel to it. People organize meetups in garages, and strangers actually show up to help.
- 替换：随意 Feels... / 正式 Has an air of
- 发音：HAS THIS... FEEL TO IT，重读has+feel，this弱读快，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ɪt ˈhæz ˈðɪs ˈfil tə ɪt/】
- 提醒：has feel=整体感，比It is cozy更美式
- 练习：It has this ___ feel + 2细节

**8. You walk in and you immediately...**
- 中文：你一进门立刻就...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：You walk in and you immediately smell fresh coffee. The lights are low, jazz is playing, and your shoulders drop in two seconds.
- 例句2 [演讲]：You walk into that gym and you immediately feel pushed. Everyone's working, no one's on phones, and you just want to level up too.
- 替换：随意 The second you enter / 正式 Upon entering
- 发音：WALK IN AND YOU im-ME-di-ate-ly，重读walk+me-，walk /wɔk/ l不发音 【IPA head: /jə ˈwɔk ˈɪn ənd jə ɪˈmidiətli/】
- 提醒：you作主语=带听众进入场景，描述必杀
- 练习：You walk in and you immediately ___ + 2感官

### 主题2：回忆故事 Recall【keep talking回忆】

**1. I still remember the first time...**
- 中文：我仍记得第一次...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I still remember the first time I hosted. My hands were shaking, I forgot my intro, and I talked way too fast for ten minutes straight.
- 例句2 [演讲]：I still remember the first time I traveled alone. I got lost in Chicago, missed my bus, and ended up making two lifelong friends.
- 替换：随意 Never forget first / 正式 Vividly recall
- 发音：STILL re-MEM-ber FIRST TIME，重读still+mem-+first，remember /rɪˈmɛmbər/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈstɪl rɪˈmɛmbər ðə ˈfɜrst ˈtaɪm/】
- 提醒：first time=故事开关，细节越具体越真
- 练习：I still remember first time ___ + 2细节

**2. That takes me back to...**
- 中文：让我回到...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：That takes me back to 2019. We were broke, sharing one laptop, and dreaming about a program nobody believed in yet.
- 例句2 [深度]：That smell takes me back to grandma's kitchen. She'd hum while cooking, and the whole house felt safe and warm.
- 替换：随意 Reminds me of / 正式 Evokes
- 发音：TAKES ME BACK TO，重读takes+back，连读 takes‿me，program美式 【IPA head: /ˈðæt ˈteɪks ˈmi ˈbæk tə/】
- 提醒：take back=穿越，感官触发回忆最自然
- 练习：That takes me back to ___ + 画面2句

**3. I hadn't thought about that in years, but...**
- 中文：好多年没想起，但...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I hadn't thought about that in years, but we used to record songs on my phone. The quality was terrible, yet we played them on repeat.
- 例句2 [播客]：I hadn't thought about that camp in years, but now I see it shaped me. I learned to organize games for 50 kids with zero budget.
- 替换：随意 Forgot about that till now / 正式 Had not recalled
- 发音：HAD-n't THOUGHT a-BOUT THAT IN YEARS，重读hadn't+thought+years，organize美式z 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈhædənt ˈθɔt əˈbaʊt ˈðæt ɪn ˈjɪrz ˈbʌt/】
- 提醒：hadn't thought in years=惊喜回忆，拉近距离
- 练习：I hadn't thought about ___ in years, but ___.

**4. Back when I was... / Back in...**
- 中文：当年我.../在...那时
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Back when I was teaching, I talked for six hours a day. My voice was gone by Friday, but my heart was full every single week.
- 例句2 [采访]：Back in 2020, we canceled everything and went fully remote. It was messy at first, then we built a rhythm that actually worked.
- 替换：随意 When I was / 正式 During the period when
- 发音：BACK WHEN I WAS，重读back，连读 back‿when，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ˈbæk ˈwɛn aɪ ˈwʌz/ / ˈbæk ɪn/】
- 提醒：back when=黄金开场，比When I was更有故事感
- 练习：Back when I was ___, ___ + 结果1句

**5. Growing up, we used to...**
- 中文：小时候我们常常...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Growing up, we used to eat outside every Sunday. My dad grilled, neighbors dropped by, and nobody checked phones because we didn't have any.
- 例句2 [深度]：Growing up, we moved a lot. I went to four schools in six years, so I learned to make friends fast and let go fast too.
- 替换：随意 As a kid / 正式 During childhood
- 发音：GROW-ing UP WE USED TO，重读grow+used，used to /ˈjuːstə/固定读法 【IPA head: /ˈɡroʊɪŋ ˈʌp wi ˈjustə/】
- 提醒：used to表过去习惯，回忆童年万能
- 练习：Growing up, we used to ___ + 细节

**6. Funny story — one time...**
- 中文：搞笑的是，有次...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Funny story — one time I said schedule wrong in a meeting. I used British /ˈʃedjuːl/, everyone laughed, and now I never forget /ˈskedʒuːl/.
- 例句2 [社交]：Funny story — one time I traveled with no bag. Airline lost it, I wore the same gray shirt three days, and still had the best trip.
- 替换：随意 True story / 正式 Anecdotally
- 发音：FUN-ny STO-ry ONE TIME，重读fun+sto+one，schedule美式 /ˈskedʒuːl/，traveled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /ˈfʌni ˈstɔri ˈwʌn ˈtaɪm/】
- 提醒：funny story=轻松插曲，活跃气氛
- 练习：Funny story — one time ___ + 反转1句

**7. As I recall, ...**
- 中文：据我记得...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：As I recall, we launched on a rainy Tuesday. Only ten people showed up, but three of them became our biggest fans for years.
- 例句2 [会议]：As I recall, we agreed to keep it small. We said we'd analyze feedback for two weeks before deciding to scale.
- 替换：随意 If I remember right / 正式 To my recollection
- 发音：AS I re-CALL，重读call，连读 as‿I，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /əz aɪ rɪˈkɔl/】
- 提醒：as I recall=不确定但自信，比I think更稳
- 练习：As I recall, ___ + 细节

**8. Looking back on that summer...**
- 中文：回看那个夏天...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Looking back on that summer, we had no money but so much time. We cooked at home, walked everywhere, and talked until 2am almost daily.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Looking back on that gray summer, I was burned out and didn't know it. I slept ten hours, still felt tired, and finally asked for help.
- 替换：随意 That summer / 正式 In retrospect
- 发音：LOOK-ing BACK ON THAT SUM-mer，重读look+back+sum-，gray美式 【IPA head: /ˈlʊkɪŋ ˈbæk ˈɑn ˈðæt ˈsʌmər/】
- 提醒：looking back on+时间=怀旧滤镜，故事感强
- 练习：Looking back on ___, ___ + 感悟

### 主题3：讲解说明 Explain【keep talking讲解】

**1. Here's how it works in three steps...**
- 中文：三步讲清原理...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Here's how it works in three steps. First you dump ideas, then you organize them by theme, and finally you cut everything that doesn't move the needle.
- 例句2 [播客]：Here's how sleep works in three steps. You wind down without screens, you keep the room cool and dark, and you wake at the same time daily.
- 替换：随意 Three steps: / 正式 The process involves three stages
- 发音：HERE'S HOW IT WORKS IN THREE STEPS，重读here's+works+three，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈhirz ˈhaʊ ɪt ˈwɜrks ɪn ˈθri ˈstɛps/】
- 提醒：three steps=化繁为简，讲解王
- 练习：Here's how ___ works in three steps + 步骤

**2. The idea is pretty simple:...**
- 中文：想法很简单...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：The idea is pretty simple: write less, say more. We cut our meeting from 60 to 20 minutes, and people actually stay focused the whole time.
- 例句2 [演讲]：The idea behind our program is simple. Center the user, remove one friction each week, and let small wins compound over months.
- 替换：随意 Simple idea / 正式 The concept is straightforward
- 发音：i-DEA IZ PRET-ty SIM-ple，重读dea+sim-，idea美式 /aɪˈdiə/ r音，center/program美式 【IPA head: /ði aɪˈdiə ɪz ˈprɪti ˈsɪmpəl/】
- 提醒：simple后必须跟冒号+例子，否则太空
- 练习：The idea is simple: ___ + 例子

**3. In other words, ...**
- 中文：换句话说...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：In other words, we do less but better. Instead of posting daily, we post twice a week and spend the rest analyzing what actually works.
- 例句2 [会议]：In other words, we're behind on hiring. We planned for ten, we have six, and the rest are still stuck in interviews.
- 替换：随意 I mean / 正式 That is to say
- 发音：IN O-ther WORDS，重读o-+words，连读 in‿other，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ɪn ˈʌðər ˈwɜrdz/】
- 提醒：in other words=翻译人话，防听不懂
- 练习：___ . In other words, ___.

**4. To break it down even further...**
- 中文：再拆细一点...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：To break it down even further, think of budget in three buckets. People, tools, and experiments — if one grows too fast, the others starve.
- 例句2 [讲解]：To break it down further, my morning has two blocks. First hour is phone-free writing, second hour is one workout plus breakfast with family.
- 替换：随意 Break it smaller / 正式 To elaborate
- 发音：BREAK IT DOWN E-ven FUR-ther，重读break+fur-，连读 break‿it‿down 【IPA head: /tə ˈbreɪk ɪt ˈdaʊn ˈivən ˈfɜrðər/】
- 提醒：further=更深，层层剥开显得透彻
- 练习：To break it down further, ___ + 分块

**5. Think of it like...**
- 中文：把它想成...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Think of it like gardening. You plant seeds daily, you water even when nothing shows, and months later everything blooms at once.
- 例句2 [播客]：Think of attention like money. You spend it every day, you can't earn it back, so you have to budget it like you mean it.
- 替换：随意 Like / 正式 Analogous to
- 发音：THINK OF IT LIKE，重读think+like，连读 think‿of‿it 【IPA head: /ˈθɪŋk əv ɪt ˈlaɪk/】
- 提醒：like后跟生活比喻，小白秒懂
- 练习：Think of it like ___ + 解释

**6. So basically, what happens is...**
- 中文：基本就是...发生...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：So basically, what happens is we meet, we argue a little, and then we land on something better than any one person's idea.
- 例句2 [日常]：So basically what happens is I travel, I work from cafes, and I send updates async so nobody has to chase me.
- 替换：随意 Basically / 正式 Essentially what occurs is
- 发音：SO BA-si-cal-ly WHAT HAP-pens IZ，重读so+bas-+hap-，basically /ˈbeɪsɪkli/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /soʊ ˈbeɪsɪkli ˈwʌt ˈhæpənz ˈɪz/】
- 提醒：what happens is=过程复述，讲流程万能
- 练习：So basically what happens is ___ + 2步

**7. The key part is... the rest is just details.**
- 中文：关键是...其余都是细节
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：The key part is consistency. The rest is just details — whether you run mornings or nights matters way less than showing up daily.
- 例句2 [演讲]：The key part of our center is trust. The rest — the gray walls, the free coffee — that's just details that support the feeling.
- 替换：随意 Main thing is / 正式 The critical element is
- 发音：KEY PART IZ... REST IZ JUST de-TAILS，重读key+rest+tails，center美式 gray美式 【IPA head: /ðə ˈki ˈpɑrt ɪz ðə ˈrɛst ɪz ˈdʒʌst ˈditeɪlz/】
- 提醒：key+details=抓大放小，显得通透
- 练习：The key part is ___ + 细节1句

**8. Here's the play-by-play of...**
- 中文：全程回放.../一步步讲...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Here's the play-by-play of launch day. We woke at 5, shipped at 9, crashed at noon, then watched signups climb all night long.
- 例句2 [采访]：Give us play-by-play of that night. You walked in nervous, forgot your lines, then owned the stage for twenty minutes straight.
- 替换：随意 Step by step / 正式 Chronological account
- 发音：PLAY-BY-PLAY /ˈpleɪ baɪ ˈpleɪ/三词都重读，连读 play‿by‿play 【IPA head: /ˈhirz ðə ˈpleɪ baɪ ˈpleɪ əv/】
- 提醒：play-by-play体育解说词，讲经历临场感强
- 练习：Here's play-by-play of ___ + 3步

### 主题4：分析评价 Analyze【keep talking分析】

**1. On the one hand... on the other hand...**
- 中文：一方面...另一方面...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：On the one hand, remote gives freedom. You save commute hours and design your day. On the other hand, you miss hallway chats that spark ideas.
- 例句2 [谈判]：On the one hand we need speed, on the other we need quality. If we rush now, we'll pay later fixing bugs nobody wants to own.
- 替换：随意 One side... other side / 正式 Whereas
- 发音：ON THE ONE HAND... ON THE O-ther HAND，重读one+o-+hand 【IPA head: /ɑn ðə ˈwʌn ˈhænd ɑn ði ˈʌðər ˈhænd/】
- 提醒：hands成对出现才完整，分析标配
- 练习：On one hand ___, on other ___.

**2. If you compare A to B...**
- 中文：对比A和B...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：If you compare writing to speaking, writing is slower but deeper. You can organize thoughts on paper, then speak with way more clarity.
- 例句2 [播客]：Compare last year to this year. We canceled three side projects, focused on one program, and revenue actually doubled while stress dropped.
- 替换：随意 Compared to / 正式 In comparison
- 发音：IF YOU com-PARE A TO B，重读pare，compare /kəmˈpɛr/美式r，canceled单l，organize美式z，program美式 【IPA head: /ɪf jə kəmˈpɛr ˈeɪ tə ˈbi/】
- 提醒：compare+具体数字/行为才有说服力
- 练习：If you compare ___ to ___, ___.

**3. The reason behind this is...**
- 中文：背后原因是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The reason behind this is attention. People don't lack time — they lack focus, so they scroll instead of finishing one hard thing.
- 例句2 [会议]：The reason behind churn is onboarding. We analyzed calls and found users get lost in week one, then quietly disappear by week three.
- 替换：随意 Because / 正式 The underlying cause is
- 发音：REA-son be-HIND THIS IZ，重读rea+hind，reason /ˈriːzən/，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ðə ˈrizən bəˈhaɪnd ˈðɪs ɪz/】
- 提醒：reason behind=深挖一层，比because正式
- 练习：The reason behind ___ is ___.

**4. What this boils down to is...**
- 中文：归根结底是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：What this boils down to is trust. You can have the best tools, but if people don't trust each other, remote just feels lonely and slow.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Strip away the hype and what this boils down to is habits. Show up daily, protect your mornings, and let small wins compound.
- 替换：随意 Comes down to / 正式 Ultimately reduces to
- 发音：BOILS DOWN TO IZ，重读boils+down，连读 boils‿down‿to /bɔɪlzdaʊntə/ 【IPA head: /ˈwʌt ˈðɪs ˈbɔɪlz ˈdaʊn tə ɪz/】
- 提醒：boil down=熬浓缩，一句话点睛
- 练习：What this boils down to is ___.

**5. Let's weigh the pros and cons...**
- 中文：权衡利弊...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's weigh the pros and cons. Pro: you learn fast and travel light. Con: you have no routine and sometimes feel untethered for weeks.
- 例句2 [决策]：Let's weigh pros and cons of hiring now. Pro is speed, con is we haven't organized training, so new folks might drown on day one.
- 替换：随意 Good vs bad / 正式 Evaluate advantages and disadvantages
- 发音：WEIGH /weɪ/ + PROS AND CONS，重读weigh+pros+cons，travel单l，organize美式z 【IPA head: /lɛts weɪ ðə proʊz ənd kɑnz/】
- 提醒：weigh发音同way，pros/cons成对说
- 练习：Pros are ___, cons are ___.

**6. The pattern I'm seeing is...**
- 中文：我看到的规律是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The pattern I'm seeing is mornings decide days. When I write before phone, I stay centered. When I scroll first, I chase noise all afternoon.
- 例句2 [播客]：The pattern across guests is clear. They all burned out once, then rebuilt around sleep, walks, and saying no way more often.
- 替换：随意 Trend is / 正式 Observable pattern is
- 发音：PAT-tern I'M SEE-ing IZ，重读pat+see，pattern /ˈpætərn/，center美式 【IPA head: /ðə ˈpætərn aɪm ˈsiɪŋ ɪz/】
- 提醒：pattern=洞察信号，分析显深度
- 练习：The pattern I'm seeing is ___.

**7. When you zoom out, what you see is...**
- 中文：拉远看，你会看到...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：When you zoom out, what you see is progress. Day to day feels gray and slow, but year over year you've traveled so far from where you started.
- 例句2 [演讲]：When you zoom out, AI looks less scary. Every tech wave felt huge at first, then we adapted, organized new jobs, and kept going.
- 替换：随意 Big picture / 正式 From a macro perspective
- 发音：ZOOM OUT WHAT YOU SEE IZ，重读zoom+see，连读 zoom‿out，gray美式，traveled单l，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈwɛn jə ˈzum ˈaʊt ˈwʌt jə ˈsi ɪz/】
- 提醒：zoom out=升维，总结升华用
- 练习：When you zoom out, ___.

**8. This lines up with...**
- 中文：这跟...吻合
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：This lines up with what users told us. They said they wanted fewer meetings, and our data shows calls dropped 30% after we went async.
- 例句2 [采访]：That lines up with my own story. I felt unseen for years, then one mentor listened fully and it changed how I lead today.
- 替换：随意 Matches / 正式 Consistent with
- 发音：LINES UP WITH，重读lines+up，连读 lines‿up‿with /laɪnzʌpwɪθ/ 【IPA head: /ˈðɪs ˈlaɪnz ˈʌp ˈwɪθ/】
- 提醒：line up with=证据互证，分析显严谨
- 练习：This lines up with ___.

### 主题5：续航 Keep Talking【不断片/过渡】

**1. And another thing is...**
- 中文：还有一点是...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：And another thing is sleep. I fixed my mornings, but nights were still messy. Once I set a bedtime, my focus doubled in a week.
- 例句2 [演讲]：And another thing is feedback. We ask for it yearly, but we need it weekly. Small loops beat big reviews every single time.
- 替换：随意 Also / 正式 Additionally
- 发音：AND a-NO-ther THING IZ，重读no-+thing，连读 another‿thing 【IPA head: /ənd əˈnʌðər ˈθɪŋ ɪz/】
- 提醒：another thing=加一点，不断片神器
- 练习：And another thing is ___ + 解释

**2. Not only that, but...**
- 中文：不仅如此...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Not only that, but we saved money too. We canceled two tools, organized files in one doc, and cut costs by $500 a month.
- 例句2 [播客]：She's kind, not only that, but she's consistent. She shows up early, remembers names, and follows through when others flake.
- 替换：随意 Plus / 正式 Moreover
- 发音：NOT ON-ly THAT BUT，重读not+that+but，canceled单l，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈnɑt ˈoʊnli ˈðæt ˈbʌt/】
- 提醒：not only递进加码，越说越强
- 练习：Not only that, but ___.

**3. And on top of that...**
- 中文：雪上加霜/锦上添花...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：And on top of that, I got sick. I had just traveled for work, my sleep was off, and then flu knocked me out for five days.
- 例句2 [日常]：And on top of that gray week, my laptop died. I lost two drafts, missed a deadline, and had to rewrite everything from memory.
- 替换：随意 Besides / 正式 Furthermore
- 发音：ON TOP OF THAT，重读top+that，连读 top‿of‿that，traveled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /ənd ɑn ˈtɑp əv ˈðæt/】
- 提醒：top of=叠加，好坏都可叠
- 练习：On top of that, ___.

**4. Which reminds me of something else...**
- 中文：让我想起另一件事...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Which reminds me of something else we tried. We stopped doing status meetings, switched to async updates, and suddenly Fridays felt free again.
- 例句2 [深度]：Which reminds me of what my dad used to say. He'd say protect your mornings, and twenty years later I finally get what he meant.
- 替换：随意 Reminds me / 正式 That recalls
- 发音：WHICH re-MINDS ME OF SOME-thing ELSE，重读minds+some+else 【IPA head: /ˈwɪtʃ rɪˈmaɪndz ˈmi əv ˈsʌmθɪŋ ˈɛls/】
- 提醒：which开头=自然跳转，不跑题
- 练习：Which reminds me of ___.

**5. Where was I? Oh right...**
- 中文：说到哪了？哦对...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Where was I? Oh right — I was telling you about Austin. We found tacos at midnight, got lost downtown, and laughed till 2am.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Where was I? Oh right, habits. I got sidetracked by travel stories, but let's bring it back to mornings because that's where change starts.
- 替换：随意 Lost my train / 正式 To resume
- 发音：WHERE WAS I OH RIGHT，重读where+right，连读 where‿was‿I，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ˈwɛr wəz aɪ oʊ ˈraɪt/】
- 提醒：where was I=断片自救，全场会笑着等你
- 练习：Where was I? Oh right, ___.

**6. Anyway, to pick up where I left off...**
- 中文：总之回到刚才...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Anyway, to pick up where I left off, we finally shipped after three delays. Users loved the simple version, and we learned less is more.
- 例句2 [播客]：Anyway, picking up where we left off — you were burned out, you took a month off traveling, and then what changed when you came back?
- 替换：随意 Back to story / 正式 To resume
- 发音：A-ny-way TO PICK UP WHERE I LEFT OFF，重读a-+pick+left，left off连读 【IPA head: /ˈɛniweɪ tə ˈpɪk ˈʌp ˈwɛr aɪ ˈlɛft ˈɔf/】
- 提醒：pick up where left off=捡回主线，跑题救星
- 练习：To pick up where I left off, ___.

**7. Let me add some color to that...**
- 中文：补充点细节/画面...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let me add some color to that. It wasn't just busy — it was 14-hour days, cold takeout at midnight, and falling asleep with laptop open.
- 例句2 [采访]：Let me add color to that win. We didn't just grow — we went from gray office with three people to a buzzing center with fifty.
- 替换：随意 For example / 正式 To elaborate
- 发音：ADD SOME CO-lor TO THAT，重读add+co-，color美式拼写不是colour，center美式 gray美式 【IPA head: /ˈlɛt ˈmi ˈæd ˈsʌm ˈkʌlər tə ˈðæt/】
- 提醒：add color=加画面，抽象变具体
- 练习：Let me add color + 细节2句

**8. To take it one step further...**
- 中文：再往深一步...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：To take it one step further, don't just journal. Share one line with a friend daily, and you'll stay accountable without even trying hard.
- 例句2 [演讲]：To take it further, don't just protect mornings. Organize your whole week around energy, not hours, and watch how fast you level up.
- 替换：随意 Even more / 正式 Furthermore
- 发音：TAKE IT ONE STEP FUR-ther，重读take+one+fur-，连读 take‿it，organize美式z 【IPA head: /tə ˈteɪk ɪt ˈwʌn ˈstɛp ˈfɜrðər/】
- 提醒：one step further=升级观点，越说越深不断片
- 练习：To take it further, ___.

### 本轮10个最值得背的keep talking chunk
1. It's the kind of place where... 2. To put it in perspective... 3. I still remember the first time... 4. Growing up, we used to... 5. Here's how it works in three steps... 6. Think of it like... 7. On the one hand... on the other... 8. What this boils down to is... 9. Not only that, but... 10. Let me add some color to that...

万能keep talking链：Here's how it works... + In other words... + To break it down further... + To take it one step further... = 讲解永动机

### 7天复习计划（keep talking不断片）
Day1 描述：kind of place / vibe / stands out 各描述1个地方2-3句录音
Day2 回忆：first time / takes me back / growing up 各讲1个故事90秒
Day3 讲解：three steps / simple / think like 各讲1个概念2分钟
Day4 分析：one hand-other / boils down / pattern 各分析1个话题
Day5 续航：another thing / not only / on top of 连说5分钟不卡
Day6 综合：描述1分钟+回忆1分钟+讲解1分钟+分析1分钟串成4分钟演讲
Day7 测试：Top10抽5个，每个连说2-3句，卡住就用 Where was I? Oh right救场

---

## 第六轮 (2026-10-13) - Keep Talking 2.0 展开/表达/回忆对比/举例/总结

> 长句2-3句连说，描述/表达/回忆/讲解/分析不断片，全新40个
> 主题：细节展开 Expand / 情感表达 Express / 回忆对比 Contrast / 讲解举例 Example / 分析总结 Conclude

### 主题1：细节展开 Expand Details【描述keep talking】

**1. Let me paint a clearer picture...**
- 中文：让我画得更清楚点...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let me paint a clearer picture. Our office isn't fancy — it's a gray loft with ten desks, one loud coffee machine, and whiteboards covered in half-erased ideas.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Let me paint a clearer picture of burnout. You wake tired, you cancel friends twice, and you stare at Slack without really reading a single line.
- 替换：随意 Picture this / 正式 To illustrate more vividly
- 发音：PAINT a CLEAR-er PIC-ture，重读paint+clear+pic-，paint you如用连读 paint‿you，gray美式，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ˈlɛt ˈmi ˈpeɪnt ə ˈklɪrər ˈpɪktʃər/】
- 提醒：paint picture=画面化，比It is...生动10倍
- 练习：Paint clearer picture of ___ + 3细节

**2. To give you a sense of scale...**
- 中文：给你个规模感...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：To give you a sense of scale, we went from 5 to 500 interviews. We organized every note in one doc, analyzed themes weekly, and shipped a report nobody expected.
- 例句2 [播客]：To give you a sense of scale, I traveled 20,000 miles last year. That's 40 flights, 12 time zones, and one very tired suitcase that finally gave up.
- 替换：随意 To show how big / 正式 To contextualize the magnitude
- 发音：SENSE OF SCALE，重读sense+scale，sense /sɛns/，organize美式z，analyze美式z，traveled单l 【IPA head: /tə ˈɡɪv jə ə ˈsɛns əv ˈskeɪl/】
- 提醒：scale=规模，讲数字必带体感
- 练习：Sense of scale: ___ + 数字2个

**3. I'm talking X, Y, even Z...**
- 中文：什么都有，X Y 甚至Z...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：I'm talking late nights, cold pizza, even sleeping on the office couch. We had no funding, but we had momentum that kept us going for months.
- 例句2 [日常]：Austin has everything — I'm talking tacos, live music, even swimming holes in January. You never run out of things to try on weekends.
- 替换：随意 Like... and stuff / 正式 Including... among others
- 发音：I'M TALK-ing X Y E-ven Z，重读talk+e-，talking /ˈtɔkɪŋ/ 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈtɔkɪŋ ˈɛks ˈwaɪ ˈivən ˈzi/】
- 提醒：I'm talking=列举强调，美式口语列清单神器
- 练习：I'm talking ___, ___, even ___.

**4. Day in, day out, it's...**
- 中文：日复一日就是...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Day in, day out, it's writing, walking, and one honest call. Nothing sexy, but those three things keep me centered when work gets chaotic.
- 例句2 [采访]：Day in, day out, it's inbox, meetings, and putting out fires. I love the team, yet I haven't had a deep-thinking morning in weeks.
- 替换：随意 Every day / 正式 On a daily basis
- 发音：DAY IN DAY OUT，重读day+out，连读 day‿in‿day‿out，center美式 【IPA head: /ˈdeɪ ɪn ˈdeɪ ˈaʊt ɪts/】
- 提醒：day in day out=日常循环，讲routine显真实
- 练习：Day in, day out, it's ___.

**5. From top to bottom, it's...**
- 中文：从上到下全是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：From top to bottom, it's organized chaos. Leaders share docs openly, interns ping CEOs directly, and nobody waits for permission to try things.
- 例句2 [描述]：From top to bottom, that house is books. Shelves in hallways, stacks on stairs, even cookbooks in the bathroom that guests actually read.
- 替换：随意 All over / 正式 Throughout
- 发音：FROM TOP TO BOT-tom，重读top+bot-，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈfrʌm ˈtɑp tə ˈbɑtəm ɪts/】
- 提醒：top to bottom=全覆盖，细节流
- 练习：From top to bottom, it's ___.

**6. Every corner of... / Every part of...**
- 中文：每个角落都...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Every corner of the city was alive. Street tacos on one block, jazz on the next, and gray murals covering walls I still think about today.
- 例句2 [日常]：Every part of the launch was messy. We canceled the first date, rewrote the homepage three times, and still shipped with two typos on day one.
- 替换：随意 All over / 正式 Throughout
- 发音：E-ve-ry COR-ner OF，重读e-+cor-，every /ˈɛvri/，gray美式，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ˈɛvri ˈkɔrnər əv/ / ˈɛvri ˈpɑrt əv/】
- 提醒：every corner=沉浸式，带听众逛
- 练习：Every corner of ___ was ___.

**7. It's not just X, it's X with...**
- 中文：不只是X，而是带...的X
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：It's not just coffee, it's coffee with a ritual. We grind beans slowly, we don't check phones, and we actually taste the first sip together.
- 例句2 [播客]：It's not just a program, it's a program with a community. You get lessons, plus weekly calls where people analyze wins and fails honestly.
- 替换：随意 Not only X / 正式 More than X
- 发音：NOT JUST X IT'S X WITH，重读not+with，program美式，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ɪts ˈnɑt ˈdʒʌst ˈɛks ɪts ˈɛks ˈwɪθ/】
- 提醒：not just X=升级定义，显得有洞察
- 练习：It's not just ___, it's ___ with ___.

**8. Close your eyes and imagine...**
- 中文：闭眼想象...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Close your eyes and imagine Monday without meetings. You wake slow, you do deep work till noon, and you end the day with energy left for friends.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Close your eyes and imagine you're 80. You traveled enough, you loved hard, and you never once wished you'd scrolled more on Sundays.
- 替换：随意 Imagine / 正式 Envision
- 发音：CLOSE YOUR EYES AND i-MA-gine，重读close+eyes+ma-，imagine /ɪˈmædʒɪn/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ˈkloʊz jər ˈaɪz ənd ɪˈmædʒɪn/】
- 提醒：close eyes=催眠式带入，演讲开场王
- 练习：Close eyes imagine ___ + 2句

### 主题2：情感表达 Express Feelings【keep talking表达】

**1. I can't even put into words how...**
- 中文：无法言说多么...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I can't even put into words how grateful I am. You stayed late three nights, you rewrote my slides, and you never once made me feel like a burden.
- 例句2 [深度]：I can't put into words how heavy that year felt. I smiled on calls, canceled trips quietly, and cried in my car almost every Friday.
- 替换：随意 No words for / 正式 Indescribable / 更强 Can't even describe
- 发音：CAN'T E-ven PUT IN-to WORDS HOW，重读can't+put+words，canceled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈkænt ˈivən ˈpʊt ˈɪntə ˈwɜrdz ˈhaʊ/】
- 提醒：can't put into words=深到说不出，比very grateful强10倍
- 练习：Can't put into words how ___.

**2. Part of me feels... another part feels...**
- 中文：一部分我觉得...另一部分觉得...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Part of me feels excited, another part feels scared. I want the new role, but I worry I'll lose the quiet mornings that keep me grounded.
- 例句2 [采访]：Part of me feels proud we scaled, another part misses the early days. Back then we were five people in a gray room dreaming way too big.
- 替换：随意 Mixed / 正式 Ambivalent
- 发音：PART OF ME FEELS... A-NO-ther PART FEELS...，重读part+feels，gray美式 【IPA head: /ˈpɑrt əv ˈmi ˈfilz əˈnʌðər ˈpɑrt ˈfilz/】
- 提醒：part of me=复杂不装，深度对话必备
- 练习：Part of me ___, another part ___.

**3. It feels like a weight lifted when...**
- 中文：当...像卸下重担
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：It feels like a weight lifted when I say no. I canceled two calls yesterday, took a walk instead, and my whole afternoon felt lighter.
- 例句2 [日常]：It felt like weight lifted when we finally shipped. Months of gray drafts were over, users replied kindly, and I slept ten hours straight.
- 替换：随意 Relief / 正式 Sense of relief
- 发音：WEIGHT LIF-ted /weɪt ˈlɪftɪd/重读weight+lift-，canceled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /ɪt ˈfilz ˈlaɪk ə ˈweɪt ˈlɪftɪd ˈwɛn/】
- 提醒：weight lifted=如释重负，画面感强
- 练习：Weight lifted when ___.

**4. I get emotional just thinking about...**
- 中文：一想起就想哭/激动...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I get emotional just thinking about graduation. My parents traveled 12 hours, sat in the back crying, and I almost forgot my speech on stage.
- 例句2 [播客]：I get emotional thinking about listeners. They write saying show helped them get back on track, and I read every note twice.
- 替换：随意 Makes me tear up / 正式 Deeply moved
- 发音：e-MO-tion-al JUST THINK-ing a-BOUT，重读mo-+think，traveled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈɡɛt ɪˈmoʊʃənəl ˈdʒʌst ˈθɪŋkɪŋ əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：get emotional=真情流露，示弱拉 trust
- 练习：I get emotional thinking about ___.

**5. There's a mix of pride and...**
- 中文：自豪又夹杂着...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：There's a mix of pride and nerves. We built the program from zero, yet launching still feels like jumping without knowing where you'll land.
- 例句2 [演讲]：There's pride and grief in leaving. I love the team I organized for years, but I know staying would slowly burn me out again.
- 替换：随意 Proud but... / 正式 Complex emotions of
- 发音：MIX OF PRIDE AND...，重读mix+pride，pride /praɪd/，program美式，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈðɛrz ə ˈmɪks əv ˈpraɪd ənd/】
- 提醒：mix of=复杂正向+脆弱，告别/毕业常用
- 练习：Mix of pride and ___.

**6. I feel grounded when...**
- 中文：当...我感到踏实...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：I feel grounded when I cook slowly. I chop, I taste, I don't rush — and the noise in my head finally quiets down for an hour.
- 例句2 [深度]：I feel most grounded on morning walks. No phone, just gray sky and footsteps, and I remember what actually matters that day.
- 替换：随意 Calm when / 正式 Centered when
- 发音：GROUN-ded WHEN /ˈɡraʊndɪd/重读ground，gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈfil ˈɡraʊndɪd ˈwɛn/】
- 提醒：grounded=接大地气，治愈系高频
- 练习：I feel grounded when ___.

**7. It fills my cup when...**
- 中文：当...给我充电...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：It fills my cup when friends drop by unannounced. We make tea, we talk on the couch for hours, and I forget to check my phone completely.
- 例句2 [日常]：Teaching fills my cup. I explain one idea three ways, someone's eyes light up, and suddenly the whole tiring week feels worth it.
- 替换：随意 Energizes me / 正式 Replenishes me
- 发音：FILLS MY CUP WHEN，重读fills+cup，cup /kʌp/短促 【IPA head: /ɪt ˈfɪlz ˈmaɪ ˈkʌp ˈwɛn/】
- 提醒：fill cup=能量补充，美国自我关怀黑话
- 练习：Fills my cup when ___.

**8. Honestly, my heart was pounding when...**
- 中文：老实说当时心狂跳...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Honestly, my heart was pounding when I hit publish. I had rewritten the post five times, analyzed every comment I might get, and still felt naked sharing it.
- 例句2 [采访]：My heart was pounding when I walked on stage. Lights were blinding, 200 faces stared back, and then I forgot to be scared after the first laugh.
- 替换：随意 Nervous when / 正式 Anxious when
- 发音：HO-nest-ly MY HEART WAS POUN-ding，重读hon-+heart+pound-，honestly /ˈɑnəstli/ h不发音，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ˈɑnəstli maɪ ˈhɑrt wəz ˈpaʊndɪŋ ˈwɛn/】
- 提醒：pounding=砰砰跳，紧张故事临场感
- 练习：Heart pounding when ___.

### 主题3：回忆对比 Recall & Contrast【回忆keep talking】

**1. Back then... fast forward to now...**
- 中文：当年...快进到现在...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Back then we had no users. Fast forward to now, we have 50,000 readers and I still write every post like it's for those first ten friends.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Back then I feared speaking. Fast forward to now, I host weekly, I still get nervous, but I know nerves mean I care about getting it right.
- 替换：随意 Then... now / 正式 Previously... presently
- 发音：BACK THEN... FAST FOR-ward TO NOW，重读back+fast+now，fast /fæst/ 【IPA head: /ˈbæk ˈðɛn ˈfæst ˈfɔrwərd tə ˈnaʊ/】
- 提醒：fast forward=电影快进，对比成长炸裂
- 练习：Back then ___. Fast forward now ___.

**2. I used to think... now I think...**
- 中文：曾以为...现在觉得...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I used to think rest was lazy. Now I think rest is strategy — I schedule it like meetings and protect it even harder than work.
- 例句2 [播客]：I used to think success was hustle. Now I think it's alignment — doing fewer things, but staying centered on what actually moves the needle.
- 替换：随意 Thought... now / 正式 Former belief... current view
- 发音：USED TO /ˈjuːstə/ + NOW I THINK，重读used+now，schedule美式 /ˈskedʒuːl/，center美式 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈjustə ˈθɪŋk ˈnaʊ aɪ ˈθɪŋk/】
- 提醒：used to now=认知升级，观点文必备
- 练习：Used to think ___, now think ___.

**3. Little did I know that...**
- 中文：当时哪知道...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Little did I know that coffee would change my career. I went for caffeine, stayed for a chat with a stranger, and left with my first real job offer.
- 例句2 [故事]：Little did I know that gray winter would teach me most. I canceled plans, stayed home reading, and rebuilt my energy one quiet morning at a time.
- 替换：随意 Had no idea / 正式 Unbeknownst
- 发音：LIT-tle DID I KNOW THAT，重读lit+know， gray美式，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ˈlɪtəl ˈdɪd aɪ ˈnoʊ ˈðæt/】
- 提醒：little did倒装=命运伏笔，故事感拉满
- 练习：Little did I know ___.

**4. At the time I didn't get it, but now...**
- 中文：当时不懂，现在...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：At the time I didn't get why dad woke at 5. Now I do — those quiet hours before phones buzz are the only time he truly owns his day.
- 例句2 [深度]：At the time I hated feedback. Now I crave it — I analyze every note, keep what helps, and let the rest go without taking it personally.
- 替换：随意 Didn't understand then / 正式 Retrospectively
- 发音：AT THE TIME I DID-n't GET IT BUT NOW，重读time+didn't+now，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /æt ðə ˈtaɪm aɪ ˈdɪdənt ˈɡɛt ɪt bət ˈnaʊ/】
- 提醒：time didn't get=延迟领悟，共鸣强
- 练习：At time didn't get ___, now ___.

**5. That summer changed how I...**
- 中文：那个夏天改变了我...的方式
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：That summer changed how I work. I traveled with just a backpack, worked async from cafes, and realized I don't need an office to do good work.
- 例句2 [演讲]：That summer in Texas changed how I rest. I napped daily, swam nightly, and learned that slowing down actually speeds up clear thinking later.
- 替换：随意 Transformed / 正式 Altered approach to
- 发音：SUM-mer CHANGED HOW I，重读sum+changed，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ˈðæt ˈsʌmər ˈtʃeɪndʒd ˈhaʊ aɪ/】
- 提醒：that summer changed=转折点，一句话带故事
- 练习：That ___ changed how I ___.

**6. If I could relive that day, I'd...**
- 中文：如果重回那天，我会...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：If I could relive launch day, I'd breathe more. I rushed hellos, forgot to eat, and missed the tiny wins my team kept pointing to all afternoon.
- 例句2 [采访]：If I could relive that talk, I'd slow down. I'd pause after each story, look up more, and let the silence do half the work for me.
- 替换：随意 Do over / 正式 Replay
- 发音：re-LIVE THAT DAY I'D /riˈlɪv/，重读live+day，live动词 /lɪv/别读 /laɪv/ 【IPA head: /ɪf aɪ ˈkʊd riˈlɪv ˈðæt ˈdeɪ aɪd/】
- 提醒：relive=重活，虚拟遗憾+成长
- 练习：Relive that day, I'd ___.

**7. The same place looks different now because...**
- 中文： same地方现在看不同因为...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：The same office looks different now because I do. Last year it felt gray and heavy — now with sleep and boundaries, the same desks feel light and full of ideas.
- 例句2 [日常]：Campus looks different now. Back then I rushed between classes, now I walk slowly, notice trees, and actually taste the cheap coffee I used to gulp.
- 替换：随意 Changed view of / 正式 New perspective on
- 发音：SAME PLACE LOOKS DIF-fer-ent NOW be-CAUSE，重读same+dif-+cause，gray美式 【IPA head: /ðə ˈseɪm ˈpleɪs ˈlʊks ˈdɪfrənt ˈnaʊ bɪˈkɔz/】
- 提醒：same place different=心境变世界变，文艺金句
- 练习：Same ___ looks different now because ___.

**8. We had nothing, yet we had everything because...**
- 中文：一无所有却拥有一切因为...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：We had nothing, yet we had everything because we had time. We cooked together, built slow, and laughed at bugs that would stress us out today.
- 例句2 [演讲]：We had nothing in that garage, yet everything mattered. Five people, one whiteboard, and a belief that small, honest work compounds into something huge.
- 替换：随意 Poor but rich / 正式 Paradoxically fulfilled
- 发音：NO-thing YET E-ve-ry-thing be-CAUSE，重读no-+e-+cause 【IPA head: /wi ˈhæd ˈnʌθɪŋ ˈjɛt wi ˈhæd ˈɛvriθɪŋ bɪˈkɔz/】
- 提醒：nothing yet everything=对比排比，演讲高潮
- 练习：Had nothing yet everything because ___.

### 主题4：讲解举例 Explain with Examples【讲解keep talking】

**1. Take... for example...**
- 中文：拿...举例...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Take mornings for example. I don't check Slack till 9, I write for 30 minutes first, and my stress dropped by half in just two weeks.
- 例句2 [会议]：Take our center in Austin. We organized events weekly, kept them small at 20 people, and now half our clients come from those cozy nights.
- 替换：随意 Like / 正式 For instance
- 发音：TAKE... FOR ex-AM-ple，重读take+am-，example /ɪɡˈzæmpəl/，center美式，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈteɪk fər ɪɡˈzæmpəl/】
- 提醒：take for example=点名举例，比for example更口语
- 练习：Take ___ for example + 2句

**2. Say you're... here's what you'd do...**
- 中文：假设你...你会...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Say you're burned out and can't focus. Here's what you'd do — cancel one thing today, walk 20 minutes, and write tomorrow's top three tonight.
- 例句2 [播客]：Say you're new to remote. You'd set work hours, over-communicate on Slack, and do weekly demos so nobody wonders what you're doing all day.
- 替换：随意 If you... / 正式 Suppose you...
- 发音：SAY YOU'RE... HERE'S WHAT YOU'D DO，重读say+here's+do，连读 say‿you're，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ˈseɪ jər ˈhirz ˈwʌt jud ˈdu/】
- 提醒：say you're=假设带入，教程体
- 练习：Say you're ___, you'd ___.

**3. The process looks like this in real life...**
- 中文：现实中流程是这样...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The process looks like this in real life. Monday we brainstorm, Wednesday we cut half the ideas, Friday we ship one small test and analyze results.
- 例句2 [讲解]：Hiring looks like this here. We do two chats, one paid trial day, then the team votes — no endless rounds that burn everyone out.
- 替换：随意 In practice / 正式 In practice it entails
- 发音：PRO-cess LOOKS LIKE THIS IN REAL LIFE，重读pro-+looks+real，process /ˈprɑsɛs/美式，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ðə ˈprɑsɛs ˈlʊks ˈlaɪk ˈðɪs ɪn ˈril ˈlaɪf/】
- 提醒：in real life=去理论化，接地气
- 练习：Process looks like ___ in real life.

**4. A good example is when we...**
- 中文：好例子是我们那次...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：A good example is when we canceled launch. We were scared, we told users honestly, and they trusted us more because we owned the delay early.
- 例句2 [演讲]：A good example is my gray year. I stopped posting, focused on sleep and walks, and came back with clearer ideas than three years of hustle gave me.
- 替换：随意 Like when / 正式 Exemplified by
- 发音：GOOD ex-AM-ple IZ WHEN WE，重读good+am-，canceled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /ə ˈɡʊd ɪɡˈzæmpəl ɪz ˈwɛn ˈwi/】
- 提醒：good example when=故事证据，说服力强
- 练习：Good example is when we ___.

**5. Here's a quick demo of how...**
- 中文：快速演示下如何...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Here's a quick demo of how I plan. I open a blank doc, I dump five tasks, I pick one lever that moves the needle and ignore the rest till noon.
- 例句2 [远程]：Quick demo of async update. I record two minutes on Loom, I drop link in chat, and nobody needs to jump on a call to stay aligned.
- 替换：随意 Let me show / 正式 Demonstration
- 发音：QUICK DE-mo OF HOW，重读quick+de-，demo /ˈdɛmoʊ/ 【IPA head: /ˈhirz ə ˈkwɪk ˈdɛmoʊ əv ˈhaʊ/】
- 提醒：demo=展示，科技/教学高频
- 练习：Quick demo of how ___.

**6. It works like this day to day...**
- 中文：日常运转是这样...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：It works like this day to day. Mornings are phone-free writing, afternoons are meetings in batches, evenings are totally off unless it's truly urgent.
- 例句2 [日常]：Remote works like this for us. We overlap four hours live, handle the rest async, and meet in person twice a year to travel and bond together.
- 替换：随意 Daily / 正式 On a day-to-day basis
- 发音：WORKS LIKE THIS DAY TO DAY，重读works+day，travel单l 【IPA head: /ɪt ˈwɜrks ˈlaɪk ˈðɪs ˈdeɪ tə ˈdeɪ/】
- 提醒：day to day=日常流，讲系统显靠谱
- 练习：Works like this day to day: ___.

**7. Most people do X, but we do Y by...**
- 中文：多数人X，我们Y靠...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Most people do big launches, but we do tiny tests by shipping weekly. We talk to five users, we fix one pain, and we repeat until it clicks.
- 例句2 [播客]：Most people take notes to remember, but I take notes to think. I rewrite ideas in my own words, I connect dots, and I throw away half by Friday.
- 替换：随意 Unlike most / 正式 In contrast
- 发音：MOST PEO-ple DO X BUT WE DO Y BY，重读most+but 【IPA head: /ˈmoʊst ˈpipəl ˈdu ˈɛks ˈbʌt ˈwi ˈdu ˈwaɪ ˈbaɪ/】
- 提醒：most vs we=差异化，讲优势必用
- 练习：Most do ___, we do ___ by ___.

**8. Let me show you what I mean with numbers...**
- 中文：用数字给你看...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let me show you what I mean with numbers. We cut meetings from 12 to 4 hours weekly, focus time tripled, and output actually went up 20%.
- 例句2 [谈判]：Let me show numbers. Last program cost $8k and got 200 signups. This lean version costs $2k, and we project 300 because we finally fixed onboarding.
- 替换：随意 For numbers / 正式 Quantitatively
- 发音：SHOW YOU WHAT I MEAN WITH NUM-bers，重读show+mean+num-，program美式 【IPA head: /ˈlɛt ˈmi ˈʃoʊ jə ˈwʌt aɪ ˈmin ˈwɪθ ˈnʌmbərz/】
- 提醒：with numbers=证据砸脸，谈判加薪必备
- 练习：With numbers: ___ + 数字

### 主题5：分析总结 Analyze & Conclude【分析keep talking】

**1. When you break it down, there are really two drivers...**
- 中文：拆开看就两个动力...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：When you break it down, there are two drivers — fear and curiosity. Fear makes you freeze and over-analyze, curiosity makes you ship and learn fast from users.
- 例句2 [会议]：Break it down and there are two drivers of churn. One is confusing onboarding, the other is slow support that leaves people hanging for days.
- 替换：随意 Two reasons / 正式 Two primary factors
- 发音：BREAK IT DOWN THERE ARE TWO DRI-vers，重读break+two+dri-，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ˈwɛn jə ˈbreɪk ɪt ˈdaʊn ˈðɛr ɑr ˈrili ˈtu ˈdraɪvərz/】
- 提醒：two drivers=化繁为二，透彻感
- 练习：Two drivers are ___ and ___.

**2. The tradeoff nobody talks about is...**
- 中文：没人说的代价是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The tradeoff nobody talks about is speed for health. You ship faster, you travel more, and quietly your sleep, mood, and mornings all pay for it.
- 例句2 [深度]：The tradeoff of saying yes is focus. Every yes to a gray meeting is a no to deep work, rest, or time with people you actually love.
- 替换：随意 Hidden cost / 正式 Unacknowledged tradeoff
- 发音：TRADE-off NO-bo-dy TALKS a-BOUT，重读trade+talks，tradeoff /ˈtreɪdɔf/，travel单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /ðə ˈtreɪdɔf ˈnoʊbɑdi ˈtɔks əˈbaʊt/】
- 提醒：nobody talks about=揭露真相，洞察人设
- 练习：Tradeoff nobody talks about is ___.

**3. Short term... long term...**
- 中文：短期...长期...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Short term it's painful. You say no, you disappoint people, you feel guilty for a week. Long term it's freedom — more energy, better work, calmer mornings.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Short term AI feels messy. Tools break, outputs need fixing. Long term it's leverage — small teams doing work that used to need fifty people.
- 替换：随意 Now vs later / 正式 In the short/long run
- 发音：SHORT TERM... LONG TERM...，重读short+long，对比语调先降后升 【IPA head: /ˈʃɔrt ˈtɜrm ˈlɔŋ ˈtɜrm/】
- 提醒：short long成对=时间纵深，分析成熟
- 练习：Short term ___, long term ___.

**4. What's working is... what's not is...**
- 中文：有效的是...无效的是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：What's working is mornings. I write early and feel centered till noon. What's not is afternoons — I stack meetings and end up drained with zero output.
- 例句2 [复盘]：What's working is our small events. What's not is ads — we spend $1k, analyze clicks for hours, and get travelers who never come back.
- 替换：随意 Good... bad / 正式 Effective... ineffective
- 发音：WHAT'S WORK-ing IZ... WHAT'S NOT IZ...，重读work+not，center美式，analyze美式z，travelers单l 【IPA head: /ˈwʌts ˈwɜrkɪŋ ɪz ˈwʌts ˈnɑt ɪz/】
- 提醒：working vs not=复盘框架，会议周报神器
- 练习：Working is ___, not is ___.

**5. If this keeps going, what happens is...**
- 中文：如果继续，结果是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：If this keeps going, what happens is burnout. You skip rest for a month, your patience thins, and the work you love starts feeling gray and heavy.
- 例句2 [演讲]：If AI keeps going this fast, what happens is junior work changes. Routine drafts get automated, and human taste, trust, and stories matter even more.
- 替换：随意 At this rate / 正式 Should this continue
- 发音：IF THIS KEEPS GO-ing WHAT HAP-pens IZ，重读keeps+hap-，gray美式 【IPA head: /ɪf ˈðɪs ˈkips ˈɡoʊɪŋ ˈwʌt ˈhæpənz ɪz/】
- 提醒：if keeps going=预测未来，警示/展望都可用
- 练习：If this keeps going, ___.

**6. The lesson underneath all this is...**
- 中文：底层教训是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：The lesson underneath all this is less is more. I canceled three goals, kept one habit of writing daily, and that single lever moved my career most.
- 例句2 [故事]：Underneath my travel year, lesson was home is people. I saw beaches and cities, but what I miss now are late-night talks, not places I photographed.
- 替换：随意 Takeaway is / 正式 Underlying lesson is
- 发音：LES-son UN-der-NEATH ALL THIS IZ，重读les-+neath，lesson /ˈlɛsən/，canceled单l，traveled单l 【IPA head: /ðə ˈlɛsən ˌʌndərˈniθ ˈɔl ˈðɪs ɪz/】
- 提醒：underneath=深一层，总结升华
- 练习：Lesson underneath is ___.

**7. So if I zoom out one more time...**
- 中文：再拉远看一次...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：So if I zoom out one more time, this year was about repair. I rested, I re-organized my days around energy, and I rebuilt trust with myself slowly.
- 例句2 [播客]：If we zoom out once more, AI is just the latest wave. We feared calculators, then internet, and each time we adapted and created more human work.
- 替换：随意 Big picture again / 正式 At a higher level
- 发音：ZOOM OUT ONE MORE TIME，重读zoom+one+time，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈsoʊ ɪf aɪ ˈzum ˈaʊt ˈwʌn ˈmɔr ˈtaɪm/】
- 提醒：one more time=收尾升华，演讲结束前摇
- 练习：Zoom out one more time, ___.

**8. Bottom line for anyone listening is...**
- 中文：给听众的底线是...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Bottom line for anyone listening is start tiny. Pick one 10-minute habit, protect it for seven days, and let small wins prove you can trust yourself again.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Bottom line is own your mornings. Organize one hour before phone, do the hard thing first, and the rest of the day gets easier almost automatically.
- 替换：随意 Takeaway / 正式 Key message is
- 发音：BOT-tom LINE FOR A-ny-one LIS-ten-ing，重读bot+line，organize美式z 【IPA head: /ˈbɑtəm ˈlaɪn fər ˈɛniwʌn ˈlɪsənɪŋ ɪz/】
- 提醒：for anyone listening=喊话听众，播客/演讲收尾王
- 练习：Bottom line for anyone is ___.

### 本轮10个最值得背的keep talking 2.0
1. To give you a sense of scale... 2. Day in, day out, it's... 3. I can't even put into words how... 4. Back then... fast forward to now... 5. I used to think... now I think... 6. Take... for example... 7. Most people do X, but we do Y by... 8. When you break it down, two drivers... 9. Short term... long term... 10. Bottom line for anyone listening is...

万能keep talking链2.0：Back then... + Little did I know... + Fast forward to now... + Lesson underneath is... = 回忆分析永动机

### 7天复习计划（keep talking 2.0不断片）
Day1 展开：scale / day in out / top to bottom 各描述1个场景2-3句
Day2 表达：can't put words / weight lifted / fills cup 各表达1段情绪
Day3 回忆对比：back then-now / used to-now / relive 各讲1个故事
Day4 举例：take example / say you're / with numbers 各讲1个方法
Day5 总结：two drivers / short-long / bottom line 各总结1个话题
Day6 综合：展开1分+回忆1分+举例1分+总结1分=4分演讲
Day7 测试：Top10抽5个连说2-3句，卡用 Anyway pick up where left off救场

---

## 第七轮 (2026-10-13) - 场景+干啥版 Keep Talking长句

> 每条新增【场景 / 干啥】：在哪用，能帮你干成什么事。长句2-3句连说，全新40个
> 主题：求职面试 / 反馈复盘 / 提请求 / 拒绝说不 / 赞美鼓励

### 主题1：求职面试 Job Interview【面试/自我介绍】

**1. I thrive in... environments where...**
- 中文：我在...环境如鱼得水
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I thrive in fast, honest environments where people share early. In my last team we shipped weekly, talked openly, and I did my best work in years.
- 例句2 [面试]：I thrive in gray, messy starts where nothing is organized yet. I love turning chaos into a clear program the team can actually follow.
- 替换：随意 Do best in / 正式 Excel in / 更强 Truly thrive in
- 发音：THRIVE IN /θraɪv ɪn/重读thrive，thrive咬舌，gray美式，organize/program美式 【IPA head: /aɪ θraɪv ɪn ɛnˈvaɪrənmənts wɛr/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 面试开场/自我介绍；干啥 30秒立人设，让面试官记住你适合什么
- 练习：I thrive in ___ where ___.

**2. A time I failed and what I learned was...**
- 中文：一次失败+学到...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：A time I failed was missing launch by two weeks. I overpromised, under-analyzed risks, and learned to pressure-test timelines before committing to clients.
- 例句2 [面试]：I once canceled a demo last minute. I hadn't practiced, I froze on stage, and since then I rehearse out loud three times before anything live.
- 替换：随意 Failed once / 正式 A failure experience involved
- 发音：FAILED AND WHAT I LEARNED WAS，重读failed+learned，analyze美式z，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ə taɪm aɪ feɪld ənd wʌt aɪ lɜrnd wʌz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 行为面试必问题；干啥 把黑历史转成成长，显诚实可靠
- 练习：A time I failed was ___ + learned ___.

**3. I'm known for...**
- 中文：大家说我是...的人
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'm known for staying calm when things break. Last quarter our server went down at midnight, I organized the fix, and we were back by 2am with zero drama.
- 例句2 [面试]：I'm known for making complex ideas simple. I take gray data, I turn it into one clear story, and teams actually act on it the same day.
- 替换：随意 People say I / 正式 Recognized for
- 发音：KNOWN FOR /noʊn fɔr/重读known，k不发音，gray美式，organize美式z 【IPA head: /aɪm noʊn fɔr/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 自我推销/推荐信口吻；干啥 一句话标签，让人记住你
- 练习：I'm known for ___ + 例子.

**4. What draws me to this role is...**
- 中文：吸引我的是...
- 正式度：professional-formal
- 例句1：What draws me to this role is the users. You serve small teams who travel constantly, and I love building tools that respect their chaotic days.
- 例句2 [面试]：What draws me here is the center's mission. You teach real skills, you stay organized around outcomes, and that lines up with how I like to work.
- 替换：随意 Why I like this / 正式 Motivated by
- 发音：DRAWS ME TO THIS ROLE，重读draws+role，draws /drɔz/美式r，travel单l，center/organize美式 【IPA head: /wʌt drɔz mi tu ðɪs roʊl ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 Why us?必答；干啥 表忠心+做过功课，不是海投
- 练习：Draws me is ___ + 原因.

**5. I bring... to the table, especially...**
- 中文：我能带来...尤其...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I bring speed without sloppiness to the table, especially in launches. I cut meetings to 20 minutes, document decisions async, and teams ship twice as fast.
- 例句2 [谈判薪资]：I bring storytelling to the table. I can analyze numbers, then turn them into a pitch that moves customers in three slides flat.
- 替换：随意 Offer / 正式 Contribute
- 发音：BRING... TO THE TA-ble es-PE-cial-ly，重读bring+ta-+pe-，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /aɪ brɪŋ tu ðə ˈteɪbəl ɛˈspɛʃəli/】
- 提醒：bring to table=贡献价值
- 场景/干啥：场景 谈优势/谈薪；干啥 把能力翻译成对方收益
- 练习：I bring ___ to table, especially ___.

**6. In my last role, I turned... around by...**
- 中文：上份工作我扭转...靠...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：In my last role, I turned onboarding around by cutting it from ten steps to three. We rewrote gray help docs, added one Loom video, and churn dropped 18%.
- 例句2 [面试]：I turned morale around by starting tiny demos. Every Friday we showed one win, gave credit loudly, and within two months people stayed late because they wanted to.
- 替换：随意 Fixed / 正式 Transformed
- 发音：TURNED... a-ROUND BY，重读turned+round，gray美式 【IPA head: /ɪn maɪ læst roʊl aɪ tɜrnd əˈraʊnd baɪ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 讲业绩STAR；干啥 用数字证明你不是吹牛
- 练习：Turned ___ around by ___.

**7. I'm at my best when...**
- 中文：我在...时状态最好
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I'm at my best when I have quiet mornings. I write before Slack, I protect two hours, and the rest of the day feels easy even when it's packed.
- 例句2 [深度]：I'm at my best when I'm learning out loud. I share drafts early, I ask dumb questions fast, and I level up way quicker than polishing alone.
- 替换：随意 Best when / 正式 Peak performance when
- 发音：AT MY BEST WHEN，重读best 【IPA head: /aɪm æt maɪ bɛst wɛn/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 谈工作风格/需求；干啥 提前立边界，防入职被榨干
- 练习：At best when ___.

**8. What I need to do my best work is...**
- 中文：要做好需要...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：What I need to do my best work is clear goals and fast feedback. Tell me what success looks like, check in weekly, and I'll own the rest without chasing.
- 例句2 [远程面试]：I need async by default and overlap four hours. I traveled across zones before, so I document everything and never block teammates waiting on me.
- 替换：随意 Need / 正式 Require to perform optimally
- 发音：NEED TO DO MY BEST WORK IZ，重读need+best，traveled单l 【IPA head: /wʌt aɪ nid tu du maɪ bɛst wɜrk ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 谈条件/远程协作；干啥 把需求说成生产力，不是矫情
- 练习：Need to do best is ___.

### 主题2：反馈复盘 Feedback【会议/1对1/评审】

**1. What's landing well is... even better if...**
- 中文：好的地方...更好如果...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：What's landing well is your energy. You owned the room for ten minutes. Even better if you slow down on data and let one story breathe a bit longer.
- 例句2 [评审]：What's landing is the simple design. Users get it in seconds. Even better if we analyze drop-off and cut one more gray confusing step this week.
- 替换：随意 Good... better / 正式 Strength... opportunity
- 发音：LAND-ing WELL... E-ven BET-ter IF，重读land+bet-，analyze美式z，gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌts ˈlændɪŋ wɛl ɪz ˈivən ˈbɛtər ɪf/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 给同事/下属反馈；干啥 三明治夸法不伤人还能改
- 练习：Landing well ___, even better ___.

**2. Can I offer a quick observation?**
- 中文：能给个观察吗？
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Can I offer a quick observation? You canceled questions twice to save time, but folks looked confused after. Maybe pause for 30 seconds next time?
- 例句2 [1对1]：Quick observation — you say yes fast, then drown later. What if you protect Fridays and push new asks to Monday mornings instead?
- 替换：随意 Quick note / 正式 May I share feedback
- 发音：OF-fer a QUICK ob-ser-VA-tion，重读of-+quick+va-，observation /ˌɑbzərˈveɪʃən/，canceled单l 【IPA head: /kæn aɪ ˈɔfər ə kwɪk ˌɑbzərˈveɪʃən/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 开口提意见前摇；干啥 要许可再说，对方不防御
- 练习：Can I offer observation about ___?

**3. One tweak that would level this up is...**
- 中文：一个小改能升级...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：One tweak that would level this up is the opening. You started with agenda, but if you start with a customer story, the whole room will lean in instantly.
- 例句2 [播客]：One tweak for audio — level up mic distance. You're a bit far, it sounds gray and hollow. Come closer two inches and you'll sound twice as warm.
- 替换：随意 One fix / 正式 One enhancement
- 发音：TWEAK THAT WOULD LE-vel THIS UP，重读tweak+le-+up，tweak /twiːk/，gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌn twik ðæt wʊd ˈlɛvəl ðɪs ʌp ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 改稿/改演讲/改设计；干啥 只说一个，小步好接受
- 练习：One tweak is ___.

**4. I noticed... impact was...**
- 中文：我注意到...影响是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I noticed you interrupted Maya twice. Impact was she shut down after. I know you didn't mean it, but pausing three seconds would change everything.
- 例句2 [会议]：I noticed we skipped retro again. Impact is same bugs return monthly because we never close the loop and organize lessons into a checklist.
- 替换：随意 Saw... / 正式 Observed...
- 发音：NO-ticed... IM-pact WAS，重读no-+im-，organize美式z 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈnoʊtɪst ˈɪmpækt wʌz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 纠正行为不攻击人；干啥 讲事实+影响，对事不对人
- 练习：Noticed ___, impact was ___.

**5. Keep / Stop / Start...**
- 中文：保持/停止/开始...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Keep the daily demos — they build momentum fast. Stop scheduling 60-minute syncs nobody reads. Start writing async updates we can skim in five minutes.
- 例句2 [复盘]：Keep your humor, it fills the room. Stop apologizing for every pause. Start owning silences and people will trust your pace even more.
- 替换：随意 Continue... / 正式 Retain... discontinue
- 发音：KEEP / STOP / START三词都重读干脆 【IPA head: /kip stɑp stɑrt/】
- 场景/干啥：场景  retro/季度复盘；干啥 3词框架10秒说明白
- 练习：Keep ___, stop ___, start ___.

**6. The story your slides tell is...**
- 中文：幻灯片讲的故事是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The story your slides tell is we're busy but lost. Ten charts, no hero, no ask. If we cut to three and end with one clear timeline, execs will say yes faster.
- 例句2 [路演]：Your deck tells we're cheap, not great. Gray tables hide wins. Let's put one customer quote huge, add numbers, and let small wins compound visually.
- 替换：随意 Decks says / 正式 Narrative conveys
- 发音：STO-ry YOUR SLIDES TELL IZ，重读sto+slides+tell，gray美式 【IPA head: /ðə ˈstɔri jɔr slaɪdz tɛl ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 改PPT/路演；干啥 从观众视角点醒，不只改字
- 练习：Story slides tell is ___.

**7. From a user's perspective...**
- 中文：从用户看...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：From a user's perspective, signup feels heavy. They hit three gray screens, get asked for phone too early, and half quit before seeing any value at all.
- 例句2 [评审]：From a listener view, intro drags. You spend two minutes on sponsors before the story starts, and I almost canceled to another show twice.
- 替换：随意 As user / 正式 Through user lens
- 发音：FROM A U-ser's per-SPEC-tive，重读u-+spec-，canceled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /frʌm ə ˈjuzərz pərˈspɛktɪv/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 产品/内容评审；干啥 搬出用户挡箭，客观不吵架
- 练习：From user perspective, ___.

**8. If I were in your shoes, I'd...**
- 中文：换我会...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：If I were in your shoes, I'd ship the ugly version Friday. Get five real calls, analyze what sticks, then polish only what users actually touch daily.
- 例句2 [导师]：If I were you with burnout, I'd cancel one project this week. Protect sleep like a meeting, walk mornings, and watch focus come back in ten days.
- 替换：随意 I'd... / 正式 In your position I would
- 发音：IF I WERE IN YOUR SHOES I'D，重读were+shoes，were /wɜr/美式r，analyze美式z，canceled单l 【IPA head: /ɪf aɪ wɜr ɪn jɔr ʃuz aɪd/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 给建议不爹味；干啥 换位+具体动作，对方愿听
- 练习：If in shoes, I'd ___.

### 主题3：提请求 Request【协作/求助】

**1. Would you be open to...? It would help me...**
- 中文：愿意...吗？对我...有帮助
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Would you be open to a 15-minute chat Thursday? It would help me organize the launch story before I present to execs who love tight timelines.
- 例句2 [求助]：Open to reviewing my intro? It would help me calm nerves — I keep rushing the first minute and losing the gray-area nuance I want to keep.
- 替换：随意 Could you...? / 正式 Would you consider
- 发音：WOULD YOU BE O-pen TO，重读would+o-，open /ˈoʊpən/，organize美式 【IPA head: /wʊd ju bi ˈoʊpən tu ɪt wʊd hɛlp mi/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 约时间/求评审；干啥 给理由+低门槛，对方好答应
- 练习：Open to ___? Help me ___.

**2. Could you do me a favor and...? Reason is...**
- 中文：帮个忙...因为...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Could you do me a favor and ping Maya? Reason is I'm stuck in back-to-back calls and she needs the doc before 3pm to stay on track.
- 例句2 [日常]：Favor — could you grab my charger? I traveled without it, laptop dies in an hour, and I have a demo I can't afford to cancel.
- 替换：随意 Favor? / 正式 Request assistance with
- 发音：COULD YOU DO ME A FA-vor AND，重读could+fa-，favor美式拼写不是favour，traveled单l，canceled单l 【IPA head: /kʊd ju du mi ə ˈfeɪvər ənd ˈrizən ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 急事求人；干啥 先说事再说因为，不道德绑架
- 练习：Favor ___? Reason ___.

**3. I hate to ask on short notice, but...**
- 中文：不好意思临时说，但...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I hate to ask on short notice, but can you cover standup? My kid's sick, I have zero bandwidth, and I already canceled my morning once this week.
- 例句2 [远程]：Hate to ask late, but can you sanity-check numbers? Client moved meeting to tomorrow and I just found two gray cells I'm not sure about.
- 替换：随意 Last minute ask / 正式 Apologize for late request
- 发音：HATE TO ASK ON SHORT NO-tice，重读hate+short+no-，notice /ˈnoʊtɪs/，canceled单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪ heɪt tu æsk ɑn ʃɔrt ˈnoʊtɪs bʌt/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 临时救火；干啥 先共情道歉，对方更愿帮
- 练习：Hate ask short notice, but ___.

**4. Would it be possible to...? Timeline is...**
- 中文：可能...吗？时间是...
- 正式度：professional-formal
- 例句1：Would it be possible to move deadline to Monday? Timeline is we lost Friday to outage, analyzed backlog Saturday, and need one clean day to ship right.
- 例句2 [客户]：Possible to get invoice early? Timeline is our program renews Thursday and finance needs it organized before they approve anything new.
- 替换：随意 Can we...? / 正式 Request extension
- 发音：WOULD IT BE POS-si-ble TO，重读would+pos-，possible /ˈpɑsəbəl/，analyze美式z，program/organize美式 【IPA head: /wʊd ɪt bi ˈpɑsəbəl tu ˈtaɪmlaɪn ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 对客户/上级正式求；干啥 给时间线显靠谱不是拖
- 练习：Possible to ___? Timeline ___.

**5. I need your eyes on... before...**
- 中文：需要你看下...在...前
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I need your eyes on the email before noon. It's going to 5,000 users, I rewrote the gray subject three times, and I don't want a typo to burn trust.
- 例句2 [协作]：Need eyes on deck before we send. Slides look good, but data on page four feels off and I want you to pressure-test it fast.
- 替换：随意 Check / 正式 Review
- 发音：NEED YOUR EYES ON... be-FORE，重读need+eyes+fore，gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪ nid jɔr aɪz ɑn bɪˈfɔr/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 求review防翻车；干啥 点名+deadline，对方秒懂优先级
- 练习：Need eyes on ___ before ___.

**6. Could we borrow 10 minutes to...?**
- 中文：借10分钟...行吗
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Could we borrow 10 minutes to align on owners? We keep going in circles on chat, and one quick call would save us three days of ping-pong.
- 例句2 [社交]：Borrow 10 mins to get your take on my talk? I added a story about traveling solo, but I'm not sure if the ending lands or drags.
- 替换：随意 Quick 10? / 正式 Brief sync
- 发音：BOR-row 10 MI-nutes TO，重读bor-+mi-，borrow /ˈbɑroʊ/，traveled单l 【IPA head: /kʊd wi ˈbɑroʊ tɛn ˈmɪnəts tu/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 约微会议；干啥 10分钟低压力，高手都愿给
- 练习：Borrow 10 mins to ___.

**7. If you have bandwidth, could you...?**
- 中文：有空的话能...吗
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：If you have bandwidth, could you intro me to Sam? We're exploring Austin centers for events and his name came up twice this week as the connector.
- 例句2 [远程]：If you have bandwidth today, could you async-review my Loom? It's two minutes, I show the new flow, and I need a gut check before Friday.
- 替换：随意 If free / 正式 Should capacity allow
- 发音：BAND-width COULD YOU，重读band+could，bandwidth /ˈbændwɪdθ/，center美式 【IPA head: /ɪf ju hæv ˈbændwɪdθ kʊd ju/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 不给压力求助；干啥 给台阶，对方没空也不尴尬
- 练习：If bandwidth, could you ___?

**8. Quick ask — could you intro me to...?**
- 中文：小请求介绍...认识？
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Quick ask — could you intro me to your designer? I love how she organizes portfolios, and I have a 20-minute question about our gray landing page.
- 例句2 [社交]：Quick ask — intro to your podcast friend? I have a story about burnout and rebuilding that lines up with their show on slow living.
- 替换：随意 Connect me / 正式 Facilitate introduction
- 发音：QUICK ASK... IN-tro ME TO，重读quick+in-，intro /ˈɪntroʊ/，organize美式z，gray美式 【IPA head: /kwɪk æsk kʊd ju ˈɪntroʊ mi tu/】
- 场景/干啥：场景  networkingcold outreach；干啥 30秒说清为啥值得介绍
- 练习：Quick ask intro to ___?

### 主题4：拒绝说不 Say No【边界/谈判】

**1. I wish I could, but I have to protect...**
- 中文：真想帮，但得保护...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I wish I could, but I have to protect mornings. I said yes twice last week, ended up drained, and my deep work never happened at all.
- 例句2 [日常]：Wish I could join Austin trip, but must protect budget. I traveled three times already and need to save for the quiet month ahead.
- 替换：随意 Can't / 正式 Unable to commit due to
- 发音：WISH I COULD BUT... pro-TECT，重读wish+could+tect，traveled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ wɪʃ aɪ kʊd bʌt aɪ hæv tu prəˈtɛkt/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 温柔拒绝朋友/同事；干啥 给珍视的东西，对方理解不记仇
- 练习：Wish could, protect ___.

**2. That doesn't work for me because... how about...?**
- 中文：不行因为...要不...?
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：That doesn't work for me because I'm on calls till 4. How about Friday 10am? I'll have analyzed feedback by then and we can decide fast.
- 例句2 [谈判]：$5k doesn't work because our costs are $7k bare. How about $8k with async support? You get speed, we keep quality without burning out.
- 替换：随意 Can't do... how about / 正式 Not feasible... alternative
- 发音：DOES-n't WORK FOR ME be-CAUSE HOW a-BOUT，重读doesn't+cause+how，analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ðæt ˈdʌzənt wɜrk fɔr mi bɪˈkɔz haʊ əˈbaʊt/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 拒绝时间/价格；干啥 because+新方案，不只说no
- 练习：Doesn't work because ___, how about ___?

**3. I'm going to have to pass this time since...**
- 中文：这次得跳过因为...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'm going to have to pass this time since my plate's full. We just canceled a vendor, I'm hiring two roles, and I can't give your event the energy it deserves.
- 例句2 [社交]：Have to pass on drinks Friday since I'm wiped. I worked late all week, need sleep, and I know I'd be bad company anyway right now.
- 替换：随意 Skip / 正式 Decline due to
- 发音：PASS THIS TIME SINCE，重读pass+since，canceled单l 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈɡoʊɪŋ tu hæv tu pæs ðɪs taɪm sɪns/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 拒邀约/合作；干啥 pass显礼貌+since给理由不断交
- 练习：Pass this time since ___.

**4. I can't commit fully, but I can...**
- 中文：全接不了，但能...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I can't commit fully, but I can review the doc tonight. I won't join daily standups, but I'll drop async comments and unblock you by 9am.
- 例句2 [帮忙]：Can't lead trip, but can organize hotels. I traveled that route last year, I know gray cheap spots that are actually clean and safe.
- 替换：随意 Can't all... but / 正式 Partial commitment
- 发音：CAN'T com-MIT FUL-ly BUT I CAN，重读can't+mit+can，organize美式z，traveled单l gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪ kænt kəˈmɪt ˈfʊli bʌt aɪ kæn/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 半拒半帮；干啥 保边界又给情面，职场高情商
- 练习：Can't fully ___, but can ___.

**5. Let's deprioritize... so we can nail...**
- 中文：降级...才能做好...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's deprioritize newsletter so we can nail onboarding. Users quit week one because gray setup confuses them, and no email fixes a broken first hour.
- 例句2 [会议]：Let's deprioritize perfect slides so we can nail story. Execs forget fonts, but they remember one customer who traveled far to thank us in person.
- 替换：随意 Drop... focus / 正式 Reprioritize
- 发音：de-pri-O-ri-tize SO WE CAN NAIL，重读o-+nail，deprioritize /diːpraɪˈɔːrɪtaɪz/，gray美式，traveled单l 【IPA head: /lɛts dipraɪˈɔrɪtaɪz soʊ wi kæn neɪl/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 砍需求/聚焦；干啥 用nail显决心，老板爱听
- 练习：Deprioritize ___ so nail ___.

**6. I need to push this to next week because...**
- 中文：得推到下周因为...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I need to push this to next week because data isn't clean. We haven't finished analyzing interviews, and shipping now would just mislead the team badly.
- 例句2 [日常]：Need to push coffee to next week — I'm swamped. I double-booked three days, canceled gym twice, and need one calm weekend to reset first.
- 替换：随意 Move / 正式 Defer
- 发音：PUSH THIS TO NEXT WEEK be-CAUSE，重读push+next+cause，analyze美式z，canceled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ nid tu pʊʃ ðɪs tu nɛkst wik bɪˈkɔz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 延期不背锅；干啥 给业务理由，不是懒
- 练习：Push ___ to next week because ___.

**7. My plate is full with... so...**
- 中文：手头满了...所以...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：My plate is full with hiring and launch. I'm interviewing six people, rewriting the program page, so I can't own the offsite till October ends.
- 例句2 [1对1]：Plate's full with sick kid plus deadlines. I'm sleeping five hours, running on fumes, so I need to drop one gray side project this month.
- 替换：随意 Swamped with / 正式 At capacity with
- 发音：PLATE IZ FULL WITH，重读plate+full，program美式，gray美式 【IPA head: /maɪ pleɪt ɪz fʊl wɪθ soʊ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 向上拒绝/求减负；干啥 列活证明忙，不是借口
- 练习：Plate full with ___, so ___.

**8. To be honest, that's out of scope for me right now since...**
- 中文：老实说超出范围因为...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：To be honest, that's out of scope for me right now since I'm centered on onboarding. I can intro you to Sam who loves gray-area ops and will do it better.
- 例句2 [ freelance ]：Honest, video editing is out of scope. I organize stories and scripts, but I don't cut footage — I know someone cheap and fast though.
- 替换：随意 Not my thing / 正式 Beyond remit
- 发音：TO BE HO-nest THAT'S OUT OF SCOPE... SINCE，重读hon+out+scope，center/organize美式，gray美式 【IPA head: /tu bi ˈɑnəst ðæts aʊt ʌv skoʊp fɔr mi raɪt naʊ sɪns/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 拒杂活防背锅；干啥 scope一词显专业+转介绍不断交
- 练习：Out of scope since ___.

### 主题5：赞美鼓励 Praise & Encourage【团队/日常/演讲】

**1. You crushed... specifically...**
- 中文：你炸场了，尤其...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：You crushed that demo, specifically the opening story. You paused, you looked up, and the whole gray room leaned in within 20 seconds.
- 例句2 [团队]：You crushed onboarding rewrite. You cut ten steps to three, added real screenshots, and support tickets dropped the very next week.
- 替换：随意 Killed it / 正式 Excelled at
- 发音：CRUSHED /krʌʃt/ THAT... spe-CI-fi-cal-ly，重读crushed+ci-，gray美式 【IPA head: /ju krʌʃt spəˈsɪfɪkli/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会后表扬/复盘；干啥 crushed+细节，夸得具体不假
- 练习：Crushed ___, specifically ___.

**2. I saw how you handled... that took...**
- 中文：看到你处理...真需要...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I saw how you handled the angry client. That took patience — you listened ten minutes, owned the delay, and turned a cancel risk into a renewal.
- 例句2 [日常]：Saw how you handled red-eye travel with kids. That took guts — delayed flight, crying toddler, and you stayed calm and funny the whole time.
- 替换：随意 Noticed how / 正式 Observed composure
- 发音：SAW HOW YOU HAND-led... THAT TOOK...，重读saw+took，traveled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ sɔ haʊ ju ˈhændəld ðæt tʊk/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 安慰难时刻；干啥 点名难+品质，对方被看见
- 练习：Saw handled ___, took ___.

**3. Credit where credit's due — you...**
- 中文：该夸就得夸，你...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Credit where credit's due — you saved launch. You stayed late organizing QA, caught two big bugs, and nobody even knew till your retro note.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Credit due to Maya — she traveled to three centers, listened to 50 users, and brought back the insight that changed our whole program.
- 替换：随意 Props to / 正式 Acknowledge contribution
- 发音：CRE-dit WHERE CRE-dit's DUE YOU，重读cre-+due，credit /ˈkrɛdɪt/，travel/organize/program美式 【IPA head: /ˈkrɛdɪt wɛr ˈkrɛdɪts du ju/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 公开表扬/颁奖；干啥 仪式感夸，团队士气拉满
- 练习：Credit due — you ___.

**4. That took guts when you...**
- 中文：那真勇，你居然...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：That took guts when you said no to the client. Everyone went quiet on Zoom, but you protected the team and we respected you way more after.
- 例句2 [朋友]：Took guts traveling solo for a month. You didn't know anyone, you got lost twice, and you came back braver and funnier than before.
- 替换：随意 Brave / 正式 Courageous
- 发音：TOOK GUTS WHEN YOU，重读took+guts，guts /ɡʌts/，traveling单l 【IPA head: /ðæt tʊk ɡʌts wɛn ju/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 鼓励冒险/裸辞/发言；干啥 guts=有种，夸勇气最到位
- 练习：Took guts when you ___.

**5. You made... look easy, but I know...**
- 中文：你看着轻松，其实我知道...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：You made hosting look easy, but I know you prepped for hours. You rewrote questions twice, practiced names, and still stayed loose when we went off script.
- 例句2 [日常]：You make parenting look easy, but I know nights are short. You work full days, handle bedtime solo, and still show up smiling every morning.
- 替换：随意 Seem easy / 正式 Effortless yet
- 发音：MADE... LOOK EA-sy BUT I KNOW，重读made+ea-+know，easy /ˈiːzi/ 【IPA head: /ju meɪd lʊk ˈizi bʌt aɪ noʊ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 夸高手/妈妈/主持；干啥 揭穿轻松=看见付出，最走心
- 练习：Made ___ look easy, but know ___.

**6. I appreciate how you... especially when...**
- 中文：感谢你...尤其当...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：I appreciate how you document everything, especially when we're rushed. Your gray one-pager saved us twice last month when new folks joined mid-chaos.
- 例句2 [1对1]：Appreciate how you stay kind under pressure, especially when clients push. You never snap, you reframe, and the room calms down in minutes.
- 替换：随意 Thanks for / 正式 Grateful for
- 发音：ap-PRE-ci-ate HOW YOU... es-PE-cial-ly WHEN，重读pre-+pe-，gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪ əˈpriʃieɪt haʊ ju ɛˈspɛʃəli wɛn/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 1对1/感谢信；干啥 appreciate+细节，正式也暖
- 练习：Appreciate how ___ especially ___.

**7. You're getting so much better at... I noticed...**
- 中文：你...进步超大，我注意到...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：You're getting so much better at pausing. I noticed in today's standup you waited three seconds, let Maya finish, and your answer was twice as clear.
- 例句2 [教练]：Getting better at saying no — I noticed you canceled one gray meeting without guilt and used the hour to finish the deck early for once.
- 替换：随意 Improved at / 正式 Progressed in
- 发音：GET-ting SO MUCH BET-ter AT... I NO-ticed，重读get+bet+no-，notice /ˈnoʊtɪs/，canceled单l gray美式 【IPA head: /jɔr ˈɡɛtɪŋ soʊ mʌtʃ ˈbɛtər æt aɪ ˈnoʊtɪst/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 带新人/带娃/教练；干啥 点进步+证据，对方愿继续
- 练习：Better at ___, noticed ___.

**8. Keep going — what you're doing is... because...**
- 中文：坚持，你做的...因为...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Keep going — what you're doing is working because users feel it. They reply to your notes, they stay longer, and small honest posts compound into trust.
- 例句2 [演讲收尾]：Keep going — what you're building matters because it's human. You center people, you protect craft, and that gray grind will turn into something bright soon.
- 替换：随意 Don't stop / 正式 Persist
- 发音：KEEP GO-ing WHAT YOU'RE DO-ing IZ... be-CAUSE，重读keep+cause，center美式 gray美式 【IPA head: /kip ˈɡoʊɪŋ wʌt jɔr ˈduɪŋ ɪz bɪˈkɔz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 低谷打气/演讲结尾；干啥 keep going+意义，鸡血不空
- 练习：Keep going ___ because ___.

### 本轮10个最值得背的场景句
1. I thrive in... where... 2. I'm known for... 3. What's landing well... even better if... 4. Would you be open to...? 5. I need your eyes on... before... 6. That doesn't work... how about...? 7. I can't commit fully, but I can... 8. You crushed... specifically... 9. Credit where credit's due — you... 10. Keep going — what you're doing is...

万能场景链：Would you be open to...? + I need your eyes on... + What's landing well... even better if... = 求助+评审不断片

### 7天复习计划（场景实战）
Day1 面试：thrive / known for / draws me 各讲1段2-3句录音
Day2 反馈：landing well / tweak / noticed-impact 各改1个真实稿
Day3 请求：open to / favor-reason / borrow10 各求人1次
Day4 拒绝：doesn't work-how about / pass since / can't fully-but 各拒1次不伤人
Day5 赞美：crushed-specifically / credit due / took guts 各夸1人
Day6 综合：面试2分+反馈1分+请求1分=4分模拟
Day7 测试：Top10抽5个连说2-3句+说出场景/干啥

---

## 第八轮 (2026-10-13) - 场景+干啥 Keep Talking长句

> 长句2-3句，全新40个，每条标场景/干啥
> 主题：会议控场 / 追问深挖 / 数据讲故事 / 救场尴尬 / 收尾锁定

### 主题1：会议控场 Meeting Control【会议/远程】

**1. Let's get everyone aligned first by...**
- 中文：先对齐... 
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's get everyone aligned first by stating the goal. We're here to pick launch date, not redesign features, so let's keep all comments tied to timeline today.
- 例句2 [远程]：Aligned first — we're deciding budget, not brainstorming dreams. I'll share doc, you drop numbers in chat, then we vote in ten minutes flat.
- 替换：随意 sync first / 正式 Establish alignment by
- 发音：a-LIGNED FIRST BY，重读ligned+first，align /əˈlaɪn/ 【IPA head: /lɛts ɡɛt ˈɛvriwʌn əˈlaɪnd fɜrst baɪ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会议开场3分钟；干啥 定议题防跑题，省30分钟
- 练习：Aligned first by ___.

**2. I'm going to timebox this to... so...**
- 中文：限时...以便...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'm going to timebox this to ten minutes so we don't boil the ocean. Share your gray-area worry in one line, then we move to vote and assign owners fast.
