- 例句2 [ facilitation ]：Timebox debate to five. After that we pressure-test top idea once, land the plane, and take the rest offline with Sam tomorrow.
- 替换：随意 10 mins max / 正式 Allocate
- 发音：TIME-box THIS TO，重读time，timebox /ˈtaɪmbɑks/，gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪm ˈɡoʊɪŋ tu ˈtaɪmbɑks ðɪs tu soʊ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 讨论发散超时；干啥 限时推进不伤和气
- 练习：Timebox ___ to ___ mins.

**3. Let's hear from folks who haven't spoken...**
- 中文：听听没发言的...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's hear from folks who haven't spoken. Maya, you worked with users traveling weekly — what's your take on cutting onboarding from ten to three steps?
- 例句2 [包容]：Folks quiet, I want you in. No wrong take here — even half-formed thought could save us from shipping something gray and confusing next week.
- 替换：随意 Quiet folks? / 正式 Invite remaining voices
- 发音：FOLKS WHO HA-ven't SPO-ken，重读folks+spo-，folks /foʊks/，traveling单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts hɪr frʌm foʊks hu ˈhævənt ˈspoʊkən/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 强势人霸麦；干啥 点名给空间，挖到金子+显领导力
- 练习：Hear from ___ who hasn't ___.

**4. Can we park opinions and look at facts?**
- 中文：放下观点看事实...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Can we park opinions and look at facts? We analyzed 200 tickets, churn clusters in week one, so fixing welcome video beats debating colors for an hour.
- 例句2 [争吵]：Park takes, check data. Last program lost 15% after price change, not after design tweak — numbers tell a different story than our gut today.
- 替换：随意 Facts first / 正式 Refer to evidence
- 发音：PARK o-PIN-ions AND LOOK AT FACTS，重读park+facts，analyze美式z，program美式 【IPA head: /kæn wi pɑrk əˈpɪnjənz ənd lʊk æt fækts/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 吵架上头；干啥 用数据灭火，冷静决策
- 练习：Park opinions, look at ___.

**5. Let's recap where we are before...**
- 中文：先总结到哪了再...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's recap where we are before we vote. We agreed users hate long forms, we killed phone-first signup, and now we're stuck between two gray button labels only.
- 例句2 [长会]：Quick recap — we aligned on goal, flagged two risks, and still need owners. Let me add color with one user quote, then we lock next steps in five.
- 替换：随意 Sum up / 正式 Summarize status
- 发音：re-CAP WHERE WE ARE be-FORE，重读cap+fore，gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts ˈrikæp wɛr wi ɑr bɪˈfɔr/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 开会1小时发蒙；干啥 30秒回血，全员同频
- 练习：Recap where we are: ___.

**6. I want to make space for disagreement by...**
- 中文：留出反对空间...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I want to make space for disagreement by asking for pushback first. If you think timeline is fantasy, say it now while we can still adjust without canceling anything.
- 例句2 [心理安全]：Space for no — what am I missing? I organized this plan solo last night, so poke holes hard now and save us a painful rewrite next week.
- 替换：随意 Disagree welcome / 正式 Encourage dissent
- 发音：MAKE SPACE FOR dis-a-GREE-ment，重读space+gree-，organize美式z，canceled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ wɑnt tu meɪk speɪs fɔr ˌdɪsəˈɡrimənt baɪ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 没人敢说真话；干啥 主动邀请反对，避大坑
- 练习：Space for disagreement: ___?

**7. We're 10 mins over, let's decide...**
- 中文：超时10分，定吧...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We're ten over, let's decide owners and date. I'll take homepage copy, you'll ping design by Friday, and we'll sanity-check numbers async instead of meeting again.
- 例句2 [超时]：Over by ten, land plane here. Keep homepage test, deprioritize blog refresh, and revisit gray newsletter after launch when we have real data to judge.
- 替换：随意 Wrap / 正式 Conclude with decisions
- 发音：TEN MINS O-ver LET'S de-CIDE，重读ten+side，gray美式 【IPA head: /wɪr tɛn mɪnz ˈoʊvər lɛts dɪˈsaɪd/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会议拖堂；干啥 强收尾有产出，不白开
- 练习：10 over, decide ___.

**8. Let's assign one owner per...**
- 中文：每事定一个负责人...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's assign one owner per task so nothing floats. Maya owns invites, I own doc, and by Thursday we each drop updates in chat without needing another call.
- 例句2 [执行]：One owner per risk. You own churn follow-up, Sam owns hiring pipeline, and we review both next Monday with numbers, not vague gray feelings.
- 替换：随意 Who owns...? / 正式 Designate accountability
- 发音：as-SIGN ONE OW-ner PER，重读sign+own，assign /əˈsaɪn/，gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts əˈsaɪn wʌn ˈoʊnər pɜr/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会议结束无落实；干啥 一事一人，下次不扯皮
- 练习：Owner per ___ is ___.

### 主题2：追问深挖 Follow-up【采访/1对1】

**1. Can you say more about...? Specifically...**
- 中文：多说点...具体...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Can you say more about burnout? Specifically that gray February when you canceled trips — what did mornings actually look like day to day?
- 例句2 [1对1]：Say more about feeling unseen. Was it reviews, pay, or just no one saying thanks? I want to get what would actually fill your cup again.
- 替换：随意 Tell more / 正式 Elaborate on
- 发音：SAY MORE a-BOUT... spe-CI-fi-cal-ly，重读say+ci-，gray美式，canceled单l 【IPA head: /kæn ju seɪ mɔr əˈbaʊt spəˈsɪfɪkli/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 对方说太笼统；干啥 逼出细节故事不断片
- 练习：Say more about ___? Specifically ___?

**2. What did that look like day to day when...?**
- 中文：那时日常具体啥样...?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What did that look like day to day when you rebuilt? Were you journaling, walking gray mornings alone, and saying no to everything fun for a while?
- 例句2 [采访]：What did remote look like daily back then? Did you have standups, async docs, or just endless Slack pings that burned the whole team out slowly?
- 替换：随意 Daily like? / 正式 Describe daily routine during
- 发音：WHAT DID THAT LOOK LIKE DAY TO DAY WHEN，重读what+look+day，gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌt dɪd ðæt lʊk laɪk deɪ tu deɪ wɛn/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 抽象变具体；干啥 要画面要流程，听众秒懂
- 练习：Day to day when ___?

**3. How did you decide between... and...?**
- 中文：咋在...和...选的?
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：How did you decide between staying and quitting? You loved the team in Austin, yet you traveled constantly and missed home every single Sunday night.
- 例句2 [决策访]：How decide between price vs speed? We cut features to ship Friday, kept quality on one core flow, and let gray nice-to-haves wait till real feedback came.
- 替换：随意 Choose how? / 正式 Decision criteria?
- 发音：de-CIDE be-TWEEN... AND...，重读cide+tween，travel单l，gray美式 【IPA head: /haʊ dɪd ju dɪˈsaɪd bɪˈtwin ənd/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 挖决策逻辑；干啥 学到权衡框架可复制
- 练习：Decide between ___ and ___?

**4. What surprised you most about...? Why?**
- 中文：最意外...为啥?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：What surprised you most about fame? Why did quiet grocery runs feel harder than stage lights and 500-person Q&As that should have scared you more?
- 例句2 [日常]：What surprised you traveling solo? I thought I'd be lonely, but gray hostel nights turned into deep talks I still think about two years later.
- 替换：随意 Unexpected? / 正式 Most surprising aspect?
- 发音：sur-PRISED YOU MOST a-BOUT... WHY，重读prised+why，surprise /sərˈpraɪz/，traveling单l gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌt sərˈpraɪzd ju moʊst əˈbaʊt waɪ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 挖反转金句；干啥 surprise=故事高光适合剪辑
- 练习：Surprised most ___? Why?

**5. If you zoom into one moment, which...?**
- 中文：放大一个瞬间...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：If you zoom into one moment, which night changed you? Was it the canceled flight, the cold airport floor, and the stranger who shared his charger and story?
- 例句2 [演讲]：Zoom into one morning that proves momentum. You woke at 6, wrote 200 gray words nobody read, and kept going for 90 days till it finally clicked.
- 替换：随意 One moment? / 正式 Singular moment?
- 发音：ZOOM IN-to ONE MO-ment WHICH，重读zoom+one+which，canceled单l gray美式 【IPA head: /ɪf ju zum ˈɪntu wʌn ˈmoʊmənt wɪtʃ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 太空泛拉回；干啥 一个瞬间=电影画面永动机
- 练习：Zoom into one moment ___.

**6. What would you do differently if you ran it back?**
- 中文：重来会咋做?
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What would you do differently if you ran it back? I'd organize feedback earlier, kill gray vanity metrics fast, and protect mornings instead of living in Slack all day.
- 例句2 [复盘]：Ran it back on launch? I'd ship uglier sooner. We polished for a month, missed window, and learned users wanted simple help, not perfect design at all.
- 替换：随意 Redo? / 正式 Retrospectively change?
- 发音：DIF-fer-ent-ly IF YOU RAN IT BACK，重读dif+ran，organize美式z gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌt wʊd ju du ˈdɪfərəntli ɪf ju ræn ɪt bæk/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 复盘/面试学到；干啥 要教训不要后悔，成长感
- 练习：Ran back, differently ___.

**7. Who influenced how you thought about...? How?**
- 中文：谁影响你看...咋影响?
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Who influenced how you thought about rest? My mentor canceled hustle talks, modeled slow mornings, and showed me calm can still ship big results twice a year.
- 例句2 [深度]：Who shaped money views? Dad traveled for work, missed birthdays, yet sent letters weekly — so I chase presence over paychecks even now at 30.
- 替换：随意 Who shaped...? / 正式 Influential figure?
- 发音：IN-flu-enced HOW YOU THOUGHT a-BOUT... HOW，重读in-+thought，influence /ˈɪnfluəns/，canceled/traveled单l 【IPA head: /hu ˈɪnfluənst haʊ ju θɔt əˈbaʊt haʊ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 挖人+故事；干啥 人物带出价值观更动人
- 练习：Who influenced ___? How?

**8. What's still unresolved for you about...?**
- 中文：还有啥没想通关于...?
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：What's still unresolved for you about leaving? You love new city, yet gray Sundays feel lonely, and part of you still wonders if you quit too soon last spring.
- 例句2 [教练]：Unresolved about co-founder split? Money is settled, but trust still feels gray and tender. You replay last fight weekly and wish you'd said one honest line sooner.
- 替换：随意 Open loop? / 正式 Outstanding?
- 发音：STILL un-re-SOLVED FOR YOU a-BOUT，重读still+solved，unresolved /ˌʌnrɪˈzɑlvd/，gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌts stɪl ˌʌnrɪˈzɑlvd fɔr ju əˈbaʊt/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 深度收尾真话；干啥 unresolved=留白，播客听众追更
- 练习：Unresolved about ___?

### 主题3：数据讲故事 Data Story【会议/演讲】

**1. The number that stopped me was... because...**
- 中文：震住我的数字...因为...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The number that stopped me was 70% quit in week one. Because onboarding has ten gray steps, users never feel a win before they get tired and just leave quietly.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Number stopped me: 4 hours sleep. Because I analyzed my year and realized I canceled workouts, snapped at friends, and called it hustle while slowly burning out.
- 替换：随意 Crazy stat / 正式 Notable metric
- 发音：NUM-ber THAT STOPPED ME WAS... be-CAUSE，重读num+stopped+cause，analyze美式z gray美式 canceled单l 【IPA head: /ðə ˈnʌmbər ðæt stɑpt mi wʌz bɪˈkɔz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 开场抓人；干啥 数字+人话，3秒立威
- 练习：Number stopped me ___ because ___.

**2. Compared to last quarter, we...**
- 中文：对比上季度...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Compared to last quarter, we cut meetings 40% and shipped twice. We organized docs async, protected maker mornings, and support replies got faster without hiring anyone new.
- 例句2 [汇报]：Compared to Q2, churn fell from 12 to 7%. We fixed gray welcome email, added one human Loom, and travelers finally finished setup instead of ghosting after day two.
- 替换：随意 Vs last / 正式 Relative to prior period
- 发音：com-PARED TO LAST QUAR-ter WE，重读pared+quar-，compare /kəmˈpɛr/，organize美式z travelers单l gray美式 【IPA head: /kəmˈpɛrd tu læst ˈkwɔrtər wi/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 汇报业绩；干啥 对比显功劳，老板秒懂
- 练习：Compared last ___, we ___.

**3. What the chart hides is...**
- 中文：图表没说的是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：What the chart hides is pain behind growth. Signups doubled, yet tickets tripled because gray help center confuses newcomers who then ping us at midnight daily.
- 例句2 [分析]：Chart hides gray burnout. Output looks flat and fine, but two stars work weekends, skip lunches, and will quit by spring if we don't hire support soon.
- 替换：随意 Behind numbers / 正式 Unseen in data
- 发音：WHAT THE CHART HIDES IZ，重读chart+hides，chart /tʃɑrt/美式r gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌt ðə tʃɑrt haɪdz ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 纠偏唯数字论；干啥 讲背后人性，显得深刻
- 练习：Chart hides ___.

**4. Let me translate that percent into real people...**
- 中文：把百分比翻译成人...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let me translate 30% into real people. That's 300 travelers stuck on gray payment screen last month, each emailing us twice while thinking we don't care at all.
- 例句2 [演讲]：5% sounds tiny till real people. That's 50 moms who canceled night shifts to join our program, then finished it while kids slept beside them nightly.
- 替换：随意 In people terms / 正式 Humanize metric
- 发音：TRANS-late THAT per-CENT IN-to REAL PEO-ple，重读trans+cent+real，translate /ˈtrænzleɪt/ travelers/program/canceled美式 【IPA head: /lɛt mi ˈtrænzleɪt ðæt pərˈsɛnt ˈɪntu riəl ˈpipəl/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 让数字有血；干啥 百分比变故事，打动签字人
- 练习：Translate ___% into ___ people.

**5. The outlier here is... and story is...**
- 中文：异常值...故事是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：The outlier here is Tuesday spike. Story is Maya's Loom went viral in one Slack group, drove 500 trials in gray dawn hours while we were all still asleep soundly.
- 例句2 [复盘]：Outlier is one gray ad that crushed. It was ugly, honest, phone-shot in Austin traffic — and it beat our polished studio video by 3x clicks in two days.
- 替换：随意 Odd one / 正式 Anomaly
- 发音：OUT-li-er HERE IZ... STO-ry IZ，重读out+sto-，outlier /ˈaʊtlaɪər/ gray美式 【IPA head: /ði ˈaʊtlaɪər hɪr ɪz ənd ˈstɔri ɪz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 解释异常；干啥 outlier变洞察，下次复制
- 练习：Outlier ___ story ___.

**6. We ran this twice and both times...**
- 中文：跑两次都...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：We ran this twice and both times short version won. We tested long vs short onboarding, analyzed completion, and gray long form lost by 25 points each round.
- 例句2 [实验]：Ran test twice in Austin and remote. Both times async updates beat meetings — folks felt centered, shipped faster, and actually liked Mondays again for once.
- 替换：随意 Twice tested / 正式 Replicated
- 发音：RAN THIS TWICE AND BOTH TIMES，重读ran+twice+both，analyze美式z center美式 gray美式 【IPA head: /wi ræn ðɪs twaɪs ənd boʊθ taɪmz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 证明不是运气；干啥 twice=严谨，可信翻倍
- 练习：Ran twice both ___.

**7. Correlation isn't proof, but...**
- 中文：相关非因果，但...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Correlation isn't proof, but sleep tracks output tightly. When I sleep seven hours, I write twice as much, snap half as often, and actually enjoy gray Mondays again.
- 例句2 [谨慎]：Correlation not proof, but program folks who journal stay longer. They organize goals weekly, share wins in chat, and churn 20% less than lurkers overall.
- 替换：随意 Not causal but / 正式 Associated though not causal
- 发音：cor-re-LA-tion IS-n't PROOF BUT，重读la+proof，correlation /ˌkɔrəˈleɪʃən/ program/organize美式 gray美式 【IPA head: /ˌkɔrəˈleɪʃən ˈɪzənt pruf bʌt/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 严谨不背锅；干啥 又专业又敢说，科学家范
- 练习：Correlation not proof, but ___.

**8. If the trend holds, by... we'll...**
- 中文：趋势继续，到...会...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：If the trend holds, by December we'll double without hiring. We add 10% monthly now, we keep canceling low-value meetings, and compounding does the rest quietly.
- 例句2 [预测]：Trend holds, by spring we'll burn out two leads. They work weekends, cover gray gaps, and haven't taken real vacation since last summer's quick trip to Texas.
- 替换：随意 At rate / 正式 Projecting forward
- 发音：IF THE TREND HOLDS BY... WE'LL，重读trend+holds，trend /trɛnd/ gray美式 canceled单l 【IPA head: /ɪf ðə trɛnd hoʊldz baɪ wil/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 要资源/预警；干啥 趋势变 deadline，老板行动
- 练习：Trend holds, by ___ we'll ___.

### 主题4：救场尴尬 Save Awkward【会议/演讲/日常】

**1. Sorry I'm late — here's caught up version...**
- 中文：迟到抱歉，快速同步...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Sorry I'm late — here's caught up version I need. Were you still on timeline? I read doc in cab, I saw gray risk flag, and I can decide in two minutes flat.
- 例句2 [远程]：Late, sorry — quick catch me up? Last Slack I saw was Maya's mock, then I traveled through tunnel and lost signal for ten gray minutes straight.
- 替换：随意 Late catch up? / 正式 Apologize for tardiness
- 发音：SOR-ry I'M LATE HERE'S CAUGHT UP，重读sor+late+caught，caught /kɔt/ gray美式 traveled单l 【IPA head: /ˈsɔri aɪm leɪt hɪrz kɔt ʌp ˈvɜrʒən/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 迟到5分钟；干啥 道歉+要总结，不耽误大家
- 练习：Late — caught up version ___?

**2. Sorry, you cut out — last thing I heard was...**
- 中文：卡了，最后听到...
- 正式度：neutral
- 例句1：Sorry, you cut out — last thing I heard was gray pricing test. You froze mid-sentence, then came back pixelated, so can you repeat the numbers after 20%?
- 例句2 [Zoom]：Cut out, last heard churn. You said 12% then robotic voice, so I missed whether we fixed onboarding or still analyzing drop in week one.
- 替换：随意 Froze missed? / 正式 Audio disruption
- 发音：CUT OUT LAST THING I HEARD WAS，重读cut+last+heard，gray美式 analyze美式z 【IPA head: /ˈsɔri ju kʌt aʊt læst θɪŋ aɪ hɜrd wʌz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 Zoom卡；干啥 报最后听到，精准续上不重讲
- 练习：Cut out, last heard ___.

**3. My bad, I dropped the ball on... here's fix...**
- 中文：我的锅掉了...补救...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：My bad, I dropped the ball on invites. I forgot to loop in Sam, meeting felt gray and thin, so here's fix — I'll resend tonight plus async recap by 9am.
- 例句2 [道歉]：Dropped ball on notes — sorry. I said I'd organize doc, then traveled and forgot. Fix is live now, color-coded, and I'll ping owners individually today.
- 替换：随意 Missed / 正式 Oversight, corrective action
- 发音：MY BAD I DROPPED THE BALL ON... HERE'S FIX，重读bad+dropped+fix，organize美式z traveled单l gray美式 【IPA head: /maɪ bæd aɪ drɑpt ðə bɔl ɑn hɪrz fɪks/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 搞砸小事；干啥认+补，信任反升
- 练习：Dropped ball ___, fix ___.

**4. Let's laugh at tech for sec, while it loads...**
- 中文：笑笑技术等加载...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Let's laugh at tech for sec, while it loads. My screen froze on my weird face, audio sounds like robot in tunnel, and we're all pretending this is totally normal now.
- 例句2 [ Buffer ]：Tech being gray today, huh? While deck loads, quick story — last time I presented from Austin cafe, blender drowned my big closing line completely.
- 替换：随意 Tech issues / 正式 Brief technical delay
- 发音：LAUGH AT TECH FOR SEC WHILE IT LOADS，重读laugh+tech+loads，laugh /læf/ gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts læf æt tɛk fɔr sɛk waɪl ɪt loʊdz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 共享卡死；干啥 幽默填空，尴尬变亲切
- 练习：Laugh tech while loads ___.

**5. I totally blanked on... let me...**
- 中文：大脑空白...让我...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：I totally blanked on her name — let me check chat fast. We met in Austin last spring, talked gray merch for an hour, and I still mess up Maya vs Mia daily.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Blanked on slide three, give me sec. I know story is about canceled launch, gray week, comeback — oh right, numbers were 10k to 3k then back to 20k.
- 替换：随意 Forgot / 正式 Momentarily lapsed
- 发音：TO-tal-ly BLANKED ON... LET ME，重读to-+blanked，blank /blæŋk/ gray美式 canceled单l 【IPA head: /aɪ ˈtoʊtəli blæŋkt ɑn lɛt mi/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 忘词忘名；干啥 诚实+查，不硬编
- 练习：Blanked on ___, let me ___.

**6. Sorry to interrupt, but we're short on time so...**
- 中文：打断下时间紧所以...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Sorry to interrupt, but we're short on time so can we land? Love gray details, but we need owners and dates in next three minutes or we'll lose the room completely.
- 例句2 [控场]：Interrupt fast — short time. Let's take details offline, keep one decision here, and I'll organize async thread tonight so nobody feels cut off coldly.
- 替换：随意 Cut in / 正式 Interject due to time
- 发音：SOR-ry TO in-ter-RUPT BUT WE'RE SHORT ON TIME SO，重读sor+rupt+short，organize美式z gray美式 【IPA head: /ˈsɔri tu ˌɪntəˈrʌpt bʌt wɪr ʃɔrt ɑn taɪm soʊ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 啰嗦超时；干啥 礼貌打断+给路，不树敌
- 练习：Interrupt, short time so ___.

**7. I misspoke earlier — what I meant was...**
- 中文：刚说错，意思是...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I misspoke earlier — what I meant was churn fell 7 points, not 70%. Gray slide had typo, I read too fast, and correct story is steady progress, not miracle overnight.
- 例句2 [纠正]：Misspoke on date — meant March, not May. Program starts March 10 in our center, apps close Feb 20, and I mixed gray calendar colors while rushing this morning.
- 替换：随意 Correction / 正式 Clarification
- 发音：mis-SPOKE EAR-li-er WHAT I MEANT WAS，重读spoke+meant，misspeak /mɪsˈspiːk/ program/center/gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪ mɪsˈspoʊk ˈɜrliər wʌt aɪ mɛnt wʌz/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 口误数据；干啥秒纠正保可信，不装没事
- 练习：Misspoke, meant ___.

**8. Let's pretend that pause was dramatic effect and...**
- 中文：假装停顿是效果...
- 正式度：casual-neutral
- 例句1：Let's pretend that pause was dramatic effect and keep going. I lost train talking about gray launch, froze five seconds, and now I'm back with the ending that actually matters most.
- 例句2 [演讲救]：Pretend freeze was suspense. You were waiting, I was panicking inside, and now punchline — we canceled that $50k plan and doubled with $5k instead.
- 替换：随意 Ignore pause / 正式 Resume after pause
- 发音：pre-TEND THAT PAUSE WAS dra-MA-tic ef-FECT AND，重读tend+ma-+fect，gray美式 canceled单l 【IPA head: /lɛts prɪˈtɛnd ðæt pɔz wʌz drəˈmætɪk ɪˈfɛkt ənd/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 冷场5秒；干啥 自嘲救场，观众笑+记得你
- 练习：Pretend pause effect and ___.

### 主题5：收尾锁定 Close & Lock【会议/演讲/播客】

**1. So to pull it together...**
- 中文：总结下...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：So to pull it together, we keep morning writing, we cancel two gray recurring calls, and we protect Fridays for deep work that actually moves the needle this quarter.
- 例句2 [演讲]：Pull together — you don't need perfect. You need one habit, one friend to ping weekly, and small wins that compound while gray doubts fade slowly.
- 替换：随意 Sum up / 正式 Synthesize
- 发音：PULL IT to-GE-ther，重读pull+ge-，pull it连读 pull‿it gray美式 canceled单l 【IPA head: /soʊ tu pʊl ɪt tuˈɡɛðər/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 讲了5分钟收束；干啥 3点收拢，听众带走东西
- 练习：Pull together ___.

**2. Action-wise, I'm taking... you're taking...**
- 中文：行动上我...你...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Action-wise, I'm taking homepage draft by Thursday. You're taking pricing calls with two travelers, and we'll async-review Friday so nobody needs another long meeting at all.
- 例句2 [分工]：I'm taking hiring pipeline, Sam's taking onboarding rewrite. We'll drop updates in chat, flag gray risks early, and review numbers together next Monday morning.
- 替换：随意 I'll... you... / 正式 Action items
- 发音：AC-tion-WISE I'M TA-king YOU'RE TA-king，重读ac-+ta-，action-wise /ˈækʃənwaɪz/ travelers/gray美式 【IPA head: /ˈækʃənwaɪz aɪm ˈteɪkɪŋ jɔr ˈteɪkɪŋ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会议最后2分钟；干啥 人+事+时，不落空
- 练习：Taking ___, you're ___.

**3. Let's lock this in writing so...**
- 中文：落字以便...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：Let's lock this in writing so we don't drift. I'll organize one doc with owners, dates, and gray open questions, then we all comment async by Friday noon sharp.
- 例句2 [承诺]：Lock in writing — we ship March 1, no new features after Feb 15. If gray idea pops up, we park it for v2 instead of delaying launch again like last fall.
- 替换：随意 Doc it / 正式 Document to confirm
- 发音：LOCK THIS IN WRI-ting SO，重读lock+wri-，lock this连读 lock‿this organize/gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts lɑk ðɪs ɪn ˈraɪtɪŋ soʊ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 口头定了怕变；干啥 落字=证据，下次不扯皮
- 练习：Lock writing ___.

**4. I'll send recap by... with...**
- 中文：...前发总结带...
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：I'll send recap by 5pm with decisions, owners, and link. You'll see gray debate boiled to three lines, plus Loom so folks who missed can catch up in four minutes.
- 例句2 [远程]：Recap by tonight with numbers. I analyzed calls, cut fluff, and you'll get one page plus next steps we can approve async without another draining call tomorrow.
- 替换：随意 Follow up with notes / 正式 Circulate summary
- 发音：SEND re-CAP BY... WITH，重读send+cap，recap /ˈriːkæp/ analyze美式z gray美式 【IPA head: /aɪl sɛnd ˈrikæp baɪ wɪθ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会后跟进；干啥 诺时间+内容，靠谱人设
- 练习：Recap by ___ with ___.

**5. One last check — anyone blocked on...?**
- 中文：最后确认卡在哪...?
- 正式度：professional
- 例句1：One last check — anyone blocked on access? Maya needs gray dashboard login, Sam waits on budget code, and unblocking now saves three pings later this week alone.
- 例句2 [收尾]：Last check blocked? If onboarding copy is stuck, say now. I'll stay ten extra, we'll unstick it together, and nobody drags gray worry home overnight.
- 替换：随意 Blocked? / 正式 Impediments?
- 发音：ONE LAST CHECK A-ny-one BLOCKED ON，重读one+check+blocked，blocked /blɑkt/ gray美式 【IPA head: /wʌn læst tʃɛk ˈɛniwʌn blɑkt ɑn/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 散会前30秒；干啥 捞阻塞，防会后烂尾
- 练习：Blocked on ___?

**6. Thanks for... especially...**
- 中文：谢谢...尤其...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Thanks for honest pushback, especially Maya. You flagged gray risk early, saved us a bad ship, and modeled how disagreement makes the whole team smarter fast.
- 例句2 [结尾]：Thanks for staying late — especially Sam who traveled today. You still showed up sharp, took notes, and volunteered for gray cleanup nobody wants to own ever.
- 替换：随意 Thank you / 正式 Appreciate
- 发音：THANKS FOR... es-PE-cial-ly，重读thanks+pe-，thanks /θæŋks/咬舌，traveled/gray美式 【IPA head: /θæŋks fɔr ɛˈspɛʃəli/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 会议/播客结尾；干啥 点名谢+细节，团队暖下次还来
- 练习：Thanks ___, especially ___.

**7. Let's celebrate one win before we go, which...**
- 中文：走前庆祝一个赢...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：Let's celebrate one win before we go, which is shipping despite outage. We stayed calm, organized fix in gray midnight hours, and users never felt a thing at all.
- 例句2 [团队]：Celebrate win — Maya's interview series. She booked five guests, edited all night twice, and downloads doubled while we slept through gray rainy weekend together.
- 替换：随意 Shoutout / 正式 Acknowledge achievement
- 发音：CE-le-brate ONE WIN be-FORE WE GO WHICH，重读ce-+win，celebrate /ˈsɛləbreɪt/ organize/gray美式 【IPA head: /lɛts ˈsɛləbreɪt wʌn wɪn bɪˈfɔr wi ɡoʊ wɪtʃ/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 低气压散会；干啥 以赢结尾，士气回血
- 练习：Celebrate win ___.

**8. See you... and async me if...**
- 中文：...见，有事异步找...
- 正式度：neutral-professional
- 例句1：See you Friday, and async me if stuck. Drop note in chat, I'll reply between deep blocks, and we'll keep gray side threads from becoming another meeting spiral.
- 例句2 [远程收]：See you next sprint, ping if blocked. I travel Thursday, I'll be slow live, but async docs I'll review nightly and unblock by 9am sharp daily.
- 替换：随意 Ping me / 正式 Contact asynchronously
- 发音：SEE YOU... AND a-SYNC ME IF，重读see+sync，async /eɪˈsɪŋk/ travel/gray美式 【IPA head: /si ju ənd eɪˈsɪŋk mi ɪf/】
- 场景/干啥：场景 远程散会；干啥 约异步通道，不失联不打扰
- 练习：Async me if ___.

### 本轮10个最值得背的场景句
1. Let's get aligned first by... 2. Timebox this to... 3. Recap where we are... 4. Say more? Specifically... 5. Zoom into one moment... 6. Number stopped me... because... 7. Translate percent into people... 8. You cut out — last heard... 9. My bad dropped ball — fix... 10. Action-wise I'm... you're...

万能链：Aligned first + Timebox + Recap + Action I'm/you're = 会议永不跑偏

### 7天复习计划（场景实战）
Day1 控场：aligned / timebox / recap 各开1个5分模拟会
Day2 追问：say more / day to day / zoom moment 各深挖朋友1次
Day3 数据：stopped number / translate people / outlier 各讲1个数字故事
Day4 救场：late / cut out / blanked 各演1次救场不卡
Day5 收尾：pull together / action / recap 各收1个会
Day6 综合：控场2分+数据1分+收尾1分=4分演讲
Day7 测试：Top10抽5个连说2-3句+说出场景/干啥

## 第九轮 (2026-10-13) - 段落版 7-10句一段 可直接背说

> 形式：每主题1个段落，每段7-10句，美式，可直接说2分钟。每段标场景/干啥+发音+练习

### R9-P1：Morning routine that actually works【日常/播客分享】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 日常闲聊/播客开场/习惯分享；干啥 2分钟讲清routine，显得自律不装
**段落8句：**
I used to wake up and check my phone in bed for 30 minutes, and my whole day felt rushed before it even started. Now I keep my phone in the kitchen, so I have to get up to turn off the alarm in the gray morning light. First I drink water and stretch for five minutes while coffee brews, which sounds tiny but it wakes my brain up fast. Then I write for 20 minutes with no phone — just messy thoughts, no editing, no judging the grammar at all. After that I look at my top three for the day, and I pick one lever that would actually move the needle if I nailed it. I canceled two recurring gray meetings last month, so my mornings finally have space to breathe instead of jumping from ping to ping. If I protect those two hours, the rest of the day feels easy, even when Slack blows up in the afternoon with urgent red dots everywhere.
- 中文：以前床上刷手机30分，一天没开始就赶；现在手机放厨房，被迫起床，喝水拉伸咖啡，写20分不评判，选3件事1个杠杆，砍2个会，保2小时，下午再炸也不慌。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 My mornings go like / 正式 My routine consists of
- 发音：phone in bed连读，gray /ɡreɪ/，canceled单l，organize如用z，needle重读，Slack blows up连读 Key IPA: routine /ruːˈtin/; gray /ɡreɪ/; canceled /ˈkænsəld/; needle /ˈnidəl/; blows up /bloʊz ʌp/
- 提醒：used to / now对比+数字+取消什么=真实可信，别只说早起很棒
- 练习：跟读整段2遍，第2遍遮住读，录音卡几次？目标0卡

### R9-P2：Burnout and bouncing back【深度对话/采访】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 深度交流/采访讲低谷；干啥 讲崩了咋爬起，让人共情信任你
**段落8句：**
Looking back, burnout didn't hit me all at once — it crept in while I was still smiling on Zoom every day. I was working late, I canceled trips twice, and I told myself rest was lazy because everyone else seemed fine pushing harder. My heart sank when I realized I couldn't focus for even ten minutes, and even small emails felt like a ton of bricks on my chest. So I did the smallest thing first: I protected sleep like a meeting, I walked gray mornings with no podcast, and I said no to one extra project that week. I started journaling three lines a night — what drained me, what filled my cup, and one tiny win I could compound tomorrow. Slowly I got back on track, not by hustling harder but by doing less better and letting my energy rebuild one calm week at a time. If nothing else, remember this: you don't need a perfect comeback, you just need to stay the course for seven honest days in a row.
- 中文：微笑Zoom中慢慢燃尽，加班取消旅行说休息懒，心沉无法专注小邮件如砖，保睡眠散步说不，三行日记耗电充电小赢，少而好回正轨，不需完美只需7天坚持。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 Burned out bad / 正式 Experienced burnout
- 发音：crept /krɛpt/，canceled单l，gray美式，fills cup重读，compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/ Key IPA: crept in /krɛpt ɪn/; canceled /ˈkænsəld/; heart sank /hɑrt sæŋk/; fill my cup /fɪl maɪ kʌp/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/
- 提醒：creep in / heart sank / weight类比喻+3个具体动作=不卖惨有方法
- 练习：讲自己 burnout 版8句，保留 creep + sank + 3动作结构

### R9-P3：Remote meeting that doesn't suck【会议/远程协作】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 远程开会/带团队；干啥 90秒讲清高效开会法，同事愿意跟
**段落8句：**
To give you some context, our team is spread across three time zones, so we only overlap four hours live each day for real talk. We handle the rest async — I drop a two-minute Loom, folks reply in chat, and nobody has to jump on a call just to stay in the loop. When we do meet, we timebox to 20 minutes, we recap where we are in 30 seconds, and we assign one owner per task before anyone jumps off. If someone's on mute or freezes, we laugh at tech for a sec, we repeat the last thing we heard, and we keep going without making it awkward for anyone. We killed status round-robins because they drained energy, and instead we weigh pros and cons of one decision that actually moves the business forward. Bottom line for anyone listening is this: fewer live hours, clearer docs, and one shared program everyone can check without chasing pings all day long.
- 中文：三时区别叠4小时，其余异步Loom+chat，开会20分30秒回顾一事一人，卡了笑笑报最后听到，砍状态轮询只议一决策，少 live 清文档一共享 program。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 How we meet / 正式 Meeting protocol
- 发音：async /eɪˈsɪŋk/，timebox /ˈtaɪmbɑks/，program/organize美式，gray如用美式 Key IPA: overlap /ˌoʊvərˈlæp/; async /eɪˈsɪŋk/; timebox /ˈtaɪmbɑks/; owner /ˈoʊnər/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/
- 提醒：overlap / async / timebox / owner=远程黑话全，显得你真带过队
- 练习：用同样结构讲你的开会法8句：重叠+异步+限时+救场+砍啥+底线

### R9-P4：My first podcast disaster【播客/演讲故事】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 播客自嘲/演讲破冰；干啥 讲翻车拉近距离，观众笑完记你
**段落9句：**
I still remember the first time I hit record — my heart was pounding so loud I thought the mic would pick it up. I forgot my intro in ten seconds, I said schedule as /ˈʃedjuːl/ instead of American /ˈskedʒuːl/, and my guest just laughed and corrected me live on the show. Then my Wi-Fi cut out mid-story, I froze on a weird face, and when I came back I had no idea where I left off at all. Where was I? Oh right — I was asking about burnout, and I blanked so hard I asked the same question twice in three minutes. After we wrapped, I wanted to cancel the whole program because it felt gray and messy from top to bottom. But that episode got the most replies ever, because people said it felt real, not polished like every other show out there. I learned this the hard way: own your mess early, keep going anyway, and listeners will trust you way more than perfect audio ever could.
- 中文：首次录音心跳 mic 收，忘词 schedule 英音被纠，Wi-Fi 卡怪脸断片，where was I 同问两次，想砍 program 灰乱，却回复最多真实，学到认 messy 坚持信任超完美音质。
- 正式度：casual-neutral | 替换：随意 Epic fail / 正式 Initial setback
- 发音：pounding /ˈpaʊndɪŋ/，schedule美式必会，canceled单l，program/gray/center美式 Key IPA: pounding /ˈpaʊndɪŋ/; schedule /ˈskɛdʒul/; blanked /blæŋkt/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/; gray /ɡreɪ/
- 提醒：pounding + cut out + blanked + where was I=灾难四件套+反转，喜剧结构
- 练习：讲自己翻车9句：心跳+口误+技术+断片+想放弃+反转+教训

### R9-P5：Learning English as an adult【个人成长/日常】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 分享成长/激励他人；干啥 讲大人学英语可复制，听众想跟你学
**段落8句：**
Growing up, I thought English was exams, so I memorized words I never used and forgot them a week after every test. Back then I was scared to open my mouth, because I waited to be perfect before speaking a single gray sentence out loud. What changed was baby steps — ten minutes shadowing a podcast daily, one phrase I actually used at work, and small wins that compounded without me noticing. I stopped boiling the ocean with grammar books, I picked one show I loved, and I repeated lines until my mouth remembered them faster than my brain did. I still miss words, I still mix up heuristics, but I keep talking anyway and let feedback fix me in real time. To put it in perspective, 15 minutes a day is one canceled short video, and a year later you travel, you joke, and you hold 30-minute calls without freezing up at all.
- 中文：小时候为考试背忘，不敢开口等完美，baby steps 跟读10分1句工作用小赢复利，不啃语法一剧重复到嘴快过大脑，仍错但 keep talking 反馈修，15分=1短视频1年旅行玩笑30分 call 不卡。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 How I learned / 正式 Language acquisition journey
- 发音：shadowing /ˈʃædoʊɪŋ/，canceled/traveled单l，gray美式，compound重读 Key IPA: shadowing /ˈʃædoʊɪŋ/; canceled /ˈkænsəld/; traveled /ˈtrævəld/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/; perspective /pərˈspɛktɪv/
- 提醒：growing up + back then-now + baby steps + perspective=成长万能链
- 练习：讲你学英语8句：过去怕+转折小步+砍啥+现在错但说+数字 перспект

### R9 Top10背诵句 + 7天计划
Top10: 1 phone in kitchen gray mornings 2 top three lever moves needle 3 creep in smiling Zoom 4 weight bricks chest 5 fills cup compound 6 overlap async Loom loop 7 timebox recap owner 8 heart pounding schedule /ˈskedʒuːl/ 9 where was I blanked 10 baby steps shadow compound
计划：D1跟读P1P2录音 D2跟读P3P4 D3跟读P5+默写 D4 讲自己routine8句 D5 讲burnout8句 D6 讲翻车或英语8句 D7 抽2段连说不断片，卡用 Anyway pick up救场

## 第十轮 (2026-10-13) - 段落版 7-10句 商务 interview 演讲

> 每段7-10句，直接说。主题：pitch/面试/反馈/说不/演讲收尾

### R10-P1：Pitching an idea to execs【会议/谈判 pitch】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 向老板/客户 pitch；干啥 90秒说清要啥为啥值得批
**段落8句：**
To give you some context, churn is 12% because onboarding has ten gray steps and users never feel a win in week one. The data tells a different story than our gut — people don't hate price, they hate confusion that makes them ping support at midnight. Here's how it works in three steps: cut to three screens, add one human Loom, and translate progress into plain language travelers can skim in a minute. If we can ship this ugly version by Friday, would you be open to testing with 100 users before we polish anything else at all? Let's look at trade-offs — fast means small bugs, slow means missing holiday window while competitors organize their push right now. Bottom line is $2k and two weeks buys us a 20% lift based on last test we ran twice, and both times short beat long by 25 points easily.
- 中文：背景流失12%十步灰无赢，数据非 gut 恨混乱午夜 ping，三步砍三屏 Loom 人话旅行1分看，周五丑版测100？权衡快 bug 慢丢假日竞 organize，底线2k2周20%测两次短胜25分。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Pitch / 正式 Proposal
- 发音：churn /tʃɜrn/，data /ˈdeɪtə/，gray/canceled/traveled/organize/program美式 Key IPA: context /ˈkɑntɛkst/; churn /tʃɜrn/; data /ˈdeɪtə/; trade-offs /ˈtreɪdɔfs/; bottom line /ˈbɑtəm laɪn/
- 提醒：context+data+3 steps+if open+tradeoff+bottom line=pitch永动机
- 练习：用此结构 pitch 你的想法8句，数字必须真实

### R10-P2：Tell me about yourself【求职面试 60秒】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 Tell me about yourself；干啥 60秒标签+证据+为啥你，不背简历
**段落8句：**
I thrive in messy starts where nothing is organized yet, and I'm known for turning gray chaos into a clear program teams actually enjoy using. In my last role I turned onboarding around by cutting ten steps to three, adding real screenshots, and watching support tickets drop the next week without hiring anyone new. I bring speed plus calm to the table — I timebox debates, I document async, and I make space for pushback early so we don't ship bugs we regret later. What draws me here is your center serves small teams who travel constantly, and I love tools that respect chaotic days instead of adding more pings to them. I'm at my best with clear goals and fast feedback, so tell me what success looks like and I'll own the rest without needing to be chased every day. What I need is four hours overlap and async by default, because I learned the hard way that constant calls drain the exact energy I need to do deep work well.
- 中文：爱 messy 未 organize 名化灰 chaos 为 program，上份砍十步三截图票降无人，带来快+静 timebox 异步留反对，爱 center 旅行小队 chaotic 减 ping，最佳清目标快反馈告 success 自 own，需4叠异步，hard way 常会耗 deep 能量。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 About me / 正式 Professional background
- 发音：thrive /θraɪv/咬舌，known /noʊn/k不发，gray/organize/program/center/traveled美式 Key IPA: thrive /θraɪv/; known /noʊn/; turned around /tɜrnd əˈraʊnd/; center /ˈsɛntər/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/
- 提醒：thrive+known+turned+bring+draws+best+need=面试7件套不断片
- 练习：写自己版8句背到不卡，录音60-90秒

### R10-P3：Giving tough feedback kindly【反馈/1对1】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 给下属/同事提缺点；干啥 改了还感激你，不记仇
**段落8句：**
Can I offer a quick observation from yesterday's demo? What's landing well is your opening story — you paused, you looked up, and the gray room leaned in within 20 seconds flat. One tweak that would level this up is pace on data, because you rushed three charts and folks looked confused instead of convinced by your numbers. I noticed you cut Maya off twice when she tried to add color, and impact was she shut down after even though her point could have saved us. From a user's perspective, your slides tell we're busy but lost — ten ideas, no hero, no clear ask to approve by Friday at all. If I were in your shoes, I'd keep the story, I'd cut to three slides, and I'd end with what success looks like in one line everyone can repeat. Thanks for trusting me with this draft — I flag it because I know you can nail it, and I'll review the next version tonight if you drop it in chat.
- 中文：能观察？好开场故事停顿抬头灰房20秒倾，小改数据节奏 rush 三图懵非服，注意到断 Maya 两次 shut 丢救命点，用户看忙丢十 idea 无 hero 无周五 ask，换我留故事砍三 slide 一 success 线，谢信任 flag 因能 nail 今晚看 chat 版。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Feedback / 正式 Constructive input
- 发音：observation /ˌɑbzərˈveɪʃən/，gray美式，tweak /twiːk/ Key IPA: observation /ˌɑbzərˈveɪʃən/; landing well /ˈlændɪŋ wɛl/; tweak /twik/; shoes /ʃuz/; gray /ɡreɪ/
- 提醒：permission+landing+tweak+noticed+user+shoes+thanks=批评不伤人链
- 练习：拿真实稿按此8句改一版发出去

### R10-P4：Saying no to protect focus【拒绝/边界】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 拒杂活/延期；干啥 保住时间还不伤关系
**段落8句：**
I wish I could say yes, but my plate is full with hiring and launch, and I have to protect mornings to do any deep work at all. That timeline doesn't work for me because I'm on calls till 4, I haven't analyzed interviews yet, and shipping now would just mislead the team badly. I'm going to have to pass on leading the offsite since we just canceled a vendor, I'm rewriting the program page, and I can't give your event the energy it truly deserves. I can't commit fully, but I can review your doc tonight, drop async comments, and unblock you by 9am so you keep momentum without waiting on me. Let's deprioritize the newsletter so we can nail onboarding — users quit in gray week one, and no email fixes a broken first hour no matter how pretty it looks. If we can move your ask to next Monday, would you be open to a 15-minute working call where we lock owners and dates in writing once and for all?
- 中文：想 yes 盘满 hiring launch 保晨 deep，此线不行会到4未 analyze 访 ship 误导，pass offsite 刚砍 vendor 改 program 页无能给能量，全接不今晚看 doc 异步 9am 解锁保 momentum，降 newsletter 钉 onboarding 灰周1退 mail 不修破首时，移下周一15分锁 owners 日期落字？
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Can't / 正式 Decline
- 发音：plate full /pleɪt/，analyze-z，canceled/program/gray/organize美式 Key IPA: plate /pleɪt/; analyze /ˈænəlaɪz/; canceled /ˈkænsəld/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/; deprioritize /dipraɪˈɔrɪtaɪz/
- 提醒：wish+doesn't work+pass+can't fully+deprioritize+if open=拒绝六连不断片
- 练习：写拒绝信8句：wish+原因+pass+半帮+降级+新方案

### R10-P5：Speech closing that moves people【演讲收尾】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 演讲/播客结尾；干啥 30秒让人记住+行动，不鸡汤空
**段落9句：**
So if I zoom out one more time, this year wasn't about doing more — it was about repair, rest, and rebuilding trust with myself slowly day by day. We started with gray burnout, we traveled through doubt and late nights, and we learned that small wins compound even when progress feels invisible for weeks at a time. I learned this the hard way: you can't pour from an empty cup, so protecting sleep is strategy, not laziness anyone should apologize for ever again. Picture this next Monday — you wake without phone, you write ten gray lines nobody judges, and you pick one tiny habit you can keep for seven days straight without fail. Don't sleep on that habit because it looks small; I read ten pages daily and a year later I'd finished 20 books without ever feeling rushed or burned out again. If nothing else, remember this: own your story, center people over programs, and let honesty do the heavy lifting slides never could. So here's my ask — tell one person today what you're working on, let them hold you accountable, and keep going because what you're building truly matters.
- 中文：拉远今年非多是修休重信，灰燃尽旅行怀疑夜小赢复利隐形周，hard way 空杯倒不出睡战略非懒想像下周一无 phone 写十灰行无人判一习7天必守，别小看小习10页日年20书不 rush 燃尽，记住 own 故事 center 人超 program 诚抬 slide 不动，我 ask 今告一人所做让 hold 责 keep 因 building 重要。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Closing / 正式 Conclusion
- 发音：zoom out /zuːm/，compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/，center/program/gray/traveled/canceled美式，accountable /əˈkaʊntəbəl/ Key IPA: zoom out /zum aʊt/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/; center /ˈsɛntər/; accountable /əˈkaʊntəbəl/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/
- 提醒：zoom+travel+hard way+picture+sleep on+nothing else+ask=收尾七件套高潮
- 练习：背此段改成你的故事9句，结尾必须 ask+accountable+keep going

## 第十一轮 (2026-10-13) - 段落版 7-10句

> 每段7-10句直接说，美式，标场景/干啥+发音+练习，全新5段

### R11-P1：Handling criticism without shutting down【会议/1对1 被批】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 被老板/客户批；干啥 不防御还拿信息，显成熟
**段落8句：**
Thanks for flagging that — I want to make sure I get it right, so can you say more about which part felt gray and confusing in my deck? What I'm hearing is the opening is strong, but the data section rushes three charts without one clear takeaway anyone can repeat after. You're right, I cut Maya off yesterday to save time, and impact was she shut down — I'll own that and pause three full seconds next time before I jump in. From my side, I was trying to land the plane in 20 minutes because we were ten over, but I see now speed cost us clarity we actually needed most. If I were in your shoes, I'd be frustrated too, so here's what I'll do: I'll keep the story, I'll cut to three slides, and I'll send a revised async version tonight for a quick sanity check. I appreciate you telling me straight — it stings for a minute, but it saves us a painful rewrite next week and makes the whole program stronger for users.
- 中文：谢指出想搞对哪灰懵？听到开场强数据 rush 三图无 takeaway，对断 Maya own 停3秒，超时20分赶 landing 丢 clarity，换位也 frust，留故事砍三 slide 今晚异步 sanity，谢直说疼1分省下周 rewrite program 强用户。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Got it / 正式 Acknowledge feedback
- 发音：flagging /ˈflæɡɪŋ/，gray/organize/program/canceled美式，sanity /ˈsænəti/ Key IPA: flagging /ˈflæɡɪŋ/; hearing /ˈhɪrɪŋ/; own /oʊn/; sanity-check /ˈsænəti tʃɛk/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/
- 提醒：thanks+say more+hearing+own+shoes+will+appreciate=被批七步不崩
- 练习：拿上次被批按此8句复述一遍录音

### R11-P2：Networking follow-up that gets replies【社交/求助 跟进】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 会后加人/求内推；干啥 3句话让人回你不尴尬
**段落8句：**
Great meeting you in Austin yesterday — I loved your story about canceling the big launch to save the team from burnout that gray spring. For folks who don't know me, I'm building a tiny program that helps remote teams organize onboarding in three steps instead of ten confusing screens. You mentioned you travel constantly for user visits, so I thought you'd get why we obsess over async Looms that save four live hours every single week. Quick ask — would you be open to a 15-minute chat next week? It would help me pressure-test pricing before we lock anything in writing for December. I'll send a two-line recap with a link, so you can skim in a minute and ignore it guilt-free if bandwidth is tight right now. Either way, thanks for trusting me with your email — I'll only ping if it's actually worth your time, not to add gray noise to your inbox.
- 中文：Austin 见爱砍大 launch 救燃尽灰春故事，不认识我建小 program 帮远程 organize 三步非十灰屏，你旅行访用户懂 obsess 异步 Loom 省4 live，open 15分下周？帮 pressure 定价锁12月前，发2行 recap 链接1分看 bandwidth 紧 guilt 忽略，谢 trust mail 只 ping 值得非灰噪。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 Follow up / 正式 Outreach
- 发音：networking /ˈnɛtwɜrkɪŋ/，canceled/traveled/program/organize/center/gray美式，async /eɪˈsɪŋk/ Key IPA: canceling /ˈkænsəlɪŋ/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/; organize /ˈɔrɡənaɪz/; async /eɪˈsɪŋk/; open to /ˈoʊpən tu/
- 提醒：compliment+who+relevance+ask+recap+thanks=跟进六件套回复率高
- 练习：写跟进信8句发给1个人：赞+我+关联+求+recap+谢

### R11-P3：Explaining AI to non-tech parents【日常/讲解 AI】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 给爸妈/外行讲AI；干啥 2分钟说明白不吓人
**段落8句：**
Think of AI like a very fast intern who read the whole internet but still needs a boss to check its work every day. The idea is pretty simple: you type a messy question, it predicts likely words, and you get a draft in seconds instead of staring at a blank gray doc for an hour. Here's how it works in real life — I dump bullet thoughts, it organizes them into an email, then I fix names and numbers because it still hallucinates little details all the time. In other words, it's not magic, it's autocomplete on steroids that saves typing but doesn't replace judgment about what actually matters to people. Garbage in, garbage out still applies — if I feed it gray confused notes, it gives back polished confusion that sounds smart but misleads my whole team fast. At the end of the day it's just a tool, so I keep a human in the loop, I protect mornings for real thinking, and I let AI handle boring first drafts I used to dread every Sunday night.
- 中文：AI 如读全网需 boss 查 intern，idea 简单 type 乱问 predict 词秒稿非盯灰 doc1时，现实 dump bullets organize mail 修名数因幻觉小节，换言之非 magic steroids 自动补省打非替判断人重要，garbage 灰乱 polished 乱误 team，底线工具 human loop 保晨真思 AI 厌首稿周日夜。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 AI 101 / 正式 Introduction
- 发音：intern /ˈɪntɜrn/，predict /prɪˈdɪkt/，hallucinate /həˈluːsɪneɪt/，gray/organize美式 Key IPA: intern /ˈɪntɜrn/; organize /ˈɔrɡənaɪz/; hallucinate /həˈluːsɪneɪt/; garbage in /ˈɡɑrbɪdʒ ɪn/; tool /tul/
- 提醒：intern+simple+real life+other words+garbage+tool=外行秒懂链
- 练习：给爸妈讲AI 8句：比喻+简单+现实+换话+垃圾+工具

### R11-P4：Apologizing and rebuilding trust【道歉/修复】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 搞砸迟到忘事；干啥 道歉一次信任反升
**段落8句：**
I'll own that — my bad. I said I'd organize the launch doc by Friday, I traveled and forgot to loop in Sam, and you walked into a gray meeting with half the numbers missing. My heart sank when I saw your face on Zoom, because I knew I'd burned ten minutes of everyone's time before we even started talking. Here's the fix: the doc is live now, color-coded, with owners and dates locked in writing, plus an async Loom so folks who missed can catch up in four minutes flat. I can't undo last night, but I can review the next version twice, set a phone reminder an hour early, and ping you before you have to chase me ever again. The lesson underneath is I overcommitted while my plate was already full with hiring, so I'm going to deprioritize one gray side project to protect what actually moves the needle. Thanks for calling it out straight — that took guts, and I'll make sure your trust compounds instead of cracking a second time around here.
- 中文：own my bad 说 organize 周五 launch doc 旅行忘 loop Sam 灰会半数缺，心沉 Zoom 脸烧10分未开谈，fix live 色码 owners 日期落字异步 Loom 4分补，难 undo 下版2查 phone 早1时 ping 不 chase，课 under overcommit 盘满 hiring 降灰 side 保 needle，谢直说 guts 保 trust 复利非裂二次。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 Sorry / 正式 Apologize
- 发音：own /oʊn/，apologize /əˈpɑlədʒaɪz/，traveled/canceled/organize/program/gray美式 Key IPA: own /oʊn/; traveled /ˈtrævəld/; organize /ˈɔrɡənaɪz/; lesson underneath /ˈlɛsən ˌʌndərˈniθ/; guts /ɡʌts/
- 提醒：own+heart sank+fix+can't undo+lesson+thanks=道歉修复六步
- 练习：写道歉8句：认+心沉+补+不再+课+谢，发出去

### R11-P5：Future vision where I'm headed【演讲/面试 未来】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 面试未来/演讲展望；干啥 讲野心不空让人想跟你
**段落8句：**
So if I zoom out one more time, the next year isn't about more hours — it's about centering on fewer things that compound while gray noise fades quietly in the background. Back then I chased every shiny tool, fast forward to now I pick one program, I go deep for 90 days, and I let results speak louder than announcements ever could. I'm in a season of building slowly: mornings for writing, afternoons for users who travel and need simple help, evenings totally off to protect the energy that fuels everything else I do. Short term it's boring — same habit, same gym, same ten pages daily — long term it's freedom, because small honest reps turn into trust, referrals, and work I actually love waking up for. Picture this next December — I ship one tiny thing weekly, I analyze what stuck, I cancel what didn't, and I end the year lighter, kinder, and clearer than the gray January version of me. Bottom line for anyone listening is start tiny, stay the course when it feels invisible, and keep going because what you're building will outlast the doubt that tries to stop you tonight.
- 中文：拉远明年非多时 center 少复利灰噪静退，当年追 shiny 快进今一 program 深90天结果声超 announce，季慢建晨写午旅行用户简助夜 off 保能量燃料一切，短期 boring 同习馆10页长期自由小 honest trust 转介爱醒事，想像明12月周 ship 小 analyze 砍 cancel 年轻 kinder 清超灰1月我，底线小 stay course 隐形 keep building 超今夜 doubt。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 Future / 正式 Vision
- 发音：zoom /zuːm/，compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/，center/program/traveled/canceled/gray/organize美式 Key IPA: zoom out /zum aʊt/; center /ˈsɛntər/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/; season /ˈsizən/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/
- 提醒：zoom+back-now+season+short-long+picture+bottom=未来演讲链
- 练习：讲未来8句：拉远+对比+季+短长+画面+底线，录音90秒

## 第十二轮 (2026-10-13) - 段落版 7-10句

### R12-P1：Cold outreach that doesn't feel cold【社交/求助 破冰】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 陌生人私信/领英；干啥 3秒不被删还能回你
**段落8句：**
I know this is out of the blue, so I'll keep it to 30 seconds — I found your talk on burnout while traveling last month, and one gray line about gray Sundays stuck with me for weeks. For context, I'm organizing a tiny meetup in Austin for folks who canceled hustle culture but still want to ship meaningful work without killing their mornings. You work with travelers who live on Slack, so you'd get why we're testing async demos that save three live hours and still feel human and warm. I hate to ask on short notice, but would you be open to a 10-minute voice note swap next week? It would help me sanity-check one idea before I lock it in writing for December. I'll drop a one-minute Loom plus two lines, so you can ignore it guilt-free if your plate is full with launch and hiring right now. Thanks either way for the work you put out — it already helped me protect Fridays, and that's why I thought you'd be the perfect person to learn from here.
- 中文：冒昧30秒旅行听燃尽灰日线记周，背景 organize Austin 小会取消 hustle 仍 ship 有意义不杀晨，你做旅行 Slack 懂异步 demo 省3 live 人暖，歉临时 open 10分语音下周？帮 sanity 锁12月前，丢1分 Loom2行盘满 hiring launch guilt 忽略，谢作品已帮保周五故学你。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 Cold DM / 正式 Outreach

- 提醒：blue+context+relevance+ask+recap+thanks=陌生信不删链

### R12-P2：Closing a project with thanks【收尾/感恩】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 项目结束/离职告别；干啥 好聚好散下次还找你
**段落8句：**
So to pull it together, we shipped in eight weeks what we once thought needed six months, and we did it without burning out the core team behind the scenes. What's working is clear — small async updates beat long gray meetings, one owner per task beats five helpers, and honest demos beat polished decks nobody trusts anymore. I want to call out Maya especially — she traveled twice for users, rewrote gray help docs at midnight, and modeled calm when the server crashed the night before launch day arrived. We had nothing at the start but a whiteboard, yet we had everything because we had trust, and that trust compounded into speed nobody could have bought with money alone. If I ran it back, I'd organize feedback earlier, I'd kill vanity metrics faster, and I'd protect one meeting-free morning a week from day one for deep thinking. Bottom line for anyone listening is thank you — you showed up tired, you stayed kind under pressure, and you made this gray grind feel bright for a while here.
- 中文：拉总8周当6月事无燃核队，working 小异步胜长灰会一人胜五帮诚 demo 胜 polish deck，点 Maya 尤其旅行2用户午夜改灰 doc 服务器崩前夜 calm，白板无却一切因 trust 复利速钱难买，重来早 organize 反馈杀虚荣保1无会晨 deep，底线谢 tired 仍 kind 压力灰磨亮阵。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Wrap / 正式 Closure
- 发音：pull together /pʊl/，traveled/canceled/organize/program/gray/async美式，compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/ Key IPA: pull together /pʊl tuˈɡɛðər/; async /eɪˈsɪŋk/; owner /ˈoʊnər/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/; gray /ɡreɪ/
- 提醒：pull+working+call out+nothing yet+ran back+bottom=收尾不烂感人链
- 练习：写项目告别8句：总+好+点名+无却有+重来+谢，发群里

### R12-P3：Explaining a delay without excuses【道歉/延期】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 延期/翻车解释；干啥 承认+方案不找借口还被信
**段落8句：**
I misspoke last week when I said Friday — what I meant was we need till Tuesday, because data is still gray and shipping now would mislead 2,000 travelers waiting on us. To put it in perspective, we analyzed 150 interviews, found onboarding breaks at step two, and fixing that one screen matters more than hitting an arbitrary date I threw out too fast. My bad, I dropped the ball by not flagging the risk earlier — I saw churn climbing, I hoped it would fix itself, and I didn't want to be the person who slowed good momentum down. Here's the tradeoff nobody talks about: fast means bugs users feel at midnight, slow means missing buzz while competitors organize their launch right now in Austin. Action-wise, I'm taking the rewrite tonight, Sam's taking support macros, and I'll send a recap by 9am with owners, dates, and a Loom so you see gray progress turning clear. I appreciate you hearing me out — give us three clean days, and we'll ship something small, honest, and solid instead of something fast, gray, and broken again.
- 中文：上周误 Friday 意周二数据灰 ship 误导2000旅行， перспект analyze 150访断步2修一屏超 arbitrary 日速抛，my bad 掉球未 flag 风险见 churn 望自愈不愿慢 momentum， tradeoff 无人快 bug 午夜慢丢 buzz 竞 organize Austin launch，行动我今夜改 Sam 宏明9am recap owners 日期 Loom 灰转清，谢听给3净日 ship 小 honest solid 非快灰破再。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Delay note / 正式 Revised timeline
- 发音：misspoke /mɪsˈspoʊk/，perspective /pərˈspɛktɪv/，analyze/traveled/canceled/organize/gray/async美式 Key IPA: misspoke /mɪsˈspoʊk/; perspective /pərˈspɛktɪv/; analyze /ˈænəlaɪz/; trade-off /ˈtreɪdɔf/; travelers /ˈtrævələrz/
- 提醒：misspeak+perspective+own+tradeoff+action+appreciate=延期不死链
- 练习：写延期8句：纠正+数据+认+权衡+分工+谢，发老板

### R12-P4：Hype vs reality of AI tools【AI/会议 去魅】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 聊AI不吹不黑；干啥 显清醒，同事愿意听你
**段落8句：**
Everyone says AI will automate away our jobs by spring, but when you zoom out, what you see is slower, messier progress that still needs humans in the loop every day. The model still hallucinates — it invents links, it mixes up gray policy names, and last week it told a traveler our refund takes 90 days instead of nine. Think of it like a fast intern: it drafts in seconds what took me an hour in a blank doc, yet I still rewrite the ending because only I know what our users actually fear at midnight. We're drowning in demos but starving for wins — we tried five tools, canceled three, kept two that organize notes and summarize calls without adding more gray pings to Slack. What this boils down to is leverage, not replacement: one centered person plus good prompts does the work of three tired people copying and pasting all afternoon long. At the end of the day it's just a tool, so I protect mornings for judgment, I let AI do gray first drafts, and I spend afternoons talking to real travelers instead of admiring shiny demos together.
- 中文：人说春 automate 岗拉远慢乱需 human loop 日日，模幻觉编链混灰策告旅行退90非9，想快 intern 秒稿1时空白灰 doc 仍改尾唯知用户午夜惧，溺 demo 饿 win 试5砍3留2 organize 笔记总 call 不加灰 ping，boil leverage 非替一 center 人好 prompt 抵3 tired 复制粘午，底线工具保晨判断 AI 灰首稿午访真旅行非赏 shiny。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 AI reality check / 正式 Assessment
- 发音：automate /ˈɔtəmeɪt/，hallucinate /həˈluːsɪneɪt/，drowning /draʊnɪŋ/，organize/center/traveled/canceled/gray/program美式 Key IPA: automate away /ˈɔtəmeɪt əˈweɪ/; hallucinate /həˈluːsɪneɪt/; drowning /ˈdraʊnɪŋ/; boils down to /bɔɪlz daʊn tu/; centered /ˈsɛntərd/
- 提醒：zoom+hallucinate+intern+drowning+boil+tool=去魅六件套理性粉
- 练习：讲AI看法8句：吹+现实+比喻+溺+归结+工具，90秒

### R12-P5：Weekend reset that fixes burnout【生活/成长 回血】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 周末复活/朋友聊天；干啥 可复制回血不躺平废
**段落8句：**
If Friday drained you, here's my gray-weekend reset that actually works without canceling your whole life for two days straight. Friday night I do a brain dump — I write what’s open, I pick Monday’s top one, and I close the laptop so gray Slack threads can’t chase me into dreams all night. Saturday I go analog till noon: I walk without phone, I cook something slow, and I let my head stay gloriously bored instead of scrolling travelers’ highlight reels that make me feel behind. I’m in a season of protecting peace, so I say no once — one gray invite I skip guilt-free — and I fill my cup with one friend who listens fully without trying to fix me in five minutes. Sunday I do a tiny reset: I organize my desk for ten minutes, I analyze last week in three lines, and I lay out gym clothes so Monday starts before willpower wakes up at all. Small wins compound here — clean desk, calm inbox, one prepped meal — and by Sunday night that gray dread turns into quiet readiness for a week you actually want to live through.
- 中文：周五 draining 灰周末 reset 不 cancel 整2日，周五夜 dump 写 open 选周一顶1关机灰 Slack 不追梦，周六模拟午 phone 散步慢煮 gloriously 无聊不刷旅行高光落后感，季保 peace 说不1灰邀 guilt 免 fill 杯1友全听不5分修，周日小 reset organize 桌10分 analyze 周3行摆衣周一 will 前启，小赢复利净桌静箱备餐周日夜灰 dread 静备想活周。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 Reset / 正式 Recovery routine
- 发音：reset /riˈsɛt/，analog /ˈænəlɔɡ/，dump /dʌmp/，analyze/organize/traveled/canceled/gray美式，compound重读 Key IPA: reset /riˈsɛt/; analog /ˈænəlɔɡ/; organize /ˈɔrɡənaɪz/; analyze /ˈænəlaɪz/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/
- 提醒：dump+analog+season+reset+compound=回血链，具体到分钟才可抄
- 练习：写周末 reset 8句：五夜+六模+说不+日备，发朋友

### R13-P1：Asking for raise without sounding entitled【谈薪/1对1】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 谈加薪升职；干啥 有证据不卑不亢，老板好批
**段落8句：**
To give you some context, I took on onboarding rewrite plus hiring while our team stayed flat at six people through a gray spring of constant pings. Compared to last quarter, support tickets fell 22% after I cut ten steps to three, added one Loom, and organized async macros the whole center now reuses daily. The data tells a different story than titles — I'm doing senior scope on mid pay, and travelers feel it in faster replies that mention me by name twice a week. What I bring to the table is calm plus speed: I timebox debates, I sanity-check numbers before we ship, and I make space for pushback so we don't burn out fixing bugs later. If we can align on what success looks like for next level, would you be open to a $8k bump to $92k with a 90-day check on churn and onboarding completion? I know budget is the elephant in the room, so I'm happy to deprioritize conference travel this fall and lock the plan in writing by Friday for finance to approve cleanly.
- 中文：背景接改版+hiring 队6人灰春 ping 不断，对比上季票降22%砍十3 Loom 异步宏 center 日用，数据非 title 高级活中级钱旅行点名周2快回，带来静+快 timebox sanity 留反对不燃修 bug，对齐 success 下级 open 8k 到92k 90天查 churn 上手？知预算象，愿降秋旅行 confer 周五落字财批净。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Raise ask / 正式 Compensation review
- 发音：raise /reɪz/，elephant /ˈɛləfənt/，sanity /ˈsænəti/，churn /tʃɜrn/，canceled/traveled/organize/center/program/gray美式 Key IPA: context /ˈkɑntɛkst/; data /ˈdeɪtə/; churn /tʃɜrn/; elephant /ˈɛləfənt/; sanity-check /ˈsænəti tʃɛk/
- 提醒：context+compared+data+bring+if open+elephant+lock=谈薪不死的链
- 练习：按此8句写你的谈薪稿：背景+对比+数据+带来+要数+象+锁

### R13-P2：Homesickness abroad and feeling between worlds【情感/深度】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 海外孤独/朋友深聊；干啥 说出夹缝感，被理解不矫情
**段落8句：**
That takes me back to my first gray winter in Chicago — I traveled 7,000 miles for freedom, yet cried in a grocery aisle because nothing tasted like home at all. Part of me feels proud I built a life here — I organized a tiny Austin circle, I host potlucks, and I can handle landlord calls without freezing up anymore. Another part feels guilty when mom says the house is quieter now, and I count canceled trips while telling myself next spring will finally be different this time. I have mixed feelings about staying — I love centered mornings writing alone, yet gray Sundays ache for noisy family lunches I used to complain about back then. What I'm learning is home isn't a place I left, it's a practice I carry: I cook one dish weekly, I call Sundays, and I let small wins compound into belonging slowly. If nothing else, remember this if you move too — you don't stop missing, you just get better at holding both pride and ache in the same heart at once.
- 中文：回首芝加哥灰冬旅行7000里自由却杂货哭无家味，部分 pride 建 Austin 小圈 potluck 房东 call 不 freeze，部分 guilt 妈说静数 cancel 旅行说明春不同，mixed 留爱 center 晨写灰日痛吵闹家午曾嫌，学 home 非地乃行周一菜周日 call 小赢复利归属，记住搬亦然不止念 Better 同心 pride ache。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 Homesick / 正式 Nostalgia
- 发音：homesick /ˈhoʊmsɪk/，ache /eɪk/，guilt /ɡɪlt/，canceled/traveled/organize/center/gray美式，compound重读 Key IPA: takes me back /teɪks mi bæk/; mixed feelings /mɪkst ˈfilɪŋz/; guilt /ɡɪlt/; centered /ˈsɛntərd/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/
- 提醒：takes back+part/another+mixed+learning+nothing else=乡愁不矫情链
- 练习：讲乡愁8句：回首+ pride/guilt + mixed + practice + 记住

### R13-P3：Teaching what you just learned Feynman【讲解/分享】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 教同事/播客讲刚学会；干啥 刚学就讲清，越讲越懂
**段落8句：**
Here's how async standups work in three steps, because I just figured it out last week after drowning in 12 hours of gray meetings that went nowhere. The idea is pretty simple: you write what you did, what’s stuck, and what’s next in five lines, then everyone skims in chat instead of sitting on Zoom waiting to talk for 30 seconds. Think of it like leaving voicemails for a friend who travels — they listen at 2x when free, they reply once, and nobody interrupts dinner to say basically nothing urgent at all. In other words, we trade live nodding for written clarity, we analyze threads weekly, and we save Fridays for deep work that actually moves the needle for users. Most people do big round-robins, but we do one thread by 10am, we flag gray blockers with owners, and we jump on a call only if two replies can't unstick it fast. To take it one step further, I record a one-minute Loom on Mondays showing the board, so new folks see faces, hear tone, and feel part of the program without another draining call added.
- 中文：三步异步站因上周溺12时灰会无果，idea 简单写做卡下5行人 chat 瞄非 Zoom 等30秒说，想如留 voicemail 旅行友2x 闲听回1次不打断晚餐无 urgent，换言之 trade live 点头书面清 analyze 周线省周五 deep 动 needle 用户，多数大轮我们1线10am flag 灰堵主2回不解才 call，再步周一1分 Loom 板新见脸听调感 program 不加 drain 会。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 How-to / 正式 Tutorial
- 发音：Feynman /ˈfaɪnmən/可不读，async /eɪˈsɪŋk/，voicemail /ˈvɔɪsmeɪl/，analyze/organize/program/traveled/canceled/gray美式 Key IPA: async /eɪˈsɪŋk/; voicemail /ˈvɔɪsmeɪl/; analyze /ˈænəlaɪz/; needle /ˈnidəl/; program /ˈproʊɡræm/
- 提醒：3 steps+simple+like+other words+most vs+further=刚学就讲透链
- 练习：教刚学会8句：三步+简单+比喻+换话+多数vs+再步，90秒

### R13-P4：Difficult client who keeps changing scope【客户/谈判】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 客户变需求/催；干啥 守边界还留单，不吵不跪
**段落8句：**
Help me understand where you're coming from — you added three screens after we locked scope, and I want to hear what worries you most about launching without them this Friday. The tradeoff nobody talks about is every yes to gray extras is a no to quality on the core flow travelers will judge us by on day one in the store. What the chart hides is our velocity already doubled after we cut standups, yet context-switching to new asks will burn the two engineers who haven't taken a real weekend since that gray Austin sprint ended. I can't commit fully to Friday plus extras, but I can ship core Friday, drop extras into a v2 doc with owners, and demo v2 next Tuesday with real numbers from 50 users to guide us. If we can agree on what success looks like — 100 happy checkouts, not ten half-done features — would you be open to protecting that win first before we chase the rest together? Thanks for pushing — it shows you care, and I'd rather pressure-test now than apologize for a gray buggy launch we both regret next month.
- 中文：帮懂 where coming 加3屏锁后最忧周五无它们 launch？ tradeoff 无人 yes 灰 extra no 核流旅行 store 日1判，chart 藏速翻砍站会然切新 ask 燃2程无真周末灰 Austin sprint 终，全诺周五 extra 不然核周五 extra v2 主下周二 demo 真数50导，定 success 100 happy 结非十半特性 open 保赢先追余？谢推显 care 宁 pressure 今非歉灰 bug 双悔下月。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Scope creep talk / 正式 Change management
- 发音：scope /skoʊp/，creep /kriːp/，velocity /vəˈlɑsəti/，travelers/organize/gray/canceled美式 Key IPA: scope /skoʊp/; trade-off /ˈtreɪdɔf/; velocity /vəˈlɑsəti/; travelers /ˈtrævələrz/; pressure-test /ˈprɛʃər tɛst/
- 提醒：help+tradeoff+chart+can't fully+agree+thanks=变需求不崩链
- 练习：写守 scope 8句：懂+代价+藏+半帮+定成功+谢，发客户

### R13-P5：Sunday reflection that sets up week【复盘/成长】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 周日复盘/周报；干啥 20分清零周一不懵
**段落8句：**
When you break it down, last week had two drivers — deep mornings that worked and gray afternoons where I said yes too fast and paid with evenings I wanted for rest. What's working is writing before Slack — I felt centered till noon three days straight without needing extra coffee to fake focus at all. What's not is context ping-pong — I kept three gray chats open, traveled mentally between threads, and ended Friday with ten tabs plus a headache nobody can bill for. The pattern I'm seeing is I protect what I schedule, so next week I'm trying to be more intentional: I canceled two recurring calls, I timebox inbox to noon and four, and I lay out gym clothes to trick mornings into starting themselves. Short term it's awkward telling people no, long term it's freedom to do one thing well instead of five things gray and half-done by Sunday night again. So to pull it together, my one lever is mornings — if I win 7 to 9, small wins compound, and by Friday that gray dread turns into proof I can trust myself one more week straight.
- 中文：拆两驱 deep 晨 work 灰午 yes 快赔夜想休，working 写先 Slack center 午3日连无咖啡假 focus，不行 ping-pong 3灰 chat 旅行线程周五10 tab 头痛无人 bill，pattern 保 schedule 下周 intentional 砍2会 timebox 箱午4摆衣骗晨自启，短期尬说不长期自由1好非5灰半周日夜，拉总一杠杆晨赢79小赢复利周五灰 dread 证信己又周。
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 Weekly review / 正式 Retrospective
- 发音：retrospective /ˌrɛtroʊˈspɛktɪv/，intentional /ɪnˈtɛnʃənəl/，compound，canceled/traveled/organize/center/gray美式 Key IPA: drivers /ˈdraɪvərz/; centered /ˈsɛntərd/; pattern /ˈpætərn/; intentional /ɪnˈtɛnʃənəl/; compound /kəmˈpaʊnd/
- 提醒：drivers+working/not+pattern+intentional+short-long+pull=复盘永动机
- 练习：写周复盘8句：驱+好坏+律+ intentional +短长+拉总，20分

## 第十四轮 (2026-10-13) - 我认为整体缺的5块 段落版

> 为啥缺：前13轮缺幽默/反对/安慰/即兴/澄清 — 全是高情商不断片刚需，全新5段每段7-10句

### R14-P1：Humor & self-deprecation幽默自嘲【社交/演讲破冰】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 破冰/冷场/跨文化；干啥 2分钟变好笑可亲，美國人先笑后信
**段落8句：**
I'm basically a professional meeting attendee who occasionally does real work in between gray calendar blocks nobody remembers by Friday. My superpower is saying schedule right on the third try — first /ˈʃedjuːl/, then /ˈskedʒuːl/, then pure confidence while everyone pretends not to notice I traveled here just to mispronounce one word. Last week I muted myself for five minutes while passionately pitching, and I only realized when Maya typed dude, you're on mute in all caps with three exclamation points. I canceled my gym membership twice because going twice counts as a habit in my book, and my treadmill is now a very expensive clothing rack for gray hoodies. Don't worry, I'm not here to be perfect — I'm here to be the cautionary tale that makes the rest of you look incredibly organized by comparison today. If you laugh once, my job is done; if you learn one thing, that's just a bonus my therapist will hear about for weeks.
- 中文： basically 专业开会偶干活灰块周五忘，超能力 schedule 三次对英转美自信假装不见旅行就错一词，上周静音5分激情 pitch Maya 大写 dude mute 三叹，取消馆2次去2次算习跑步机晾灰帽，不求完美求反例衬你 organize 笑1次 job 完学1 bonus 治疗师听周。
- 正式度：casual-neutral | 替换：随意 Funny intro / 正式 Light icebreaker
- 发音：self-deprecating /ˌsɛlfdɛprəkeɪtɪŋ/，schedule美式/cautionary /ˈkɔʃənɛri/，canceled/traveled/organize/gray美式 Key IPA: schedule /ˈskɛdʒul/; traveled /ˈtrævəld/; canceled /ˈkænsəld/; organized /ˈɔrɡənaɪzd/; cautionary /ˈkɔʃənɛri/
- 提醒：self-roast+具体糗事+不贬他人=美式幽默安全线，别开弱势玩笑
- 练习：写自嘲8句：身份梗+口误+技术+懒+免责+收笑，60秒讲笑1次

### R14-P2：Disagree agreeably温柔反对【会议/谈判 分歧】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 不同意老板/客户；干啥 反对还被爱，方案被采纳
**段落8句：**
I’m aligned on the goal — we both want launch by Friday — I’m just not sold on adding three gray screens the night before we ship to 2,000 travelers waiting. Help me understand where you’re coming from, because you see upside I might be missing after staring at the same doc for six hours straight today. The data tells a slightly different story: we analyzed drop-off, long forms lose 25%, and every extra field adds support pings at midnight nobody wants to own. What if we pressure-test both ways — ship core Friday, park extras in v2, and let 50 real users vote with clicks instead of us debating opinions for another hour? I could be wrong here, so correct me if I’m off — maybe there’s one must-have I’m blind to because I organized the flow around speed over delight this time. Either way, I’d rather disagree now while it’s cheap than apologize for a gray buggy launch we both regret next month in front of execs.
- 中文：对齐目标周五 launch 不 buy 前夜加3灰屏2000旅行等，帮懂 where coming 你 upside 我盯6时 doc 盲，数据微异 analyze 掉25%每 field 午夜 ping 无人 own，何不 pressure 双测核周五 extra v2 50真点投票非吵1时？我错纠 off 或一 must 盲因 organize 速超 delight，宁今廉争非下月灰 bug 歉 exec 前悔。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Pushback nicely / 正式 Respectful dissent
- 发音：aligned /əˈlaɪnd/，sold /soʊld/，pressure-test /ˈprɛʃər tɛst/，analyze/traveled/organize/gray美式 Key IPA: aligned /əˈlaɪnd/; analyze /ˈænəlaɪz/; pressure-test /ˈprɛʃər tɛst/; organize /ˈɔrɡənaɪz/; travelers /ˈtrævələrz/
- 提醒：aligned+help+data+what if+correct me+either way=反对不树敌链
- 练习：写反对8句：对齐+懂+数据+what if+纠我+宁今，下次会用

### R14-P3：Comfort & hold space安慰陪伴【深度/朋友低谷】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 朋友哭/失败/失去；干啥 不灌鸡汤，陪住让人哭完好受
**段落8句：**
Thanks for trusting me with that — I can hear how heavy this gray month has been, carrying interviews, sick mom, and canceled sleep all at the same time without telling anyone. That must have been a lot to carry alone, and it makes complete sense you’d feel numb on calls and then cry in the car right after parking at home. I’m not going to rush to fix it, because some things just need to be sat with for a while before they turn into lessons we can actually use later. What I’m hearing is you did everything right and still lost — you prepped, you showed up early, and the outcome still depended on stuff no one can control on any given Tuesday. I’m here, I’m not going anywhere, and we can sit in the gray for as long as you need without forcing silver linings tonight at all. When you have a little energy back, we’ll do one tiny thing together — a 20-minute walk, one canceled email rewritten, one small win to prove you can trust yourself again very slowly.
- 中文：谢 trust 听出灰月重面试病妈 cancel 睡同扛无人说，must lot 独扛麻 call 车停家哭合理，不 rush 修需 sit 阵转 lesson 后用，听到全对仍输备早到果周二无人控，我在不走 sit 灰多久不逼 silver lining 今夜，有点能一起一小事20走1 cancel mail 改1小赢证信己慢慢。
- 正式度：neutral | 替换：随意 Here for you / 正式 Condolences/support
- 发音：grief /ɡriːf/如用，numb /nʌm/ b不发，canceled美式，gray美式，silver lining /ˈsɪlvər ˈlaɪnɪŋ/ Key IPA: trusting /ˈtrʌstɪŋ/; carry /ˈkæri/; numb /nʌm/; hearing /ˈhɪrɪŋ/; silver lining /ˈsɪlvər ˈlaɪnɪŋ/
- 提醒：thanks+must+not fix+hearing+here+tiny=安慰不鸡汤链，别说至少…
- 练习：写安慰8句：谢+ must +不修+听到+我在+小事，发给低谷友

### R14-P4：Impromptu & tough Q&A即兴+难问【演讲/面试 突发】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 被点名/难问懵；干啥 30秒有话说不冷场还加分
**段落8句：**
Great question — let me think out loud for ten seconds so I don’t give you a polished gray answer that sounds good but says nothing real at all. To be honest, I don’t have the full data yet — we’ve analyzed 40 calls, not 400 — so here’s my take based on what travelers actually told us last month in Austin. On the one hand, speed wins demos; on the other, trust wins renewals, and right now we’re optimizing for applause instead of the quiet retention that pays salaries every month. If I zoom into one moment, it was Tuesday’s canceled onboarding test — five users stared at step two for 90 seconds, and that silence taught me more than ten charts ever could. So here’s what I’d do: ship the ugly three-step flow Friday, watch 50 real clicks, then come back with numbers instead of opinions next Monday morning. I might be missing something, so what am I not seeing from your side? You’re closer to support pings, and I’d love to pressure-test this together before we lock it in writing.
- 中文：好问想10秒不给 polish 灰空话，老实无全数 analyze 40 非400 take 旅行 Austin 上月告，一手速赢 demo 一手信赢续现优掌声非静留付薪，zoom 一刻周二 cancel 上手测5用户瞪步2 90秒静胜十表，故 ship 丑三步周五看50点周一带数非见，我缺啥你侧近 ping 爱 pressure 锁字前共测？
- 正式度：neutral-professional | 替换：随意 Stall well / 正式 Extemporaneous response
- 发音：impromptu /ɪmˈprɑmptuː/，extemporaneous可不读，analyze/traveled/canceled/organize/gray美式 Key IPA: take /teɪk/; analyze /ˈænəlaɪz/; travelers /ˈtrævələrz/; canceled /ˈkænsəld/; impromptu /ɪmˈprɑmptu/
- 提醒：great+honest+one-other+zoom+do+missing=即兴六步不裸奔
- 练习：抽难问按6步答60秒录音：好问+老实+一手+瞬间+做+你侧？

### R14-P5：Clarify & confirm澄清确认【跨文化/远程 防错】
**场景/干啥：** 场景 口音快/数字钱/时间；干啥 30秒确认省3天返工
**段落8句：**
Just to make sure I got it — you mean $2k by March 1 for v1, not $20k, and gray onboarding cut to three steps before we demo to execs on Friday morning, right? Correct me if I’m wrong, but at a high level we’re saying async by default, four hours overlap for live debate, and one shared program doc everyone checks instead of chasing pings all day. Can you say more about “soon” — do you mean tonight, or Monday after you travel back from Austin and actually have bandwidth to review the Loom I dropped? Let me play back what I’ll do: I’ll organize the recap tonight, I’ll lock owners and dates in writing, and I’ll ping the gray risk thread so nobody wakes up surprised tomorrow at all. If I’m off by even 10%, flag it now while it’s cheap to fix — I’d rather look slow for 30 seconds than ship the wrong thing fast and apologize for a week straight after.
- 中文：确认 $2k 3月1 v1 非20k 灰上手砍3步周五晨 demo exec 对？纠错错 high level 异步默4叠 live 辩一共享 program doc 非追 ping，say more soon 今夜或旅行 Austin 回有 bandwidth 看 Loom？回放我做今夜 organize recap 锁主日期 ping 灰险无人明早惊，偏10%今 flag 廉修宁慢30秒非错快歉周连。
- 正式度：professional | 替换：随意 Double-check / 正式 Confirm understanding
- 发音：clarify /ˈklærəfaɪ/，confirm /kənˈfɜrm/，sure /ʃʊr/，travel/Austin/bandwidth/async/program/organize/gray美式 Key IPA: clarify /ˈklærəfaɪ/; confirm /kənˈfɜrm/; sure /ʃʊr/; async /eɪˈsɪŋk/; organize /ˈɔrɡənaɪz/; bandwidth /ˈbændwɪdθ/
- 提醒：make sure+correct me+say more+play back+flag=防错五连，钱数日期必回读
- 练习：下次会用此5连确认1件事：钱+时+谁+做+错 flag
